
2) src/pricing_engine.py — Purpose: tiered pricing and discounts utility (used by billing).  
```python
def apply_tiers(qty: float, tiers: list):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    tiers: ordered list of dicts {upto: Optional[float], unit_price: float}
    returns total cost for qty according to tiers
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    remaining = qty
    cost = 0.0
    prev = 0.0
    for t in tiers:
        upto = t.get(&quot;upto&quot;)
        price = float(t[&quot;unit_price&quot;])
        if upto is None:
            take = remaining
        else:
            take = max(0.0, min(remaining, upto - prev))
        cost += take * price
        remaining -= take
        prev = upto if upto is not None else prev
        if remaining &lt;= 0:
            break
    return round(cost, 6)

def apply_discount(subtotal: float, percent: float = 0.0, flat: float = 0.0):
    discounted = max(0.0, subtotal * (1.0 - percent / 100.0) - flat)
    return round(discounted, 6)
```

3) src/persist_billing_runs.py — Purpose: move aged preview runs into final invoice tables (idempotent), return persisted count.  
```python
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, uuid

def persist_billing_runs(session: Session, older_than_minutes: int = 60, preview_only: bool = True):
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT preview_id, preview_json
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW
        WHERE preview_ts &lt;= DATEADD(minute, -{older_than_minutes}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    persisted = []
    for r in rows:
        preview_id = r[&#39;PREVIEW_ID&#39;]
        preview = json.loads(r[&#39;PREVIEW_JSON&#39;])
        if not preview_only:
            invoice_id = preview.get(&#39;invoice_hash&#39;) or uuid.uuid4().hex
            session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES tgt
                USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_id}&#39; AS invoice_id) src
                ON tgt.invoice_id = src.invoice_id
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
                  INSERT (invoice_id, account_id, period_start, period_end, subtotal, markup, tax, total, created_at)
                  VALUES (&#39;{invoice_id}&#39;, &#39;{preview.get(&#39;lines&#39;)[0][&#39;account_id&#39;] if preview.get(&#39;lines&#39;) else &#39;MULTI&#39;}&#39;, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{preview.get(&#39;period_start&#39;,&#39;1970-01-01&#39;)}&#39;), TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{preview.get(&#39;period_end&#39;,&#39;1970-01-01&#39;)}&#39;), {preview.get(&#39;subtotal&#39;,0)}, {preview.get(&#39;markup&#39;,0)}, {preview.get(&#39;tax&#39;,0)}, {preview.get(&#39;total&#39;,0)}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
            &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
            # line items insertion should also be idempotent (not shown fully here)
        persisted.append(preview_id)
    return {&quot;persisted&quot;: len(persisted), &quot;preview_only&quot;: preview_only}
```

def compute_similarity_costs(session: Session, price_per_query: float):

        SELECT query_id, org_id, index_id
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.similarity_query_logs

    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

def entitlement_check(session: Session, account_id: str, feature_key: str):
    r = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT 1
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ENTITLEMENTS e
        WHERE e.account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39; AND e.feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39; AND e.active = TRUE
        LIMIT 1

compute_similarity_costs.py — Purpose: convert recent similarity_query_logs into usage events for billing (per-query costing), idempotent per query_id.

# compute_similarity_costs.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Optional

def compute_similarity_costs(session: Session, price_per_query: float = 0.01, lookback_minutes: int = 60) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Reads similarity_query_logs from the last `lookback_minutes`, deduplicates by query_id
    and inserts usage_events_detailed rows charging `price_per_query` per query.
    Returns processed count.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Select distinct recent queries that do not yet have a usage_events_detailed entry
    select_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    WITH recent AS (
      SELECT DISTINCT query_id, org_id, index_id
      FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.similarity_query_logs
      WHERE created_at &gt;= DATEADD(minute, -{lookback_minutes}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    )
    SELECT r.query_id, r.org_id, r.index_id
    FROM recent r
    LEFT JOIN AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_events_detailed u
      ON u.request_payload:query_id::STRING = r.query_id
    WHERE u.usage_id IS NULL
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(select_sql).collect()
    processed = 0
    insert_sql_template = &quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_events_detailed
      (usage_id, org_id, feature_code, model_id, tokens, latency_ms, cost, request_payload, created_at)
    VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    for r in rows:
        usage_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        org_id = r[&quot;ORG_ID&quot;]
        feature_code = &quot;SIMILARITY_QUERY&quot;
        model_id = &quot;ANN&quot;
        tokens = 0
        latency_ms = None
        cost = float(price_per_query)
        # capture the originating query id in request_payload for traceability
        request_payload_json = f&#39;{{&quot;query_id&quot;:&quot;{r[&quot;QUERY_ID&quot;]}&quot;,&quot;index_id&quot;:&quot;{r[&quot;INDEX_ID&quot;]}&quot;}}&#39;
        session.sql(
            insert_sql_template,
            (usage_id, org_id, feature_code, model_id, tokens, latency_ms, cost, request_payload_json)
        ).collect()
        processed += 1
    return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed}


enforce_ann_rate_limit.py — Purpose: evaluate QPS against per-org ANN limits and record backpressure events when exceeded.

# enforce_ann_rate_limit.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Dict

def enforce_ann_rate_limit(session: Session, org_id: str) -&gt; Dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Checks ANN QPS for org_id over the last 1 minute vs configured max_qps.
    If exceeded, writes a backpressure event to ingestion_backpressure_events.
    Returns status and measured QPS.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    limit_row = session.sql(
        f&quot;SELECT max_qps FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_rate_limits WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;
    ).collect()
    if not limit_row:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;NO_LIMIT&quot;, &quot;qps&quot;: 0.0, &quot;limit&quot;: None}

    max_qps = float(limit_row[0][&quot;MAX_QPS&quot;])
    qps_row = session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT (COUNT(*) / 60.0) AS qps
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.similarity_query_logs
        WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;
          AND created_at &gt;= DATEADD(minute, -1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()
    qps = float(qps_row[0][&quot;QPS&quot;]) if qps_row and qps_row[0][&quot;QPS&quot;] is not None else 0.0

    if qps &gt; max_qps:
        event_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
        details = f&#39;{{&quot;qps&quot;: {qps}, &quot;limit&quot;: {max_qps}}}&#39;
        session.sql(
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ingestion_backpressure_events
              (event_id, org_id, event_type, details, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;,
            (event_id, org_id, &quot;ann_rate_limit&quot;, details)
        ).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;THROTTLED&quot;, &quot;qps&quot;: qps, &quot;limit&quot;: max_qps}
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;, &quot;qps&quot;: qps, &quot;limit&quot;: max_qps}

run_ann_healthcheck.py — Purpose: run synthetic queries against ANN to measure latency/correctness and write ann_healthcheck_history.

# run_ann_healthcheck.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def run_ann_healthcheck(session: Session, index_snapshot_id: str, sample_queries: int = 5) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Performs a healthcheck by running sample queries (the actual ANN query implementation
    should be provided in SQL UDFs or external services).
    Stores aggregated metrics in ann_healthcheck_history.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # For a Snowpark-only implementation we read a small set of sample vectors and call
    # the ANN query SQL function if available; else we record a synthetic check.
    # Here we perform a lightweight measurement and persist results.
    # Attempt to compute p50/p95 latencies from a synthetic source if available.
    # NOTE: Replace the ANN call below with the platform-specific ANN invocation if present.
    p50 = 0
    p95 = 0
    qps = 0
    correctness = 1.0

    # Synthetic example: if there is an ann_healthcheck_source table with prior metrics, aggregate them
    agg = session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT
          COALESCE(AVG(latency_ms), 0) AS avg_latency,
          COALESCE(PERCENTILE_CONT(0.50) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY latency_ms), 0) AS p50,
          COALESCE(PERCENTILE_CONT(0.95) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY latency_ms), 0) AS p95,
          COALESCE(AVG(success_flag::INT), 1) AS correctness
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_healthcheck_samples
        WHERE index_snapshot_id = &#39;{index_snapshot_id}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()
    if agg:
        p50 = float(agg[0][&quot;P50&quot;] or 0)
        p95 = float(agg[0][&quot;P95&quot;] or 0)
        correctness = float(agg[0][&quot;CORRECTNESS&quot;] or 1.0)
        qps = float(agg[0][&quot;AVG_LATENCY&quot;] and (1000.0 / agg[0][&quot;AVG_LATENCY&quot;]) or 0.0)

    hc_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_healthcheck_history
          (hc_id, index_snapshot_id, qps, p50_latency_ms, p95_latency_ms, correctness_sample_score, created_at)
        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (hc_id, index_snapshot_id, qps, p50, p95, correctness)
    ).collect()
    return {&quot;hc_id&quot;: hc_id, &quot;index_snapshot_id&quot;: index_snapshot_id, &quot;p50&quot;: p50, &quot;p95&quot;: p95, &quot;correctness&quot;: correctness}

registerviaconnector.py — Purpose: fallback Python registrar that calls Snowflake connector to register procedures / upload imports when other flows fail.

# registerviaconnector.py
import snowflake.connector
import os
from typing import Optional

def register_procedure(sql_registration: str, account: str, user: str, password: str,
                       role: Optional[str] = None, warehouse: Optional[str] = None,
                       database: Optional[str] = None, schema: Optional[str] = None) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Executes the given SQL registration string (CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE / PUT etc)
    using the Snowflake Python connector. Returns simple status/result.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    conn = snowflake.connector.connect(
        account=account,
        user=user,
        password=password,
        role=role,
        warehouse=warehouse,
        database=database,
        schema=schema,
        client_session_keep_alive=True
    )
    try:
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(sql_registration)
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;}
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

src/usageingest_sp.py — Purpose: idempotent Snowpark Python stored procedure to ingest usage rows (from a staged CSV/JSON or a source table), deduplicate by a stable event id, normalize fields, and upsert into AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE.

# src/usageingest_sp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session, DataFrame
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List
import uuid, json

def usage_ingest_sp(session: Session, source_table: str, dedupe_key_col: str = &quot;event_id&quot;, batch_limit: int = 10000) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Idempotent ingestion stored-procedure.
    - source_table: fully-qualified table or stage view containing incoming events
    - dedupe_key_col: column in source used to deduplicate (string)
    - batch_limit: max rows to process in a single run
    Returns summary dict.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Read from source (assumes source_table is accessible in current DB/Schema or fully qualified)
    src_sql = f&quot;SELECT * FROM {source_table} LIMIT {batch_limit}&quot;
    src_df = session.sql(src_sql)
    rows = src_df.collect()
    processed = 0
    inserted = 0
    for r in rows:
        processed += 1
        event_id = r.get(dedupe_key_col) or uuid.uuid4().hex
        # Idempotency: skip if event already ingested
        exists = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE WHERE event_id = &#39;{event_id}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
        if exists and len(exists) &gt; 0:
            continue
        # Normalize fields (adapt to expected schema)
        account_id = r.get(&quot;account_id&quot;)
        feature_key = r.get(&quot;feature_key&quot;)
        units = float(r.get(&quot;units&quot;) or 0.0)
        unit_price = float(r.get(&quot;unit_price&quot;) or 0.0)
        usage_ts = r.get(&quot;usage_timestamp&quot;) or &quot;CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()&quot;
        # Use parameterized INSERT via SQL with explicit casting where needed
        session.sql(
            f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE
              (usage_id, event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp, created_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex}&#39;,&#39;{event_id}&#39;,&#39;{account_id}&#39;,&#39;{feature_key}&#39;,{units},{unit_price},TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{usage_ts}&#39;) , CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
        ).collect()
        inserted += 1
    return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed, &quot;inserted&quot;: inserted}


src/embeddingingest_sp.py — Purpose: idempotent Snowpark Python stored procedure to ingest embedding vectors (from stage), store vector metadata and a reference to the FAISS/ANN index snapshot. Writes to AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_STORE and index loader triggers.

# src/embeddingingest_sp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any, List
import uuid, json, base64

def embedding_ingest_sp(session: Session, source_table: str, id_col: str = &quot;doc_id&quot;, vector_col: str = &quot;embedding&quot;, batch_limit: int = 5000) -&gt; Dict[str, int]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Read embedding rows from source_table, dedupe by id_col, insert metadata into EMBEDDING_STORE,
    and persist vector bytes (base64) into staging columns for FAISS loader jobs.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT * FROM {source_table} LIMIT {batch_limit}&quot;).collect()
    processed = 0
    inserted = 0
    for r in rows:
        processed += 1
        doc_id = r.get(id_col) or uuid.uuid4().hex
        # idempotency check
        exists = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_STORE WHERE doc_id = &#39;{doc_id}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
        if exists and len(exists) &gt; 0:
            continue
        # Expect vector as JSON array or base64-encoded bytes
        vec = r.get(vector_col)
        if isinstance(vec, (list, tuple)):
            # store as JSON VARIANT for traceability; external loader will convert to FAISS format
            vec_json = json.dumps(vec)
            session.sql(
                f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
                INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_STORE (embedding_id, doc_id, vector_json, index_snapshot_ref, created_at)
                VALUES (&#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex}&#39;, &#39;{doc_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{vec_json.replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), NULL, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
            ).collect()
            inserted += 1
        elif isinstance(vec, str):
            # assume base64 encoded serialized vector
            session.sql(
                f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
                INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_STORE (embedding_id, doc_id, vector_b64, index_snapshot_ref, created_at)
                VALUES (&#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex}&#39;, &#39;{doc_id}&#39;, &#39;{vec.replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;, NULL, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
            ).collect()
            inserted += 1
        else:
            # skip malformed
            continue
    return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed, &quot;inserted&quot;: inserted}


src/writeevidencebundle.py — Purpose: Snowpark Python SP that collects evidence files/variants for an incident or run, writes an evidence bundle object (as VARIANT/OBJECT) to AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES and optionally archives to a stage.

# src/writeevidencebundle.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any, List
import uuid, json

def write_evidence_bundle(session: Session, owner_id: str, items: List[Dict[str, Any]], bundle_id: str = None, archive_stage: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Combine multiple evidence items (attachments, JSON payloads) into a single bundle record.
    - items: list of {type: &#39;text&#39;|&#39;file&#39;|&#39;json&#39;, payload: ...}
    - archive_stage: optional &#39;@~/&#39; stage path to PUT serialized bundle
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    bundle_id = bundle_id or uuid.uuid4().hex
    created = session.sql(&quot;SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS now&quot;).collect()[0][&quot;NOW&quot;]
    bundle = {
        &quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id,
        &quot;owner_id&quot;: owner_id,
        &quot;items&quot;: items,
        &quot;created_at&quot;: str(created)
    }
    bundle_json = json.dumps(bundle).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    session.sql(
        f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES (bundle_id, owner_id, bundle_payload, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, &#39;{owner_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{bundle_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
    ).collect()
    # Optional archive to stage
    if archive_stage:
        # write a small text file to a user stage (requires PUT privileges; this is a simple SQL method to persist)
        filename = f&quot;{bundle_id}.json&quot;
        # Simplified approach: insert into a stage table or use COPY/PUT externally; we store a pointer instead
        session.sql(
            f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_ARCHIVE(bundle_id, stage_path, archived_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, &#39;{archive_stage}/{filename}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
        ).collect()
    return {&quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id, &quot;items&quot;: len(items)}


# tests/testdeterministicbilling.py
import hashlib, json
from src.pricing_engine import apply_tiers, apply_discount

def deterministic_invoice_hash(invoice_obj: dict) -&gt; str:
    # canonicalize lines ordering to ensure deterministic hash
    lines = sorted(invoice_obj.get(&quot;lines&quot;, []), key=lambda l: (l.get(&quot;account_id&quot;,&quot;&quot;), l.get(&quot;feature_key&quot;,&quot;&quot;)))
    canonical = {&quot;lines&quot;: lines, &quot;subtotal&quot;: invoice_obj.get(&quot;subtotal&quot;,0.0), &quot;markup&quot;: invoice_obj.get(&quot;markup&quot;,0.0), &quot;tax&quot;: invoice_obj.get(&quot;tax&quot;,0.0), &quot;total&quot;: invoice_obj.get(&quot;total&quot;,0.0)}
    s = json.dumps(canonical, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), sort_keys=True)
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode()).hexdigest()

def test_hash_stability():
    inv = {&quot;lines&quot;:[{&quot;account_id&quot;:&quot;A&quot;,&quot;feature_key&quot;:&quot;f1&quot;,&quot;units&quot;:100,&quot;unit_price&quot;:0.01},{&quot;account_id&quot;:&quot;A&quot;,&quot;feature_key&quot;:&quot;f2&quot;,&quot;units&quot;:10,&quot;unit_price&quot;:1.0}], &quot;subtotal&quot;:2.0, &quot;markup&quot;:0.17, &quot;tax&quot;:0.0, &quot;total&quot;:2.17}
    h1 = deterministic_invoice_hash(inv)
    h2 = deterministic_invoice_hash(inv)
    assert h1 == h2



procedures/process_index_retirement.py — Purpose: worker-style Snowpark Python SP to mark an index retirement queue item as processed after the external snapshot deletion step completed (auditability).

# procedures/process_index_retirement.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def process_index_retirement(session: Session, retirement_id: str) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Marks the retirement queue entry as processed. Intended to be called after external deletion of snapshot.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Defensive: ensure retirement_id exists
    found = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_retirement_queue WHERE retirement_id = &#39;{retirement_id}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
    if not found or len(found) == 0:
        return &quot;NOT_FOUND&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_retirement_queue SET processed = TRUE, processed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE retirement_id = &#39;{retirement_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
    # Insert an audit row
    audit_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(f&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_retirement_audit (audit_id, retirement_id, note, created_at) VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{retirement_id}&#39;, &#39;processed by SP&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;).collect()
    return &quot;PROCESSED&quot;

from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def record_similarity_query(session: Session, query_id: str, org_id: str, index_id: str, query_vector: List[float], top_k: int, returned_count: int, latency_ms: int) -&gt; dict:

    ).collect()

infra/registerviaconnector_fallback.py — Purpose: more robust registrar wrapper that uploads Python artifacts to a stage and runs CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE registrations using the Snowflake connector; expands on the basic registerviaconnector.

# infra/registerviaconnector_fallback.py
import snowflake.connector
import os
from typing import Optional, Tuple

def register_files_and_sql(account: str, user: str, password: str, registration_sql: str, file_paths: Optional[list] = None, role: Optional[str] = None, warehouse: Optional[str] = None, database: Optional[str] = None, schema: Optional[str] = None) -&gt; dict:
    conn = snowflake.connector.connect(account=account, user=user, password=password, role=role, warehouse=warehouse, database=database, schema=schema)
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        # Upload files to user stage if provided (requires PUT via local CLI normally; simplified)
        if file_paths:
            for p in file_paths:
                cur.execute(f&quot;PUT file://{p} @%AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
        # Execute registration SQL
        cur.execute(registration_sql)
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;}
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()



src/entitlement_check_helper.py — Purpose: richer entitlement-check Snowpark SP/utility that validates account/feature entitlements including tier/limits and returns structured reason when disallowed. (Consolidates simple entitlement_check into a production-ready helper).

# src/entitlement_check_helper.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict

def entitlement_check_helper(session: Session, account_id: str, feature_key: str) -&gt; Dict:
    r = session.sql(
        f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
        SELECT e.account_id, e.feature_key, e.active, e.expires_at, e.quota_limit
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ENTITLEMENTS e
        WHERE e.account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39; AND e.feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39; LIMIT 1
        \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
    ).collect()
    if not r or len(r) == 0:
        return {&quot;account_id&quot;: account_id, &quot;feature_key&quot;: feature_key, &quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;No entitlement found&quot;}
    row = r[0]
    if not row[&quot;ACTIVE&quot;]:
        return {&quot;account_id&quot;: account_id, &quot;feature_key&quot;: feature_key, &quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;Entitlement inactive&quot;}
    if row.get(&quot;EXPIRES_AT&quot;):
        # if expired
        from datetime import datetime
        if row[&quot;EXPIRES_AT&quot;] and row[&quot;EXPIRES_AT&quot;] &lt; datetime.utcnow():
            return {&quot;account_id&quot;: account_id, &quot;feature_key&quot;: feature_key, &quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;Entitlement expired&quot;}
    # Optionally validate usage vs quota here (omitted for brevity)
    return {&quot;account_id&quot;: account_id, &quot;feature_key&quot;: feature_key, &quot;allowed&quot;: True}



run_billing.py — Purpose: orchestration Snowpark Python stored procedure that computes billing for a tenant batch, applies rate rules via pricing_engine, writes billing runs, and enqueues persistence. Returns billing_run_id and summary.

# run_billing.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json
from typing import Dict, Any, List
from datetime import datetime

# assumes pricing_engine.apply_tiers and apply_discount are available in scope or imported

def run_billing(session: Session, run_date: str = None, tenant_filter: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Compute billing run for tenants (optionally filtered).
    - run_date: ISO date string for run (defaults to today)
    - tenant_filter: optional SQL predicate to limit tenants, e.g., &quot;org_id = &#39;ORG_123&#39;&quot;
    Returns: {billing_run_id, tenant_count, invoice_count}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    run_date = run_date or datetime.utcnow().date().isoformat()
    billing_run_id = uuid.uuid4().hex

    # Collect tenants to bill
    tenant_sql = &quot;SELECT DISTINCT org_id FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_events_detailed&quot;
    if tenant_filter:
        tenant_sql += &quot; WHERE &quot; + tenant_filter

    tenants = session.sql(tenant_sql).collect()
    tenant_count = len(tenants)
    invoice_count = 0

    for t in tenants:
        org_id = t[&quot;ORG_ID&quot;]
        # gather usage rows for this org in the run window (example: last billing period - placeholder dates)
        usage_rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            SELECT usage_id, feature_code, model_id, tokens, cost, request_payload
            FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_events_detailed
            WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND billed = FALSE
            ORDER BY created_at
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        if not usage_rows:
            continue

        # Aggregate by feature_key / model etc
        lines = {}
        for r in usage_rows:
            key = (r[&quot;FEATURE_CODE&quot;], r.get(&quot;MODEL_ID&quot;))
            units = r.get(&quot;TOKENS&quot;) or 0
            lines.setdefault(key, {&quot;units&quot;: 0, &quot;cost&quot;: 0.0, &quot;rows&quot;: []})
            lines[key][&quot;units&quot;] += units
            lines[key][&quot;cost&quot;] += float(r.get(&quot;COST&quot;) or 0.0)
            lines[key][&quot;rows&quot;].append(r[&quot;USAGE_ID&quot;])

        # Build invoice lines applying pricing rules
        invoice_lines = []
        subtotal = 0.0
        for (feat, model), vals in lines.items():
            # Call pricing engine (assumed to exist) to compute unit price and discounts
            # pricing_engine.apply_tiers should return dict {unit_price, line_total, details}
            pe = apply_tiers(feat, vals[&quot;units&quot;])
            line_total = pe[&quot;line_total&quot;]
            invoice_lines.append({
                &quot;feature_code&quot;: feat,
                &quot;model_id&quot;: model,
                &quot;units&quot;: vals[&quot;units&quot;],
                &quot;unit_price&quot;: pe[&quot;unit_price&quot;],
                &quot;line_total&quot;: line_total,
                &quot;pricing_details&quot;: pe.get(&quot;details&quot;)
            })
            subtotal += line_total

        # Apply discounts / markups
        final = apply_discount(org_id, subtotal)  # returns dict with subtotal, discount, total
        invoice_id = uuid.uuid4().hex

        invoice_record = {
            &quot;invoice_id&quot;: invoice_id,
            &quot;billing_run_id&quot;: billing_run_id,
            &quot;org_id&quot;: org_id,
            &quot;run_date&quot;: run_date,
            &quot;subtotal&quot;: final[&quot;subtotal&quot;],
            &quot;discount&quot;: final.get(&quot;discount&quot;, 0.0),
            &quot;total&quot;: final[&quot;total&quot;],
            &quot;lines&quot;: invoice_lines,
            &quot;created_at&quot;: datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
        }

        # Persist invoice (VARIANT) and mark usage rows as billed
        invoice_json = json.dumps(invoice_record).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.invoices (invoice_id, billing_run_id, org_id, invoice_payload, created_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{invoice_id}&#39;, &#39;{billing_run_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{invoice_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        # update usage rows billed flag
        usage_ids = &quot;,&quot;.join([f&quot;&#39;{u}&#39;&quot; for u in sum([v[&quot;rows&quot;] for v in lines.values()], [])])
        if usage_ids:
            session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_events_detailed
                SET billed = TRUE, billed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), invoice_id = &#39;{invoice_id}&#39;
                WHERE usage_id IN ({usage_ids})
            &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        invoice_count += 1

    # Insert billing_run metadata
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.billing_runs (billing_run_id, run_date, tenant_count, invoice_count, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{billing_run_id}&#39;, &#39;{run_date}&#39;, {tenant_count}, {invoice_count}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;billing_run_id&quot;: billing_run_id, &quot;tenant_count&quot;: tenant_count, &quot;invoice_count&quot;: invoice_count}


persist_billing_runs.py — Purpose: persisted Snowpark Python procedure to materialize billing run artifacts (e.g., invoice lines into normalized tables) and emit audit. Idempotent on billing_run_id.

# persist_billing_runs.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def persist_billing_run(session: Session, billing_run_id: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Persist invoices from a billing run into normalized invoice_lines table.
    Idempotent: skips lines already persisted via invoice_line_id uniqueness.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    invoices = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT invoice_id, org_id, invoice_payload
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.invoices
        WHERE billing_run_id = &#39;{billing_run_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    persisted = 0
    for inv in invoices:
        invoice_id = inv[&quot;INVOICE_ID&quot;]
        payload = inv[&quot;INVOICE_PAYLOAD&quot;]
        # payload is VARIANT; extract lines using SQL to avoid client-side JSON parsing when large
        lines = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            SELECT line.value:feature_code::STRING AS feature_code,
                   line.value:model_id::STRING AS model_id,
                   line.value:units::FLOAT AS units,
                   line.value:unit_price::FLOAT AS unit_price,
                   line.value:line_total::FLOAT AS line_total
            FROM TABLE(FLATTEN(input =&gt; PARSE_JSON(&#39;{str(payload).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;):&quot;lines)) line
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        for ln in lines:
            line_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
            session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.invoice_lines (line_id, invoice_id, org_id, feature_code, model_id, units, unit_price, line_total, created_at)
                SELECT &#39;{line_id}&#39;, &#39;{invoice_id}&#39;, &#39;{inv[&#39;ORG_ID&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{ln[&#39;FEATURE_CODE&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{ln.get(&#39;MODEL_ID&#39;)}&#39;, {ln[&#39;UNITS&#39;]}, {ln[&#39;UNIT_PRICE&#39;]}, {ln[&#39;LINE_TOTAL&#39;]}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                WHERE NOT EXISTS (
                    SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.invoice_lines WHERE invoice_id = &#39;{invoice_id}&#39; AND feature_code = &#39;{ln[&#39;FEATURE_CODE&#39;]}&#39; AND units = {ln[&#39;UNITS&#39;]} AND line_total = {ln[&#39;LINE_TOTAL&#39;]} LIMIT 1
                )
            &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
            persisted += 1

    # Audit
    audit_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.billing_run_persist_audit (audit_id, billing_run_id, persisted_at, persisted_count)
        VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{billing_run_id}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), {persisted})
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;billing_run_id&quot;: billing_run_id, &quot;persisted&quot;: persisted}


pricing_engine.py — Purpose: consolidated pricing helpers (apply_tiers, apply_discount). Deterministic, pure-Python logic used by run_billing.

# pricing_engine.py
from typing import Dict, Any, List

# Example tier definition: [{&#39;up_to&#39;:1000, &#39;unit_price&#39;:0.001}, {&#39;up_to&#39;:10000, &#39;unit_price&#39;:0.0008}, {&#39;up_to&#39;:None, &#39;unit_price&#39;:0.0005}]
DEFAULT_TIERS = [
    {&quot;up_to&quot;: 1000, &quot;unit_price&quot;: 0.001},
    {&quot;up_to&quot;: 10000, &quot;unit_price&quot;: 0.0008},
    {&quot;up_to&quot;: None, &quot;unit_price&quot;: 0.0005}
]

def apply_tiers(feature_code: str, units: float, tiers: List[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Apply tiered pricing to units. Returns dict with unit_price (effective), line_total, details.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    tiers = tiers or DEFAULT_TIERS
    remaining = units
    total = 0.0
    details = []
    for t in tiers:
        cap = t[&quot;up_to&quot;]
        price = float(t[&quot;unit_price&quot;])
        if cap is None:
            qty = remaining
        else:
            qty = max(0.0, min(remaining, cap if cap is not None else remaining))
        if qty &lt;= 0:
            continue
        line = qty * price
        details.append({&quot;qty&quot;: qty, &quot;unit_price&quot;: price, &quot;line_total&quot;: line})
        total += line
        remaining -= qty
        if remaining &lt;= 0:
            break
    effective_unit_price = details[-1][&quot;unit_price&quot;] if details else tiers[-1][&quot;unit_price&quot;]
    return {&quot;unit_price&quot;: effective_unit_price, &quot;line_total&quot;: total, &quot;details&quot;: details}

def apply_discount(org_id: str, subtotal: float) -&gt; Dict[str, float]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Compute discounts/markups. Simple example: volume discount or flat partner discount.
    In production this would consult ENTITLEMENTS or RATE_RULES tables.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    discount = 0.0
    if subtotal &gt; 10000:
        discount = subtotal * 0.05  # 5% volume discount
    total = subtotal - discount
    return {&quot;subtotal&quot;: subtotal, &quot;discount&quot;: discount, &quot;total&quot;: total}

def log_model_call(session: Session, model_name: str, org_id: str, request_payload: Dict[str, Any], response_summary: Dict[str, Any], latency_ms: int, cost: float) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:

    response_json = json.dumps(response_summary).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.model_call_logs (log_id, model_name, org_id, request_payload, response_summary, latency_ms, cost, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{log_id}&#39;, &#39;{model_name}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{payload_json}&#39;), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{response_json}&#39;), {latency_ms}, {cost}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())

    return {&quot;log_id&quot;: log_id}

archive_old_invoices.py — Purpose: archive invoices older than X days to a user stage (record pointers) and mark archived flag.

# archive_old_invoices.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

def archive_old_invoices(session: Session, older_than_days: int = 90, archive_stage: str = &quot;@~/invoices_archive&quot;) -&gt; dict:
    cutoff = (datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(days=older_than_days)).date().isoformat()
    invoices = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT invoice_id
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.invoices
        WHERE created_at &lt; &#39;{cutoff}&#39; AND archived = FALSE
        LIMIT 1000
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    archived = 0
    for inv in invoices:
        invoice_id = inv[&quot;INVOICE_ID&quot;]
        # Pointer-based archive: write a row to invoice_archive with stage path (actual PUT may be done externally)
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.invoice_archive (invoice_id, archive_stage, archived_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{invoice_id}&#39;, &#39;{archive_stage}/{invoice_id}.json&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.invoices SET archived = TRUE, archived_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE invoice_id = &#39;{invoice_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
        archived += 1

    return {&quot;archived_count&quot;: archived}


retention_job_scheduler.py — Purpose: schedule and record retention job triggers (simple scheduler record manager).

# retention_job_scheduler.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def schedule_retention_job(session: Session, policy_name: str, run_cron: str, owner: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    job_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.retention_jobs (job_id, policy_name, run_cron, owner, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{job_id}&#39;, &#39;{policy_name}&#39;, &#39;{run_cron}&#39;, &#39;{owner}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;job_id&quot;: job_id}


cleanup_failed_jobs.py — Purpose: locate failed background job runs, attempt retry or mark terminal failure, and write job_audit rows.

# cleanup_failed_jobs.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def cleanup_failed_jobs(session: Session, max_retries: int = 3) -&gt; dict:
    failed = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT job_run_id, job_id, status, retry_count
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.job_runs
        WHERE status = &#39;FAILED&#39; AND retry_count &lt; {max_retries}
        LIMIT 200
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    retried = 0
    terminal = 0
    for r in failed:
        job_run_id = r[&quot;JOB_RUN_ID&quot;]
        retry_count = int(r[&quot;RETRY_COUNT&quot;] or 0)
        job_id = r[&quot;JOB_ID&quot;]
        # mark as retry and enqueue (simplified as incrementing retry_count and set status)
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.job_runs
            SET retry_count = {retry_count + 1}, status = &#39;RETRY_QUEUED&#39;, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            WHERE job_run_id = &#39;{job_run_id}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        retried += 1

    # mark terminal failures for those exceeding retries
    terminal_rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT job_run_id FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.job_runs WHERE status = &#39;FAILED&#39; AND retry_count &gt;= {max_retries} LIMIT 200
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    for tr in terminal_rows:
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.job_runs
            SET status = &#39;FAILED_TERMINAL&#39;, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            WHERE job_run_id = &#39;{tr[&#39;JOB_RUN_ID&#39;]}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        terminal += 1

    return {&quot;retried&quot;: retried, &quot;terminal_marked&quot;: terminal}


export_bundle.py — Purpose: generates an export file for an evidence bundle (creates export record and returns staged path pointer).

# export_bundle.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json
from typing import Dict, Any

def export_bundle(session: Session, bundle_id: str, target_stage: str = &quot;@~/evidence_exports&quot;) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    # Read bundle
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT bundle_id, owner_id, bundle_payload
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.evidence_bundles
        WHERE bundle_id = &#39;{bundle_id}&#39; LIMIT 1
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;NOT_FOUND&quot;, &quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id}

    payload = rows[0][&quot;BUNDLE_PAYLOAD&quot;]
    export_filename = f&quot;{bundle_id}.json&quot;
    staged_path = f&quot;{target_stage}/{export_filename}&quot;

    # Record export pointer (actual PUT to stage may be done externally)
    export_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.evidence_exports (export_id, bundle_id, stage_path, exported_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{export_id}&#39;, &#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, &#39;{staged_path}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;export_id&quot;: export_id, &quot;stage_path&quot;: staged_path}

``

52) persist_billing_proc.py — Persist previewed billing runs older than threshold; idempotent persistence of invoices and audit.
```python
# persist_billing_proc.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def persist_billing_runs(session: Session, older_than_minutes: int = 60, preview_only: bool = True) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Persist previews older than older_than_minutes.
    - preview_only True =&gt; return count without persisting invoices lines (useful for dry run)
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(
        &quot;select * from AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW where PREVIEW_TS &lt;= dateadd(&#39;minute&#39;, -{0}, current_timestamp())&quot;.format(older_than_minutes)
    ).collect()

    persisted = []
    for r in rows:
        data = json.loads(r[&quot;PREVIEW_JSON&quot;])
        if preview_only:
            persisted.append(r[&quot;PREVIEW_ID&quot;])
            continue

        # idempotent invoice insertion by invoice_hash
        invoice_hash = data.get(&quot;invoice_hash&quot;) or uuid.uuid4().hex
        invoice_id = invoice_hash
        session.sql(
            f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;merge into AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES t
               using (select &#39;{invoice_id}&#39; as invoice_id) s
               on t.invoice_id = s.invoice_id
               when not matched then
                 insert (invoice_id, account_id, period_start, period_end, subtotal, markup, tax, total, created_at)
                 values (&#39;{invoice_id}&#39;, &#39;{data.get(&#39;account_id&#39;,&#39;MULTI&#39;)}&#39;, to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{data.get(&#39;period_start&#39;,&#39;&#39;)}&#39;), to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{data.get(&#39;period_end&#39;,&#39;&#39;)}&#39;), {data.get(&#39;subtotal&#39;,0)}, {data.get(&#39;markup&#39;,0)}, {data.get(&#39;tax&#39;,0)}, {data.get(&#39;total&#39;,0)}, current_timestamp());
            \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
        ).collect()

        # persist lines
        for ln in data.get(&quot;lines&quot;, []):
            session.sql(
                f\&quot;\&quot;\&quot;merge into AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM t
                   using (select &#39;{invoice_id}&#39; as billing_run_id, &#39;{ln[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39; as feature_key) s
                   on t.billing_run_id = s.billing_run_id and t.feature_key = s.feature_key
                   when not matched then
                     insert (billing_run_id, account_id, feature_key, usage_qty, base_cost, line_total, created_at)
                     values (&#39;{invoice_id}&#39;, &#39;{ln[&#39;account_id&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{ln[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39;, {ln[&#39;units&#39;]}, {ln[&#39;unit_price&#39;]}, {ln[&#39;line_total&#39;]}, current_timestamp());
                \&quot;\&quot;\&quot;
            ).collect()

        persisted.append(r[&quot;PREVIEW_ID&quot;])

    return {&quot;persisted&quot;: len(persisted), &quot;preview_only&quot;: preview_only}
```

53) reconcile_proc.py — Compare TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE vs BILLING_LINE_ITEM, record mismatches to RECONCILIATION_AUDIT.
```python
# reconcile_proc.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

def reconcile_billing_vs_usage(session: Session, account_id: Optional[str] = None, window_start: Optional[str] = None, window_end: Optional[str] = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    cond = []
    if account_id:
        cond.append(f&quot;account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39;&quot;)
    if window_start and window_end:
        cond.append(f&quot;usage_timestamp between to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_start}&#39;) and to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_end}&#39;)&quot;)
    where = &#39; and &#39;.join(cond) if cond else &#39;1=1&#39;

    usage = session.sql(f&quot;select account_id, feature_key, sum(units) units from AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE where {where} group by account_id, feature_key&quot;).collect()
    billing = session.sql(f&quot;select account_id, feature_key, sum(line_total) billed from AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM where {where} group by account_id, feature_key&quot;).collect()

    u_map = {(r[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;], r[&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;]): r[&quot;UNITS&quot;] for r in usage}
    b_map = {(r[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;], r[&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;]): r[&quot;BILLED&quot;] for r in billing}
    issues = []
    keys = set(list(u_map.keys()) + list(b_map.keys()))
    for k in keys:
        u = u_map.get(k, 0)
        b = b_map.get(k, 0)
        if abs(u - b) &gt; 0.01:
            issues.append({&quot;account_id&quot;: k[0], &quot;feature_key&quot;: k[1], &quot;usage&quot;: u, &quot;billed&quot;: b, &quot;diff&quot;: u - b})

    if issues:
        for i in issues:
            session.sql(
                f&quot;insert into AI_FEATURE_HUB.RECONCILIATION_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY, USAGE_SUM, BILL_SUM, MISMATCH, RUN_AT) values (&#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex}&#39;, &#39;{i[&#39;account_id&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{i[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39;, {i[&#39;usage&#39;]}, {i[&#39;billed&#39;]}, {i[&#39;diff&#39;]}, current_timestamp())&quot;
            ).collect()
    return {&quot;issues&quot;: len(issues)}
```

# upsert_producer_config.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

             VALUES (&#39;{producer_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{embedding_model_id}&#39;, {batch_size}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());

# update_retention_policy_and_audit.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json

def update_retention_policy_and_audit(session: Session, org_id: str, embedding_days: int, provenance_days: int, changed_by: str) -&gt; str:

    ).collect()

63) purge_old_embeddings.py — Delete embeddings older than retention_days for an org; writes deletion audit rows for provenance
```python
# purge_old_embeddings.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def purge_old_embeddings(session: Session, org_id: str, retention_days: int, dry_run: bool = True) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Delete embeddings older than retention_days for the org. If dry_run, return count only.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(
        f&quot;SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{retention_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    ).collect()
    cnt = rows[0][&#39;CNT&#39;] if rows else 0
    result = {&quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;retention_days&quot;: retention_days, &quot;candidates&quot;: int(cnt), &quot;dry_run&quot;: dry_run}
    if dry_run or cnt == 0:
        return result
    # Delete in batches to avoid long-running single transaction (simple single-statement here)
    session.sql(
        f&quot;DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{retention_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    ).collect()
    # Record audit row
    audit_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    session.sql(
        f&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_deletion_audit (audit_id, org_id, deleted_count, retention_days, created_at) VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, {int(cnt)}, {retention_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    ).collect()
    result.update({&quot;deleted&quot;: int(cnt), &quot;audit_id&quot;: audit_id})
    return result
```

65) apply_provenance_retention.py — Safely delete provenance entries per policy and write retention audit

# apply_provenance_retention.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def apply_provenance_retention(session: Session, org_id: str, provenance_days: int, dry_run: bool = True) -&gt; dict:

    ).collect()

    ).collect()

66) check_quota_and_reserve.py — Atomically check tenant quota and reserve capacity (returns reservation token if successful)
```python
# check_quota_and_reserve.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def check_quota_and_reserve(session: Session, org_id: str, requested_units: float, reservation_ttl_seconds: int = 300) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Atomically check current usage vs quota and create a reservation row.
    Returns {&quot;ok&quot;: True, &quot;reservation_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;} on success or {&quot;ok&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;...&quot;}.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Read quota &amp; current usage (simple single-statement pattern; wrap in transaction for strong atomicity if supported)
    quota_row = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT quota_units FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.tenant_quotas WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
    if not quota_row:
        return {&quot;ok&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;no_quota_configured&quot;}
    quota = float(quota_row[0][&#39;QUOTA_UNITS&#39;])
    used_row = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT SUM(reserved_units) AS reserved FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.quota_reservations WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND expires_at &gt; CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()&quot;).collect()
    reserved = float(used_row[0][&#39;RESERVED&#39;] or 0.0)
    available = quota - reserved
    if requested_units &gt; available:
        return {&quot;ok&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;insufficient_quota&quot;, &quot;available&quot;: available}
    reservation_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    expires_at_expr = f&quot;DATEADD(second, {reservation_ttl_seconds}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    session.sql(
        f&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.quota_reservations (reservation_id, org_id, reserved_units, created_at, expires_at) VALUES (&#39;{reservation_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, {requested_units}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), {expires_at_expr})&quot;
    ).collect()
    return {&quot;ok&quot;: True, &quot;reservation_id&quot;: reservation_id, &quot;reserved&quot;: requested_units, &quot;expires_at&quot;: reservation_ttl_seconds}
```

67) aggregate_usage_daily.py — Daily aggregation of usage events into a rollup table for billing/analytics
```python
# aggregate_usage_daily.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def aggregate_usage_daily(session: Session, run_date: str = None) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregate raw usage events into daily usage rollup.
    run_date: ISO date string (YYYY-MM-DD) to aggregate; defaults to yesterday if None.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    if not run_date:
        # default to yesterday
        run_date = session.sql(&quot;SELECT TO_CHAR(DATEADD(day, -1, CURRENT_DATE()), &#39;YYYY-MM-DD&#39;) AS d&quot;).collect()[0][&#39;D&#39;]
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.daily_feature_usage (org_id, account_id, feature_key, usage_date, units, unit_price, created_at)
        SELECT org_id, account_id, feature_key, TO_DATE(usage_timestamp) as usage_date, SUM(units) as units, MAX(unit_price) as unit_price, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE
        WHERE TO_DATE(usage_timestamp) = TO_DATE(&#39;{run_date}&#39;)
        GROUP BY org_id, account_id, feature_key, TO_DATE(usage_timestamp);
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()
    return run_date
```

68) compute_invoice_hash.py — Assemble invoice rows and compute deterministic SHA256 invoice hash used for idempotency
```python
# compute_invoice_hash.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import hashlib, json

def compute_invoice_hash(session: Session, invoice_payload: dict) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Given invoice payload (dict with sorted stable fields), compute deterministic SHA256 hash.
    This helper is used by billing flows to create idempotent invoice IDs.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Ensure canonical ordering
    canonical = json.dumps(invoice_payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(&#39;,&#39;, &#39;:&#39; ), ensure_ascii=False)
    h = hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode(&#39;utf-8&#39;)).hexdigest()
    return h
```

69) run_billing_reconciliation.py — Compare invoices vs aggregated usage and produce discrepancy report rows in BILLING_RECONCILIATION
```python
# run_billing_reconciliation.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def run_billing_reconciliation(session: Session, period_start: str, period_end: str) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Compare SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES and BILLING_LINE_ITEM totals against daily_feature_usage aggregates and record mismatches.
    Returns summary dict with counts.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Build aggregated usage totals per account+feature
    agg = session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        WITH usage_aggr AS (
            SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(units * unit_price) AS usage_amount
            FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.daily_feature_usage
            WHERE usage_date BETWEEN TO_DATE(&#39;{period_start}&#39;) AND TO_DATE(&#39;{period_end}&#39;)
            GROUP BY account_id, feature_key
        ),
        billed AS (
            SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(line_total) AS billed_amount
            FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM
            WHERE created_at BETWEEN to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{period_start}&#39;) AND to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{period_end}&#39;)
            GROUP BY account_id, feature_key
        )
        SELECT COALESCE(u.account_id, b.account_id) AS account_id, COALESCE(u.feature_key, b.feature_key) AS feature_key, COALESCE(u.usage_amount,0) AS usage_amount, COALESCE(b.billed_amount,0) AS billed_amount
        FROM usage_aggr u FULL OUTER JOIN billed b ON u.account_id = b.account_id AND u.feature_key = b.feature_key
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()
    mismatches = 0
    for r in agg:
        usage_amt = float(r[&#39;USAGE_AMOUNT&#39;] or 0)
        billed_amt = float(r[&#39;BILLED_AMOUNT&#39;] or 0)
        if abs(usage_amt - billed_amt) &gt; 0.01:
            mismatches += 1
            session.sql(
                f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RECONCILIATION (recon_id, account_id, feature_key, usage_amount, billed_amount, diff, created_at)
                VALUES (&#39;{str(uuid.uuid4())}&#39;, &#39;{r[&#39;ACCOUNT_ID&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]}&#39;, {usage_amt}, {billed_amt}, {usage_amt - billed_amt}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
            ).collect()
    return {&quot;period_start&quot;: period_start, &quot;period_end&quot;: period_end, &quot;mismatches&quot;: mismatches}
```

91) run_embedding_quality_checks.py — Run a set of embedding quality checks (coverage, cardinality, staleness) and persist results ```python
# run_embedding_quality_checks.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json, statistics, math

def run_embedding_quality_checks(session: Session, org_id: str, index_id: str, sample_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Sample embeddings for index_id and compute quality metrics:
      - avg_vector_dim
      - null_vector_fraction
      - duplicate_document_fraction (simple hash-based)
      - staleness_days (avg)
    Persist a JSON result row and return summary.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    sample_q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT document_id, vector_dim, created_at, MD5(TO_HEX(HASH(vector))) AS vec_hash
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings
    WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND index_id = &#39;{index_id}&#39;
    SAMPLE ({min(sample_limit, 1000)})
    LIMIT {sample_limit}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(sample_q).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;ok&quot;: True, &quot;checked&quot;: 0}

    dims = [r[&#39;VECTOR_DIM&#39;] or 0 for r in rows]
    hashes = [r[&#39;VEC_HASH&#39;] for r in rows]
    created = [r[&#39;CREATED_AT&#39;] for r in rows]

    avg_dim = float(sum(dims)) / len(dims) if dims else 0.0
    null_frac = sum(1 for d in dims if d == 0) / len(dims)
    dup_frac = (len(hashes) - len(set(hashes))) / len(hashes)
    now_ts = session.sql(&quot;SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS now&quot;).collect()[0][&#39;NOW&#39;]
    staleness_days = float(
        sum([(now_ts - c).days for c in created]) / len(created)
    )

    result = {
        &quot;org_id&quot;: org_id,
        &quot;index_id&quot;: index_id,
        &quot;avg_vector_dim&quot;: avg_dim,
        &quot;null_vector_fraction&quot;: null_frac,
        &quot;duplicate_vector_fraction&quot;: dup_frac,
        &quot;avg_staleness_days&quot;: staleness_days,
        &quot;sample_count&quot;: len(rows)
    }

    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_quality_checks (check_id, org_id, index_id, result, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{str(uuid.uuid4())}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{index_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(result).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    return result

evaluate_assembly_validations.py — Evaluate validation rules for assembled documents (e.g., required metadata present) and persist outcomes ```python

evaluate_assembly_validations.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Sessionimport uuid, json
def evaluate_assembly_validations(session: Session, org_id: str, assembly_id: str, rules: list) -&gt; dict:    &quot;&quot;&quot;    Apply a list of validation rules to an assembled document and persist results.    rules: list of dicts {rule_id, description, check_type, params}    Returns dict with pass/fail counts.    &quot;&quot;&quot;    # Fetch assembly payload    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT assembly_payload FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.assembled_documents WHERE assembly_id = &#39;{assembly_id}&#39; AND org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()    if not rows:        return {&quot;ok&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;assembly_not_found&quot;}
payload = rows[0][&#39;ASSEMBLY_PAYLOAD&#39;]
pass_count = 0
fail_count = 0
details = []

for rule in rules:
    rid = rule.get(&quot;rule_id&quot;)
    desc = rule.get(&quot;description&quot;)
    # Example simple checks
    if rule.get(&quot;check_type&quot;) == &quot;required_field&quot;:
        field = rule.get(&quot;params&quot;, {}).get(&quot;field&quot;)
        passed = field in payload and payload.get(field) not in (None, &quot;&quot;)
    else:
        passed = True  # default to pass for unknown rule types

    details.append({&quot;rule_id&quot;: rid, &quot;description&quot;: desc, &quot;passed&quot;: passed})
    if passed:
        pass_count += 1
    else:
        fail_count += 1

result = {&quot;assembly_id&quot;: assembly_id, &quot;pass&quot;: pass_count, &quot;fail&quot;: fail_count, &quot;details&quot;: details}

session.sql(
    f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.assembly_validation_results (result_id, org_id, assembly_id, result, created_at)
    VALUES (&#39;{str(uuid.uuid4())}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{assembly_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(result).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
).collect()

return result

compute_query_cost_estimate.py — Estimate cost of a query (semantic + retrieval + LLM cost) and return estimate JSON ```python

compute_query_cost_estimate.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Sessionimport json
def compute_query_cost_estimate(session: Session, org_id: str, estimated_similarity_ops: int, estimated_tokens: int, cost_params: dict = None) -&gt; dict:    &quot;&quot;&quot;    Simple cost estimator that combines similarity op cost and token cost using provided parameters.    cost_params example: {&quot;cost_per_similarity&quot;: 0.0005, &quot;cost_per_token&quot;: 0.00002}    &quot;&quot;&quot;    cp = cost_params or {&quot;cost_per_similarity&quot;: 0.0005, &quot;cost_per_token&quot;: 0.00002}    sim_cost = estimated_similarity_ops * float(cp[&quot;cost_per_similarity&quot;])    llm_cost = estimated_tokens * float(cp[&quot;cost_per_token&quot;])    total = round(sim_cost + llm_cost, 6)    estimate = {&quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;similarity_ops&quot;: estimated_similarity_ops, &quot;tokens&quot;: estimated_tokens, &quot;sim_cost&quot;: sim_cost, &quot;llm_cost&quot;: llm_cost, &quot;total&quot;: total}    # Persist estimate for telemetry    session.sql(        f&quot;&quot;&quot;        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.query_cost_estimates (estimate_id, org_id, estimate, created_at)        VALUES (RANDOM_UUID(), &#39;{org_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(estimate).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());        &quot;&quot;&quot;    ).collect()    return estimate


97) run_prereq_checkset.py — Run a set of prerequisite checks before a heavy operation (e.g., index rebuild) and persist result ```python
# run_prereq_checkset.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json

def run_prereq_checkset(session: Session, org_id: str, checkset_id: str, targets: dict) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Execute a set of prerequisite checks and persist an aggregated result.
    targets: dict describing resources to check (e.g., {&quot;index_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;min_docs&quot;: 100})
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    results = {&quot;ok&quot;: True, &quot;checks&quot;: []}
    # Example check: minimum document count
    idx = targets.get(&quot;index_id&quot;)
    min_docs = int(targets.get(&quot;min_docs&quot;, 0))
    if idx:
        row = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND index_id = &#39;{idx}&#39;&quot;).collect()[0]
        cnt = int(row[&#39;CNT&#39;] or 0)
        passed = cnt &gt;= min_docs
        results[&quot;checks&quot;].append({&quot;check&quot;: &quot;min_docs&quot;, &quot;index_id&quot;: idx, &quot;required&quot;: min_docs, &quot;found&quot;: cnt, &quot;passed&quot;: passed})
        if not passed:
            results[&quot;ok&quot;] = False

    results_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.prereq_check_results (result_id, org_id, checkset_id, result, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{results_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{checkset_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(results).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    return results

99) assemble_evidence_bundle.py — Assemble evidence bundle from provenance entries and persist the bundle (used by archival/export) ```python
# assemble_evidence_bundle.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json

def assemble_evidence_bundle(session: Session, org_id: str, bundle_id: str, provenance_ids: list, created_by: str) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Read provenance entries, assemble an evidence bundle JSON, persist into evidence_bundles table,
    and return bundle_id. Idempotent if bundle_id exists.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Gather provenance rows
    ids_list = &quot;,&quot;.join([f&quot;&#39;{pid}&#39;&quot; for pid in provenance_ids])
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT provenance_id, document_id, snippet, details FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.query_provenance WHERE provenance_id IN ({ids_list}) AND org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
    entries = []
    for r in rows:
        entries.append({&quot;provenance_id&quot;: r[&#39;PROVENANCE_ID&#39;], &quot;document_id&quot;: r[&#39;DOCUMENT_ID&#39;], &quot;snippet&quot;: r[&#39;SNIPPET&#39;], &quot;details&quot;: r[&#39;DETAILS&#39;]})

    bundle = {&quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id or str(uuid.uuid4()), &quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;created_by&quot;: created_by, &quot;entries&quot;: entries}

    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.evidence_bundles tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{bundle[&#39;bundle_id&#39;]}&#39; AS bundle_id) src
        ON tgt.bundle_id = src.bundle_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET bundle = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(bundle).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (bundle_id, org_id, bundle, created_at) VALUES (&#39;{bundle[&#39;bundle_id&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(bundle).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    # Write audit row
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.evidence_bundle_audit (audit_id, bundle_id, org_id, entry_count, created_at)
        VALUES (RANDOM_UUID(), &#39;{bundle[&#39;bundle_id&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, {len(entries)}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    return bundle[&#39;bundle_id&#39;]



ingestion_idempotent_sp.py — Idempotent ingestion Snowpark procedure (usage/embedding ingestion helper)

# ingestion_idempotent_sp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json

def ingest_usage_or_embedding(session: Session, payload: dict, payload_type: str = &#39;usage&#39;) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Generic idempotent ingest helper. payload must include an &#39;event_id&#39; for idempotency.
    payload_type: &#39;usage&#39; or &#39;embedding&#39;
    Returns event_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    event_id = payload.get(&#39;event_id&#39;) or str(uuid.uuid4())
    if payload_type == &#39;usage&#39;:
        # Idempotent insert into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE by event_id
        session.sql(
            f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE t
            USING (SELECT &#39;{event_id}&#39; AS event_id) s
            ON t.event_id = s.event_id
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
              INSERT (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp, created_at)
              VALUES (&#39;{event_id}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&#39;account_id&#39;)}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&#39;feature_key&#39;)}&#39;, {payload.get(&#39;units&#39;,0)}, {payload.get(&#39;unit_price&#39;,0)}, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{payload.get(&#39;usage_timestamp&#39;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
        ).collect()
    else:
        # Embedding ingest into DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS
        vec_json = json.dumps(payload.get(&#39;vector&#39;) or []).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
        session.sql(
            f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS t
            USING (SELECT &#39;{event_id}&#39; AS event_id) s
            ON t.event_id = s.event_id
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
              INSERT (event_id, org_id, document_id, section_id, embedding_vector, metadata, created_at)
              VALUES (&#39;{event_id}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&#39;org_id&#39;)}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&#39;document_id&#39;)}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&#39;section_id&#39;)}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{vec_json}&#39;), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(payload.get(&#39;metadata&#39;,{})).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
        ).collect()
    return event_id

# evidence_bundle_exporter.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, uuid

def export_evidence_bundle(session: Session, bundle_id: str, target_stage: str) -&gt; dict:

similarity_service_fastapi.py — FastAPI service skeleton for similarity queries (FAISS-backed) used by external function container

# similarity_service_fastapi.py
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
import uuid, json

app = FastAPI()

class QueryRequest(BaseModel):
    query: str
    top_k: int = 10
    index_id: str = None
    params: dict = {}

@app.post(&quot;/similarity/query&quot;)
def similarity_query(req: QueryRequest):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Accepts a query and returns top-K nearest neighbors.
    This is a service skeleton; real implementation should call FAISS index and return results with ids/scores.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    if not req.index_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=&quot;index_id required&quot;)
    # Placeholder deterministic response shape
    results = [{&quot;doc_id&quot;: f&quot;doc-{i}&quot;, &quot;score&quot;: 1.0/(i+1)} for i in range(req.top_k)]
    return {&quot;query_id&quot;: str(uuid.uuid4()), &quot;index_id&quot;: req.index_id, &quot;results&quot;: results}



faiss_index_loader.py — FAISS index snapshot loader (helper used in container)

# faiss_index_loader.py
import faiss, os, json

def load_index_snapshot(snapshot_path: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Load a FAISS index snapshot from disk and return index object.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    if not os.path.exists(snapshot_path):
        raise FileNotFoundError(snapshot_path)
    index = faiss.read_index(snapshot_path)
    return index

def load_index_metadata(meta_path: str):
    with open(meta_path, &#39;r&#39;) as f:
        return json.load(f)



register_procedures_via_connector.py — Python connector fallback registrar (registers stored procs/PUT files)

# register_procedures_via_connector.py
from snowflake.connector import connect
import os, glob, sqlparse

def register_all_procs(conn_params: dict, sql_dir: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Use Snowflake Python connector to execute registration SQL files found under sql_dir/register/create/procedures/
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    with connect(**conn_params) as conn:
        cur = conn.cursor()
        files = glob.glob(os.path.join(sql_dir, &quot;register&quot;, &quot;create&quot;, &quot;procedures&quot;, &quot;*.sql&quot;))
        for fpath in sorted(files):
            with open(fpath, &#39;r&#39;) as f:
                sql = f.read()
            # Optionally split and execute statements
            stmts = sqlparse.split(sql)
            for s in stmts:
                cur.execute(s)

reconcile_ci.py — lightweight CI reconcile (connector-based) to compare billed vs usage (CLI/test harness)

# reconcile_ci.py
import os
from snowflake import connector

def reconcile():
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Connect via snowflake-connector and compare aggregate billed vs usage cost.
    Prints billed and usage_cost totals.
    Intended for CI/smoke checks.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    c = connector.connect(
        user=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;),
        password=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;),
        account=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;),
        warehouse=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_WAREHOUSE&quot;),
        database=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DB&quot;),
        schema=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    )
    try:
        cur = c.cursor()
        cur.execute(&quot;SELECT COALESCE(SUM(line_total),0) AS billed FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM&quot;)
        billed = cur.fetchone()[0] or 0.0
        cur.execute(&quot;SELECT COALESCE(SUM(units*unit_price),0) AS usage_cost FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE&quot;)
        usage_cost = cur.fetchone()[0] or 0.0
        print(&quot;billed,usage_cost&quot;, float(billed), float(usage_cost))
        return {&quot;billed&quot;: float(billed), &quot;usage_cost&quot;: float(usage_cost)}
    finally:
        cur.close()
        c.close()

usage_ingest_sp.py — Snowpark stored-procedure style ingestion for usage events (idempotent)

# usage_ingest_sp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def usage_ingest_sp(session: Session, event_id: str, account_id: str, feature_key: str, units: float, unit_price: float, usage_ts: str) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Idempotent ingestion of a usage event into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE.
    Uses MERGE on event_id to avoid duplicates when called repeatedly.
    usage_ts must be an ISO string acceptable to TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ.
    Returns event_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
    USING (SELECT &#39;{event_id}&#39; AS event_id) src
      ON tgt.event_id = src.event_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET
      account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39;,
      feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39;,
      units = {units},
      unit_price = {unit_price},
      usage_timestamp = TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{usage_ts}&#39;),
      updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp, created_at)
      VALUES (&#39;{event_id}&#39;, &#39;{account_id}&#39;, &#39;{feature_key}&#39;, {units}, {unit_price}, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{usage_ts}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return event_id

embedding_ingest_sp.py — Snowpark stored-procedure ingestion for embeddings (idempotent, stores metadata + vector)

# embedding_ingest_sp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid

def embedding_ingest_sp(session: Session, event_id: str, org_id: str, document_id: str, section_id: str, vector: list, metadata: dict = None) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Idempotent ingestion of an embedding into DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS.
    Vector is stored as JSON array; metadata stored as VARIANT.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    vec_json = json.dumps(vector).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    meta_json = json.dumps(metadata or {}).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    embedding_id = event_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS tgt
    USING (SELECT &#39;{embedding_id}&#39; AS embedding_id) src
      ON tgt.embedding_id = src.embedding_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET
      org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;,
      document_id = &#39;{document_id}&#39;,
      section_id = &#39;{section_id}&#39;,
      embedding_vector = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{vec_json}&#39;),
      metadata = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{meta_json}&#39;),
      updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (embedding_id, org_id, document_id, section_id, embedding_vector, metadata, created_at)
      VALUES (&#39;{embedding_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{document_id}&#39;, &#39;{section_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{vec_json}&#39;), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{meta_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # ingest event audit
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_ingest_events (event_id, embedding_id, org_id, document_id, section_id, created_at)
    VALUES (RANDOM_UUID(), &#39;{embedding_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{document_id}&#39;, &#39;{section_id}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return embedding_id

writeevidencebundle.py — write/export evidence bundle to a stage-record and audit (idempotent)

# writeevidencebundle.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, uuid

def write_evidence_bundle(session: Session, bundle_id: str, org_id: str, entries: list, target_stage_path: str, created_by: str) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Persist an evidence bundle object and record an export manifest row.
    The routine writes a JSON payload into an evidence_exports table (acts as staged artifact entry),
    and upserts the evidence_bundles manifest row (idempotent).
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    payload = {&quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id, &quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;entries&quot;: entries, &quot;created_by&quot;: created_by}
    payload_json = json.dumps(payload).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    # Upsert manifest
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES tgt
    USING (SELECT &#39;{bundle_id}&#39; AS bundle_id) src
      ON tgt.bundle_id = src.bundle_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET bundle = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{payload_json}&#39;), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (bundle_id, org_id, bundle, created_at)
      VALUES (&#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{payload_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # Register export artifact (represents writing to stage)
    export_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.evidence_exports (export_id, bundle_id, target_stage, payload, created_at)
    VALUES (&#39;{export_id}&#39;, &#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, &#39;{target_stage_path}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{payload_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return bundle_id

snowflake_provisioning.py — helper to provision stages/integrations/roles (connector-based)

# snowflake_provisioning.py
from snowflake.connector import connect
import sqlparse

def provision(conn_params: dict, statements: list):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Execute a list of DDL statements (e.g., create stage, file format, integration, grants).
    conn_params: dict for snowflake.connector.connect
    statements: list of SQL strings
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    with connect(**conn_params) as conn:
        cur = conn.cursor()
        try:
            for s in statements:
                # split into individual statements for robust execution
                for stmt in sqlparse.split(s):
                    if not stmt.strip():
                        continue
                    cur.execute(stmt)
            conn.commit()
        finally:
            cur.close()

test_deterministic_billing.py — pytest for deterministic invoice hash generation

# test_deterministic_billing.py
import hashlib, uuid
from pricing_engine import apply_tiers

def deterministic_invoice_hash(seed: str, total: float) -&gt; str:
    return hashlib.sha256((seed + str(total)).encode()).hexdigest()

def test_apply_tiers_basic():
    tiers = [{&quot;upto&quot;: 100, &quot;price&quot;: 0.1}, {&quot;upto&quot;: 1000, &quot;price&quot;: 0.08}, {&quot;upto&quot;: None, &quot;price&quot;: 0.05}]
    assert apply_tiers(50, tiers) == round(50 * 0.1, 6)
    assert apply_tiers(150, tiers) == round(100*0.1 + 50*0.08, 6)

def test_invoice_hash_determinism():
    s = &quot;seed-123&quot;
    h1 = deterministic_invoice_hash(s, 123.45)
    h2 = deterministic_invoice_hash(s, 123.45)
    assert h1 == h2
