          VALUES (:1, :2, :3, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        session.sql(merge_entry, (entry_id, index_id, embedding_id)).collect()

    return &quot;OK&quot;


entitlement_check.py — Snowpark Python stored-procedure: verify account/tenant entitlements for a feature (full handler)

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def entitlement_check(session: Session, account_id: str, feature_key: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Returns {&#39;allowed&#39;: bool, &#39;reason&#39;: str, &#39;details&#39;: {...}}
    Checks AI_FEATURE_HUB.entitlements (or equivalent table) for active entitlements,
    expiry and usage caps. Designed to be called inside Snowpark-proc registration.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # 1) basic entitlement lookup
    q = &quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT e.account_id, e.feature_key, e.enabled, e.effective_from, e.expires_at,
           e.max_units_per_period, e.period_seconds
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.entitlements e
    WHERE e.account_id = :1 AND e.feature_key = :2
    LIMIT 1
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(q, (account_id, feature_key)).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;no_entitlement&quot;, &quot;details&quot;: {}}

    row = rows[0]
    enabled = bool(row[&quot;ENABLED&quot;])
    if not enabled:
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;disabled_entitlement&quot;, &quot;details&quot;: row.asDict()}

    # 2) check expiry
    exp_q = &quot;SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AS now&quot;
    now = session.sql(exp_q).collect()[0][&quot;NOW&quot;]
    if row[&quot;EXPIRES_AT&quot;] is not None and now &gt; row[&quot;EXPIRES_AT&quot;]:
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;expired&quot;, &quot;details&quot;: row.asDict()}

    # 3) if usage cap defined, compute recent usage in period
    max_units = row[&quot;MAX_UNITS_PER_PERIOD&quot;]
    period_seconds = row[&quot;PERIOD_SECONDS&quot;]
    if max_units is not None and period_seconds is not None:
        usage_q = &quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT COALESCE(SUM(u.units),0) AS used
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE u
        WHERE u.account_id = :1
          AND u.feature_key = :2
          AND u.usage_timestamp &gt;= DATEADD(second, -:3, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        used_row = session.sql(usage_q, (account_id, feature_key, period_seconds)).collect()[0]
        used = float(used_row[&quot;USED&quot;] or 0)
        if used &gt;= float(max_units):
            return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;quota_exhausted&quot;, &quot;details&quot;: {&quot;used&quot;: used, &quot;quota&quot;: max_units}}

        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: True, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;details&quot;: {&quot;used&quot;: used, &quot;quota&quot;: max_units}}
    else:
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: True, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;details&quot;: {}}



src/usage/ingest/sp.py — idempotent usage ingestion Snowpark Python procedure (single/batch ingest)

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import List, Dict, Any
import uuid, json

def ingest_usage_events(session: Session, events: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Idempotent ingestion of usage events into AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE.
    Each event is expected to include an external_id (for dedupe), account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp.
    Uses MERGE to ensure idempotency on external_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    insert_sql = &quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
    USING (SELECT :1 AS external_id) src
      ON tgt.external_id = src.external_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET account_id = :2, feature_key = :3, units = :4, unit_price = :5, usage_timestamp = :6, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (usage_id, external_id, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp, created_at)
         VALUES (:7, :1, :2, :3, :4, :5, :6, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    for ev in events:
        external_id = ev.get(&quot;external_id&quot;) or str(uuid.uuid4())
        usage_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        session.sql(insert_sql, (external_id, ev.get(&quot;account_id&quot;), ev.get(&quot;feature_key&quot;), ev.get(&quot;units&quot;, 0), ev.get(&quot;unit_price&quot;, 0.0), ev.get(&quot;usage_timestamp&quot;), usage_id)).collect()
    return {&quot;ingested&quot;: len(events)}



src/embedding/ingest/sp.py — idempotent embedding ingestion Snowpark Python procedure

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import List, Dict, Any
import uuid, json

def ingest_embeddings(session: Session, items: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Idempotent ingestion of embeddings into AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings.
    Each item should include embedding_id (or external id), org_id, document_id, section_id/clause_id, vector (list), metadata (dict).
    Uses MERGE on embedding_id to upsert.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    merge_sql = &quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings tgt
    USING (SELECT :1 AS embedding_id) src
      ON tgt.embedding_id = src.embedding_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET org_id = :2, document_id = :3, section_id = :4, vector = :5, metadata = PARSE_JSON(:6), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (embedding_id, org_id, document_id, section_id, vector, metadata, created_at)
         VALUES (:7, :2, :3, :4, :5, PARSE_JSON(:6), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    for it in items:
        embedding_id = it.get(&quot;embedding_id&quot;) or str(uuid.uuid4())
        session.sql(merge_sql, (embedding_id, it.get(&quot;org_id&quot;), it.get(&quot;document_id&quot;), it.get(&quot;section_id&quot;) or it.get(&quot;clause_id&quot;), json.dumps(it.get(&quot;vector&quot;) or []), json.dumps(it.get(&quot;metadata&quot;) or {}), embedding_id)).collect()
    return {&quot;ingested&quot;: len(items)}



infra/register/via_connector.py — registration helper (register procedures from stage if connector unavailable)

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import List
import os

def register_procedures_from_stage(session: Session, stage_path: str, procedure_files: List[str]):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Fallback registrar: reads SQL/JS files from a stage path and executes them to register procedures/functions.
    Assumes files are small SQL/CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE scripts retrievable via &#39;@{stage_path}/file.sql&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    for fname in procedure_files:
        full_ref = f&quot;@{stage_path}/{fname}&quot;
        # Read file content via GET? In many deployments, the file is already staged and registration SQL references IMPORTS = (&#39;@~/file.py&#39;)
        # Here we attempt a simple registration pattern that sources the staged SQL via IDENTIFIER =&gt; using GET not available in session; instead issue a CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE referencing IMPORTS if needed.
        # For portability, attempt to load the file into a VARIANT via GET_FILE (pseudo). If environment doesn&#39;t support, rely on documented stage registration patterns.
        # Minimal approach: call a documented registration wrapper that expects registration SQL in stage as &#39;register_&lt;name&gt;.sql&#39;
        try:
            register_sql = session.sql(f&quot;select $1 from table(stage_read(&#39;{full_ref}&#39;))&quot;).collect()
        except Exception:
            # Fallback: log and continue
            continue
        if register_sql:
            sql_text = register_sql[0][0]
            session.sql(sql_text).collect()
    return {&quot;registered&quot;: len(procedure_files)}



infra/snowflake_provisioning.py — provisioning helper (create roles, stages, grants)

from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def create_role_and_grants(session: Session, role_name: str, target_schema: str, grantee: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Creates a role and grants USAGE on the schema and SELECT/INSERT on tables as examples.
    Intended to be run by an administrative user.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;CREATE ROLE IF NOT EXISTS {role_name}&quot;).collect()
    session.sql(f&quot;GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA {target_schema} TO ROLE {role_name}&quot;).collect()
    # Example: grant select on all tables in schema (template)
    session.sql(f&quot;GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA {target_schema} TO ROLE {role_name}&quot;).collect()
    session.sql(f&quot;GRANT ROLE {role_name} TO USER {grantee}&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;role&quot;: role_name, &quot;granted_to&quot;: grantee}



write_evidence_bundle.py — Snowpark Python: build a staged evidence package (zip manifest + metadata upsert)

# write_evidence_bundle.py
# Consolidated implementation: collects staged evidence items, packages them into a zip,
# writes zip to a deterministic stage path, and upserts manifest/contents into AI_FEATURE_HUB tables.
import os
import io
import json
import uuid
import zipfile
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def write_evidence_bundle(session: Session, manifest_id: str, org_id: str, item_refs: List[Dict[str, Any]], target_stage_prefix: str, created_by: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    item_refs: list of dicts with keys: document_id, staged_path (e.g., @mystage/path/file.pdf), optional filename
    target_stage_prefix: e.g. @~/evidence_bundles/orgid
    Returns: dict with manifest_id, staged_zip_path, item_count
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    now = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    # deterministic zip name
    zip_name = f&quot;evidence_bundle_{org_id}_{manifest_id}.zip&quot;
    local_tmp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=&quot;evidence_bundle_&quot;)
    local_zip_path = os.path.join(local_tmp_dir, zip_name)

    # Note: In many Snowpark environments the actual file transfer to a Snowflake stage is done via PUT.
    # Here we create the zip locally and then rely on a Snowflake PUT command executed via SQL (using a client-side file URL).
    # Alternative deployments can upload to S3 and record the S3 path in manifest.

    # Create zip from staged item refs by downloading from stage via &#39;GET&#39; is not always available in session;
    # To keep this procedure portable we will assume the caller has ensured the staged files are accessible locally on the worker,
    # otherwise we create a manifest-only package with references (deterministic).
    with zipfile.ZipFile(local_zip_path, mode=&quot;w&quot;, compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for itm in item_refs:
            fname = itm.get(&quot;filename&quot;) or f&quot;{itm.get(&#39;document_id&#39;)}.bin&quot;
            # If a local_path is provided, include file; otherwise include a small pointer file containing the staged_path
            local_path = itm.get(&quot;local_path&quot;)
            staged_path = itm.get(&quot;staged_path&quot;)
            if local_path and os.path.exists(local_path):
                zf.write(local_path, arcname=fname)
            else:
                # write pointer file
                pointer = {&quot;document_id&quot;: itm.get(&quot;document_id&quot;), &quot;staged_path&quot;: staged_path}
                zf.writestr(fname + &quot;.pointer.json&quot;, json.dumps(pointer))

    # Build stage path
    staged_zip_path = f&quot;{target_stage_prefix.rstrip(&#39;/&#39;)}/{zip_name}&quot;

    # Attempt to PUT file to user stage via Snowflake PUT using the internal connector if available
    # Many Snowpark installs do not expose a direct PUT API. We attempt to use the session connection cursor as fallback.
    try:
        # Use connector cursor to execute PUT (requires file be accessible to client running this code)
        conn = session._conn  # this uses internal connector; widely used pattern in Snowpark procs
        cur = conn.cursor()
        put_stmt = f&quot;PUT file://{local_zip_path} {staged_zip_path} OVERWRITE=TRUE&quot;
        cur.execute(put_stmt)
    except Exception:
        # If PUT is not available in this runtime, record the manifest and let infra pick up the local zip file.
        pass

    # Upsert manifest record and items (deterministic metadata)
    manifest_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.docgen_archive_manifest tgt
    USING (SELECT &#39;{manifest_id}&#39; AS manifest_id) src
      ON tgt.manifest_id = src.manifest_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET export_status = &#39;STAGED&#39;, package_path = &#39;{staged_zip_path}&#39;, finished_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (manifest_id, org_id, package_path, package_size_bytes, export_status, created_by, created_at)
      VALUES (&#39;{manifest_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{staged_zip_path}&#39;, NULL, &#39;STAGED&#39;, &#39;{created_by}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(manifest_sql).collect()

    # Upsert item rows
    for itm in item_refs:
        item_id = itm.get(&quot;item_id&quot;) or str(uuid.uuid4())
        doc_id = itm.get(&quot;document_id&quot;)
        item_path = itm.get(&quot;staged_path&quot;) or None
        metadata = json.dumps(itm.get(&quot;metadata&quot;) or {})
        insert_item = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.evidence_bundle_items tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{item_id}&#39; AS item_id) src
          ON tgt.item_id = src.item_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET item_path = &#39;{item_path}&#39;, metadata = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{metadata}&#39;), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (item_id, manifest_id, org_id, document_id, item_type, item_path, metadata, created_at)
          VALUES (&#39;{item_id}&#39;,&#39;{manifest_id}&#39;,&#39;{org_id}&#39;,&#39;{doc_id}&#39;,&#39;FILE&#39;,&#39;{item_path}&#39;,PARSE_JSON(&#39;{metadata}&#39;),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        session.sql(insert_item).collect()

    return {&quot;manifest_id&quot;: manifest_id, &quot;staged_zip_path&quot;: staged_zip_path, &quot;item_count&quot;: len(item_refs)}



enforce_embedding_retention.py — Snowpark Python: enforce embedding retention policies (queue purge / mark retired)

# enforce_embedding_retention.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import uuid

def enforce_embedding_retention(session: Session, org_id: str, retention_days: int = 90) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Finds embeddings older than retention_days for org_id, inserts purge jobs into index_retirement_queue
    and marks embeddings as &#39;retired&#39; (soft-delete) in document_embeddings.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # 1) find embeddings older than retention
    q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT embedding_id, org_id, document_id
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings
    WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{retention_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()) AND retired IS NOT TRUE
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(q).collect()
    queued = 0
    for r in rows:
        emb_id = r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;]
        retirement_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        # insert into retirement queue
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_retirement_queue(retirement_id, index_snapshot_id, org_id, reason, created_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{retirement_id}&#39;, &#39;{emb_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;retention_policy&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        # soft-delete / mark retired
        session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings SET retired = TRUE, retired_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE embedding_id = &#39;{emb_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
        queued += 1
    return {&quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;queued_retirements&quot;: queued}



apply_provenance_retention.py — Snowpark Python: enforce provenance retention (delete or archive provenance records)

# apply_provenance_retention.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def apply_provenance_retention(session: Session, org_id: str, retention_days: int = 365) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Applies retention for provenance tables: finds provenance records older than retention_days and archives or deletes them.
    This implementation archives metadata rows into provenance_retention_audit and then deletes from main provenance table.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Select candidates
    select_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT provenance_id FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_provenance
    WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;
      AND created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{retention_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(select_sql).collect()
    processed = 0
    for r in rows:
        pid = r[&quot;PROVENANCE_ID&quot;]
        # archive minimal audit row
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.provenance_retention_audit(audit_id, provenance_id, org_id, action, action_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{str(uuid.uuid4())}&#39;, &#39;{pid}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;archived_for_retention&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        # delete row
        session.sql(f&quot;DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_provenance WHERE provenance_id = &#39;{pid}&#39;&quot;).collect()
        processed += 1
    return {&quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;archived&quot;: processed}



enforce_attachment_purge.py — Snowpark Python: purge customer attachments per retention jobs (uploads can be staged or S3)

# enforce_attachment_purge.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import boto3
import os
from urllib.parse import urlparse

def _parse_stage_or_s3(uri: str):
    # support s3://bucket/key or @stage/path or https://s3...
    if uri.startswith(&quot;s3://&quot;):
        parsed = urlparse(uri)
        return (&quot;s3&quot;, parsed.netloc, parsed.path.lstrip(&quot;/&quot;))
    return (&quot;stage&quot;, None, uri)

def enforce_attachment_purge(session: Session, max_to_process: int = 100) -&gt; dict:
    # Fetch pending purge jobs
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT purge_id, artifact_path FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.customer_attachment_purge_jobs WHERE status = &#39;PENDING&#39; LIMIT {max_to_process}&quot;).collect()
    s3 = boto3.client(&quot;s3&quot;)
    processed = 0
    for r in rows:
        pid = r[&quot;PURGE_ID&quot;]
        path = r[&quot;ARTIFACT_PATH&quot;]
        kind, bucket, key = _parse_stage_or_s3(path)
        success = False
        try:
            if kind == &quot;s3&quot;:
                s3.delete_object(Bucket=bucket, Key=key)
                success = True
            else:
                # stage path: attempt to REMOVE via Snowflake API
                # We will attempt a stage remove SQL command if path looks like @stage/path
                try:
                    session.sql(f&quot;REMOVE &#39;{path}&#39;&quot;).collect()
                    success = True
                except Exception:
                    success = False
            status = &#39;DONE&#39; if success else &#39;FAILED&#39;
            session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.customer_attachment_purge_jobs SET status = &#39;{status}&#39;, processed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE purge_id = &#39;{pid}&#39;&quot;).collect()
            processed += 1
        except Exception as e:
            session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.customer_attachment_purge_jobs SET status = &#39;FAILED&#39;, error_message = &#39;{str(e).replace(\&quot;&#39;\&quot;, \&quot;\\&#39;\&quot;)}&#39;, processed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE purge_id = &#39;{pid}&#39;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed}



advanced_pricing_admin.py — Streamlit admin UI to create/edit RATE_RULE_TEMPLATES and RATE_RULES (consolidated, runnable)

# advanced_pricing_admin.py
import streamlit as st
import json
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

# Config: in production set via Secrets or ENV. Here we expect SNOWFLAKE_CONFIG env or streamlit secrets
def get_session():
    # Example: ST expects SNOWFLAKE_CONFIG dict in st.secrets or env-based builder
    cfg = st.secrets.get(&quot;snowflake&quot;, {})
    return Session.builder.configs(cfg).create()

def list_templates(session):
    return session.sql(&quot;SELECT template_id, name, description, created_at FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rule_templates ORDER BY created_at DESC&quot;).collect()

def upsert_template(session, template_id, name, description, template_json):
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rule_templates tgt
    USING (SELECT &#39;{template_id}&#39; AS template_id) src
      ON tgt.template_id = src.template_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET name = &#39;{name}&#39;, description = &#39;{description}&#39;, template = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(template_json)}&#39;), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (template_id, name, description, template, created_at) VALUES (&#39;{template_id}&#39;, &#39;{name}&#39;, &#39;{description}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(template_json)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

def main():
    st.title(&quot;Advanced Pricing Admin&quot;)
    session = get_session()
    st.sidebar.header(&quot;Actions&quot;)
    action = st.sidebar.selectbox(&quot;Action&quot;, [&quot;List Templates&quot;, &quot;Create/Edit Template&quot;, &quot;List Rules&quot;, &quot;Create Rule&quot;])
    if action == &quot;List Templates&quot;:
        rows = list_templates(session)
        for r in rows:
            st.write(r.asDict())
    elif action == &quot;Create/Edit Template&quot;:
        template_id = st.text_input(&quot;Template ID&quot;, value=&quot;&quot;)
        name = st.text_input(&quot;Name&quot;, value=&quot;&quot;)
        description = st.text_area(&quot;Description&quot;, value=&quot;&quot;)
        template_raw = st.text_area(&quot;Template JSON&quot;, value=&quot;{}&quot;)
        if st.button(&quot;Save Template&quot;):
            try:
                template_json = json.loads(template_raw)
                if not template_id:
                    template_id = &quot;tpl_&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]
                upsert_template(session, template_id, name, description, template_json)
                st.success(&quot;Saved&quot;)
            except Exception as e:
                st.error(f&quot;Error: {e}&quot;)
    elif action == &quot;List Rules&quot;:
        rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT rule_id, template_id, feature_key, effective_from, effective_to, created_at FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rules ORDER BY created_at DESC&quot;).collect()
        for r in rows:
            st.write(r.asDict())
    elif action == &quot;Create Rule&quot;:
        rule_id = st.text_input(&quot;Rule ID (optional)&quot;)
        template_id = st.text_input(&quot;Template ID&quot;)
        feature_key = st.text_input(&quot;Feature Key&quot;)
        effective_from = st.date_input(&quot;Effective From&quot;)
        effective_to = st.date_input(&quot;Effective To&quot;)
        if st.button(&quot;Create Rule&quot;):
            rid = rule_id or (&quot;rule_&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8])
            session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rules(rule_id, template_id, feature_key, effective_from, effective_to, created_at)
            VALUES(&#39;{rid}&#39;,&#39;{template_id}&#39;,&#39;{feature_key}&#39;, to_date(&#39;{effective_from.isoformat()}&#39;), to_date(&#39;{effective_to.isoformat()}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
            st.success(&quot;Created&quot;)
if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    main()



faiss_index_builder.py — wrapper to call FAISS index builder (consolidated, calls index loader and handles manifest)

# faiss_index_builder.py
import os
import json
import uuid
from typing import List
from pathlib import Path
from faiss import IndexFlatL2  # requires faiss installed
# This wrapper invokes the lower-level index building logic (index_loader_prod style)

def build_index_from_ndjson_files(json_files: List[str], output_dir: str, metric: str = &quot;L2&quot;) -&gt; dict:
    import numpy as np
    try:
        import faiss
    except Exception as e:
        raise RuntimeError(&quot;faiss library required: &quot; + str(e))
    ids = []
    vecs = []
    dim = None
    for f in json_files:
        with open(f, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
            for ln in fh:
                if not ln.strip():
                    continue
                obj = json.loads(ln)
                vid = str(obj.get(&quot;id&quot;))
                v = obj.get(&quot;vec&quot;)
                if v is None:
                    continue
                if dim is None:
                    dim = len(v)
                elif len(v) != dim:
                    raise ValueError(&quot;dimension mismatch&quot;)
                ids.append(vid)
                vecs.append(v)
    if not vecs:
        raise RuntimeError(&quot;no vectors&quot;)
    X = np.array(vecs, dtype=&quot;float32&quot;)
    if metric.upper() in (&quot;IP&quot;, &quot;INNER_PRODUCT&quot;):
        index = faiss.IndexFlatIP(X.shape[1])
    else:
        index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(X.shape[1])
    index.add(X)
    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)
    index_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f&quot;index_{uuid.uuid4().hex}.faiss&quot;)
    faiss.write_index(index, index_path)
    idmap_path = os.path.join(output_dir, &quot;id_map.json&quot;)
    with open(idmap_path, &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
        json.dump(ids, fh)
    manifest = {&quot;index_path&quot;: index_path, &quot;id_map&quot;: idmap_path, &quot;count&quot;: len(ids)}
    with open(os.path.join(output_dir, &quot;manifest.json&quot;), &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
        json.dump(manifest, fh)
    return manifest



similarity_service.py — FastAPI FAISS similarity microservice (consolidated)

# similarity_service.py
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import List, Dict, Any
import faiss
import numpy as np
import json

app = FastAPI(title=&quot;FAISS Similarity Service&quot;)

class QueryRequest(BaseModel):
    vector: List[float]
    top_k: int = 10

class QueryResultItem(BaseModel):
    id: str
    score: float

class QueryResponse(BaseModel):
    results: List[QueryResultItem]
    took_ms: int

# Load index and id_map at startup
INDEX_PATH = &quot;/opt/faiss/index.faiss&quot;
ID_MAP_PATH = &quot;/opt/faiss/id_map.json&quot;
_index = None
_idmap = None

@app.on_event(&quot;startup&quot;)
def load_index():
    global _index, _idmap
    try:
        _index = faiss.read_index(INDEX_PATH)
        with open(ID_MAP_PATH, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
            _idmap = json.load(fh)
    except Exception as e:
        _index = None
        _idmap = None

@app.post(&quot;/v1/query&quot;, response_model=QueryResponse)
def query(req: QueryRequest):
    if _index is None or _idmap is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=&quot;index not loaded&quot;)
    vec = np.array(req.vector, dtype=&quot;float32&quot;).reshape(1, -1)
    D, I = _index.search(vec, req.top_k)
    res = []
    for score, idx in zip(D[0].tolist(), I[0].tolist()):
        if idx &lt; 0:
            continue
        res.append(QueryResultItem(id=_idmap[idx], score=float(score)))
    return QueryResponse(results=res, took_ms=0)

infra/deploy_snowpark_container.py — helper to build/push docker images and register container-based external functions

# infra/deploy_snowpark_container.py
import subprocess
import os

def build_and_push(image_name: str, dockerfile_dir: str, registry: str, tag: str = &quot;latest&quot;):
    full_tag = f&quot;{registry}/{image_name}:{tag}&quot;
    subprocess.check_call([&quot;docker&quot;, &quot;build&quot;, &quot;-t&quot;, full_tag, dockerfile_dir])
    subprocess.check_call([&quot;docker&quot;, &quot;push&quot;, full_tag])
    return full_tag

def register_external_function(session, function_sql: str):
    # function_sql: full CREATE EXTERNAL FUNCTION ... statement
    session.sql(function_sql).collect()
    return {&quot;registered&quot;: True}



snapshot_index_uploader.py — upload built FAISS index artifacts to S3 or Snowflake stage (consolidated)

# snapshot_index_uploader.py
import os
import boto3
from urllib.parse import urlparse

def upload_to_s3(local_path: str, s3_uri: str, aws_profile: str = None) -&gt; dict:
    if aws_profile:
        import boto3
        session = boto3.Session(profile_name=aws_profile)
        s3 = session.client(&quot;s3&quot;)
    else:
        s3 = boto3.client(&quot;s3&quot;)
    parsed = urlparse(s3_uri)
    bucket = parsed.netloc
    key_prefix = parsed.path.lstrip(&quot;/&quot;)
    base = os.path.basename(local_path)
    key = f&quot;{key_prefix.rstrip(&#39;/&#39;)}/{base}&quot;
    s3.upload_file(local_path, bucket, key)
    return {&quot;s3_uri&quot;: f&quot;s3://{bucket}/{key}&quot;}

def upload_to_stage_via_put(session, local_path: str, stage_path: str) -&gt; dict:
    # PUT requires local file to be accessible to the Snowpark client process
    try:
        conn = session._conn
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute(f&quot;PUT file://{local_path} {stage_path} OVERWRITE=TRUE&quot;)
        return {&quot;stage_path&quot;: stage_path}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: str(e)}



persist_billing_runs.py — Snowpark Python to persist billing previews into final invoices (consolidated)

# persist_billing_runs.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any, List
import json, uuid

def persist_billing_runs(session: Session, older_than_minutes: int = 60, preview_only: bool = True) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Find rows in BILLING_PREVIEW older than threshold and persist them into SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES and BILLING_LINE_ITEM.
    If preview_only is True, only returns count; otherwise persists.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    preview_q = f&quot;select PREVIEW_ID, PREVIEW_JSON from AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW where PREVIEW_TS &lt;= dateadd(&#39;minute&#39;, -{older_than_minutes}, current_timestamp())&quot;
    rows = session.sql(preview_q).collect()
    persisted = []
    for r in rows:
        preview_id = r[&quot;PREVIEW_ID&quot;]
        preview_json = r[&quot;PREVIEW_JSON&quot;]
        data = json.loads(preview_json) if isinstance(preview_json, str) else preview_json
        invoice_hash = data.get(&quot;invoice_hash&quot;) or str(uuid.uuid4())
        account = data.get(&quot;account_id&quot;) or &quot;MULTI&quot;

        if not preview_only:
            # persist invoice header idempotently
            session.sql(
                f&quot;merge into AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES t using (select &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; as invoice_id) s on t.invoice_id = s.invoice_id &quot;
                &quot;when not matched then insert (invoice_id, account_id, period_start, period_end, subtotal, markup, tax, total, created_at) &quot;
                f&quot;values (&#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{account}&#39;, to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{data.get(&#39;period_start&#39;)}&#39;), to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{data.get(&#39;period_end&#39;)}&#39;), {data.get(&#39;subtotal&#39;)}, {data.get(&#39;markup&#39;)}, {data.get(&#39;tax&#39;)}, {data.get(&#39;total&#39;)}, current_timestamp())&quot;
            ).collect()

            for ln in data.get(&quot;lines&quot;, []):
                session.sql(
                    f&quot;merge into AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM t using (select &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; as billing_run_id, &#39;{ln.get(&#39;feature_key&#39;)}&#39; as feature_key) s &quot;
                    &quot;on t.billing_run_id = s.billing_run_id and t.feature_key = s.feature_key &quot;
                    &quot;when not matched then insert (billing_run_id, account_id, feature_key, usage_qty, base_cost, line_total, created_at) &quot;
                    f&quot;values (&#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{ln.get(&#39;account_id&#39;)}&#39;, &#39;{ln.get(&#39;feature_key&#39;)}&#39;, {ln.get(&#39;units&#39;)}, {ln.get(&#39;unit_price&#39;)}, {ln.get(&#39;line_total&#39;)}, current_timestamp())&quot;
                ).collect()

        persisted.append(preview_id)

        # Optionally mark preview row as persisted
        session.sql(f&quot;update AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW set persisted = true, persisted_at = current_timestamp() where PREVIEW_ID = &#39;{preview_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;persisted_count&quot;: len(persisted), &quot;preview_only&quot;: preview_only}

# record_embedding_deletion.py

from typing import Dict, Any

def record_embedding_deletion(session: Session, embedding_id: str, deleted_by: str, reason: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:

    ).collect()

run_embedding_quality_checks.py — Snowpark Python to run embedding quality validations (consolidated)

# run_embedding_quality_checks.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any, List
import numpy as np

def run_embedding_quality_checks(session: Session, org_id: str, sample_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Sample embeddings for org_id and compute simple quality metrics:
    - dimension consistency
    - norm distribution (for normalization checks)
    - empty vector counts
    Results written to AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_quality_metrics.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    sample_q = f&quot;select embedding_id, vector from AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings where org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; limit {sample_limit}&quot;
    rows = session.sql(sample_q).collect()
    dims = []
    empty_count = 0
    norms = []
    for r in rows:
        vec = r[&quot;VECTOR&quot;]
        if not vec:
            empty_count += 1
            continue
        v = np.array(vec, dtype=&quot;float32&quot;)
        dims.append(v.size)
        norms.append(float(np.linalg.norm(v)))

    dim_set = set(dims)
    avg_norm = float(np.mean(norms)) if norms else 0.0
    metric_id = f&quot;eqm-{org_id}-{str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]}&quot; if False else f&quot;eqm-{org_id}&quot;

    session.sql(
        f&quot;insert into AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_quality_metrics(metric_id, org_id, sample_count, empty_count, dimension_cardinality, avg_norm, assessed_at) &quot;
        f&quot;values (&#39;{metric_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, {len(rows)}, {empty_count}, {len(dim_set)}, {avg_norm}, current_timestamp())&quot;
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;metric_id&quot;: metric_id, &quot;sample_count&quot;: len(rows), &quot;empty_count&quot;: empty_count, &quot;dimension_cardinality&quot;: len(dim_set), &quot;avg_norm&quot;: avg_norm}




purge_old_embeddings.py — Snowpark Python to purge old embeddings per policy (consolidated)

# purge_old_embeddings.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def purge_old_embeddings(session: Session, org_id: str, older_than_days: int = 365, batch_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Find embeddings older than threshold and create purge jobs; mark rows retired.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    candidates_q = f&quot;select embedding_id from AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings where org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; and created_at &lt; dateadd(day, -{older_than_days}, current_timestamp()) and retired is not true limit {batch_limit}&quot;
    rows = session.sql(candidates_q).collect()
    queued = 0
    for r in rows:
        emb_id = r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;]
        purge_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        session.sql(
            f&quot;insert into AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_purge_jobs(purge_id, embedding_id, org_id, status, created_at) values (&#39;{purge_id}&#39;, &#39;{emb_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;QUEUED&#39;, current_timestamp())&quot;
        ).collect()
        session.sql(f&quot;update AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings set retired = true, retired_at = current_timestamp() where embedding_id = &#39;{emb_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
        queued += 1
    return {&quot;queued&quot;: queued}



compute_quality_metrics.py — Snowpark Python for quality metric computations &amp; storing results (consolidated)

# compute_quality_metrics.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import numpy as np

def compute_quality_metrics(session: Session, org_id: str, sample_limit: int = 500) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Compute several quality metrics (dimension consistency, mean similarity, norm stats) and upsert into quality_metrics table.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;select embedding_id, vector from AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings where org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; limit {sample_limit}&quot;).collect()
    vecs = []
    for r in rows:
        v = r[&quot;VECTOR&quot;]
        if v:
            vecs.append(np.array(v, dtype=&quot;float32&quot;))
    if not vecs:
        metrics = {&quot;count&quot;: 0}
    else:
        dims = [v.size for v in vecs]
        mean_dim = float(np.mean(dims))
        norms = [float(np.linalg.norm(v)) for v in vecs]
        metrics = {
            &quot;count&quot;: len(vecs),
            &quot;mean_dim&quot;: mean_dim,
            &quot;min_norm&quot;: float(np.min(norms)),
            &quot;max_norm&quot;: float(np.max(norms)),
            &quot;avg_norm&quot;: float(np.mean(norms)),
        }

    metric_id = f&quot;qm-{org_id}&quot;
    session.sql(
        f&quot;merge into AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_quality_summary tgt using (select &#39;{metric_id}&#39; as metric_id) src on tgt.metric_id = src.metric_id &quot;
        &quot;when matched then update set metrics = parse_json(&#39;{json.dumps(metrics)}&#39;), updated_at = current_timestamp() &quot;
        &quot;when not matched then insert (metric_id, org_id, metrics, created_at) values (&#39;{metric_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, parse_json(&#39;{json.dumps(metrics)}&#39;), current_timestamp())&quot;
    ).collect()
    return {&quot;metric_id&quot;: metric_id, &quot;metrics&quot;: metrics}



rollup_semantic_index_stats.py — Snowpark Python to roll up index stats (consolidated)

# rollup_semantic_index_stats.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def rollup_semantic_index_stats(session: Session, index_id: str, window_days: int = 7) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregate usage/maintenance stats for a semantic index into a summary table (daily/weekly).
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    agg_q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    select
      &#39;{index_id}&#39; as index_id,
      count(*) as total_queries,
      sum(case when success = true then 1 else 0 end) as successes,
      avg(latency_ms) as avg_latency
    from AI_FEATURE_HUB.semantic_index_query_log
    where index_id = &#39;{index_id}&#39; and created_at &gt;= dateadd(day, -{window_days}, current_timestamp())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    row = session.sql(agg_q).collect()
    if row:
        r = row[0]
        session.sql(
            f&quot;insert into AI_FEATURE_HUB.semantic_index_stats(index_id, period_days, total_queries, successes, avg_latency, rolled_at) values (&#39;{index_id}&#39;, {window_days}, {r[&#39;TOTAL_QUERIES&#39;]}, {r[&#39;SUCCESSES&#39;]}, {r[&#39;AVG_LATENCY&#39;]}, current_timestamp())&quot;
        ).collect()
        return {&quot;index_id&quot;: index_id, &quot;total_queries&quot;: int(r[&quot;TOTAL_QUERIES&quot;])}
    return {&quot;index_id&quot;: index_id, &quot;total_queries&quot;: 0}



link_query_to_provenance.py — Snowpark Python to link a similarity/query result to provenance records (consolidated)

# link_query_to_provenance.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json
from typing import Dict, Any, List

def link_query_to_provenance(session: Session, query_id: str, results: List[Dict[str, Any]], linked_by: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Given a query_id and a list of result items (each containing document_id, section_id, score),
    insert provenance_link rows and optionally update a query_provenance summary.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    inserted = 0
    for r in results:
        prov_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        doc_id = r.get(&quot;document_id&quot;)
        section_id = r.get(&quot;section_id&quot;)
        score = float(r.get(&quot;score&quot;, 0.0))
        session.sql(
            f&quot;insert into AI_FEATURE_HUB.query_provenance(provenance_id, query_id, document_id, section_id, score, linked_by, linked_at) &quot;
            f&quot;values (&#39;{prov_id}&#39;, &#39;{query_id}&#39;, &#39;{doc_id}&#39;, &#39;{section_id}&#39;, {score}, &#39;{linked_by}&#39;, current_timestamp())&quot;
        ).collect()
        inserted += 1

    # update summary count
    session.sql(
        f&quot;merge into AI_FEATURE_HUB.query_provenance_summary tgt using (select &#39;{query_id}&#39; as query_id) src on tgt.query_id = src.query_id &quot;
        &quot;when matched then update set linked_count = linked_count + {inserted}, last_linked_at = current_timestamp() &quot;
        &quot;when not matched then insert (query_id, linked_count, last_linked_at) values (&#39;{query_id}&#39;, {inserted}, current_timestamp())&quot;
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;query_id&quot;: query_id, &quot;linked&quot;: inserted}

322 index_snapshot_roller.py — rotate snapshots (keep N latest) and mark metadata
# index_snapshot_roller.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def roll_index_snapshots(session: Session, index_id: str, keep_latest: int = 3) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Retain only the most-recent `keep_latest` snapshots for index_id; mark older as &#39;archived&#39;.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        session.sql(
            &quot;WITH ranked AS (SELECT snapshot_id, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY created_at DESC) rn FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_snapshots WHERE index_id = %s) &quot;
            &quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_snapshots s SET status = &#39;ARCHIVED&#39; FROM ranked r WHERE s.snapshot_id = r.snapshot_id AND r.rn &gt; %s&quot;,
            (index_id, keep_latest)
        ).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

def is_item_embargoed(session: Session, item_id: str) -&gt; Dict[str, bool]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Returns whether the given item_id is currently under embargo (true/false).
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        rows = session.sql(
            &quot;SELECT COUNT(*) as ct FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.embargo_schedule es JOIN AI_FEATURE_HUB.evidence_items ei ON ei.bundle_id = es.bundle_id &quot;
            &quot;WHERE ei.item_id = %s AND es.status = &#39;SCHEDULED&#39; AND es.release_at &gt; CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()&quot;,
            (item_id,)
        ).collect()
        ct = int(rows[0][&quot;CT&quot;]) if rows else 0
        return {&quot;embargoed&quot;: ct &gt; 0}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

325 provisioning_usage_quota_enforcer.py — enforce per-account quota on feature usage
# provisioning_usage_quota_enforcer.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict

def enforce_usage_quota(session: Session, account_id: str, feature_key: str, increment_units: float = 1.0) -&gt; Dict[str, object]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Atomically checks quota and increments usage; returns {&#39;allowed&#39;: bool, &#39;remaining&#39;: float}.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        # Atomic MERGE-style update pattern
        session.sql(
            &quot;MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_quotas tgt USING (SELECT %s AS account_id, %s AS feature_key) src &quot;
            &quot;ON tgt.account_id = src.account_id AND tgt.feature_key = src.feature_key &quot;
            &quot;WHEN MATCHED AND tgt.used + %s &lt;= tgt.quota THEN UPDATE SET used = used + %s, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() &quot;
            &quot;WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (account_id, feature_key, used, quota, created_at) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, 0, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
            (account_id, feature_key, increment_units, increment_units, account_id, feature_key, increment_units)
        ).collect()
        # Read remaining
        rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT quota, used FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_quotas WHERE account_id = %s AND feature_key = %s&quot;, (account_id, feature_key)).collect()
        if not rows:
            return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;remaining&quot;: 0.0}
        quota = float(rows[0][&quot;QUOTA&quot;])
        used = float(rows[0][&quot;USED&quot;])
        remaining = max(0.0, quota - used)
        allowed = used &lt;= quota
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: allowed, &quot;remaining&quot;: remaining}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

326 embedding_vector_normalizer.py — L2-normalize vectors before storage/query
# embedding_vector_normalizer.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, math
from typing import List, Dict

def normalize_vector(vec: List[float]) -&gt; List[float]:
    norm = math.sqrt(sum(float(x) * float(x) for x in vec)) or 1.0
    return [float(x) / norm for x in vec]

def normalize_and_store(session: Session, embedding_id: str, vector_json: str) -&gt; Dict[str, object]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Normalizes a JSON array vector and updates document_embeddings.vector field.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        vec = json.loads(vector_json)
        normed = normalize_vector(vec)
        session.sql(
            &quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings SET vector = PARSE_JSON(%s), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE embedding_id = %s&quot;,
            (json.dumps(normed), embedding_id)
        ).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;, &quot;dim&quot;: len(normed)}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

330 — run_billing_enhanced.py — Billing run (aggregates usage → preview or commit). Sources:
# run_billing_enhanced.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from hashlib import sha256
from datetime import datetime
import json

def run_billing_enhanced(session: Session, run_id: str, window_start: str, window_end: str, dry_run: bool = True, markup_pct: float = 0.10):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregate TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE for window, compute line items, subtotal, markup, taxes,
    and either return preview JSON (dry_run=True) or persist into SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES/BILLING_LINE_ITEM idempotently.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ts = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    # 1) aggregate usage
    usage_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(units) AS units, SUM(cost) AS raw_cost
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE
    WHERE usage_timestamp &gt;= TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{window_start}&#39;)
      AND usage_timestamp &lt; TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{window_end}&#39;)
    GROUP BY account_id, feature_key
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(usage_sql).collect()

    # 2) build line items
    lines = []
    subtotal = 0.0
    for r in rows:
        account_id = r[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;]
        feature_key = r[&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;]
        units = float(r[&quot;UNITS&quot;] or 0)
        base_cost = float(r[&quot;RAW_COST&quot;] or 0)
        line_subtotal = base_cost
        lines.append({
            &quot;account_id&quot;: account_id,
            &quot;feature_key&quot;: feature_key,
            &quot;units&quot;: units,
            &quot;unit_cost&quot;: (line_subtotal / units) if units else 0.0,
            &quot;line_subtotal&quot;: line_subtotal
        })
        subtotal += line_subtotal

    # 3) markup, tax, totals
    markup = round(subtotal * float(markup_pct), 6)
    tax = round((subtotal + markup) * 0.0, 6)  # default 0% tax; replace if tax rules applied
    total = round(subtotal + markup + tax, 6)

    # 4) invoice hash for idempotency
    payload = {&quot;run_id&quot;: run_id, &quot;window_start&quot;: window_start, &quot;window_end&quot;: window_end, &quot;subtotal&quot;: subtotal, &quot;markup&quot;: markup, &quot;tax&quot;: tax, &quot;total&quot;: total, &quot;lines&quot;: lines}
    invoice_hash = sha256(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True).encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;)).hexdigest()

    preview = {&quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;payload&quot;: payload, &quot;generated_at&quot;: ts}

    if dry_run:
        return preview

    # 5) persist idempotently (MERGE on invoice_hash)
    try:
        # invoice header
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS invoice_hash) src
        ON tgt.invoice_hash = src.invoice_hash
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
          INSERT (invoice_id, invoice_hash, run_id, subtotal, markup, tax, total, created_at)
          VALUES (UUID_STRING(), &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{run_id}&#39;, {subtotal}, {markup}, {tax}, {total}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        # line items: delete existing then insert (simpler idempotency)
        session.sql(f&quot;DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM WHERE invoice_hash = &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;&quot;).collect()
        for ln in lines:
            session.sql(
                &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM (line_id, invoice_hash, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_cost, line_subtotal, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
                (invoice_hash, ln[&quot;account_id&quot;], ln[&quot;feature_key&quot;], ln[&quot;units&quot;], ln[&quot;unit_cost&quot;], ln[&quot;line_subtotal&quot;])
            ).collect()

        # run audit
        session.sql(
            &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RUN_AUDIT (audit_id, run_id, invoice_hash, committed_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
            (run_id, invoice_hash)
        ).collect()

        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;COMMITTED&quot;, &quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

331 — persist_billing_proc.py — Persist preview rows to final invoices (bulk commit). Source:
# persist_billing_proc.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from datetime import datetime

def persist_billing_proc(session: Session, older_than_minutes: int = 10, preview_only: bool = False):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Move rows from AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW older than X minutes into SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES/BILLING_LINE_ITEM.
    Optionally run in preview mode (no commit).
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    cutoff = f&quot;DATEADD(minute, -{int(older_than_minutes)}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    preview_rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW WHERE created_at &lt; {cutoff}&quot;).collect()
    results = []
    for pr in preview_rows:
        invoice_hash = pr[&quot;INVOICE_HASH&quot;]
        if preview_only:
            results.append({&quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;PREVIEW&quot;})
            continue
        try:
            # idempotent header
            session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES tgt
            USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS invoice_hash) src
            ON tgt.invoice_hash = src.invoice_hash
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (invoice_id, invoice_hash, run_id, subtotal, markup, tax, total, created_at)
            VALUES (UUID_STRING(), &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, {repr(pr.get(&#39;RUN_ID&#39;))}, {pr.get(&#39;SUBTOTAL&#39;,0)}, {pr.get(&#39;MARKUP&#39;,0)}, {pr.get(&#39;TAX&#39;,0)}, {pr.get(&#39;TOTAL&#39;,0)}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

            # move line items
            session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM (line_id, invoice_hash, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_cost, line_subtotal, created_at)
            SELECT UUID_STRING(), invoice_hash, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_cost, line_subtotal, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW_LINE WHERE invoice_hash = &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;
            &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

            # delete preview rows
            session.sql(&quot;DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW_LINE WHERE invoice_hash = %s&quot;, (invoice_hash,)).collect()
            session.sql(&quot;DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW WHERE invoice_hash = %s&quot;, (invoice_hash,)).collect()
            results.append({&quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;COMMITTED&quot;})
        except Exception as e:
            results.append({&quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)})
    return {&quot;results&quot;: results}

334 — approve_rate_rule.py — Promote staging rule into RATE_RULES and record APPROVE audit. Sources:
# approve_rate_rule.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def approve_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_id: str, approver: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Merge staging row into RATE_RULES (upsert) and mark staging as approved; write APPROVE audit.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        # Merge into active rules
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES tgt
        USING (SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING WHERE staging_id = &#39;{staging_id}&#39;) src
        ON tgt.rule_key = src.rule_key
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET rule_json = src.rule_json, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (rule_id, rule_key, rule_json, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), src.rule_key, src.rule_json, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        # Mark staging approved
        session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING SET approved = TRUE, approved_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), approved_by = %s WHERE staging_id = %s&quot;, (approver, staging_id)).collect()

        # Audit
        session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (audit_id, staging_id, action, actor, acted_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, &#39;APPROVE&#39;, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (staging_id, approver)).collect()

        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;APPROVED&quot;}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

338 — pricing_engine.py — Pricing helpers: apply_tiers and apply_discount. Source:
# pricing_engine.py
from typing import List, Dict

def apply_tiers(units: float, tiers: List[Dict]) -&gt; float:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    tiers: list of {&quot;upto&quot;: &lt;units or None&gt;, &quot;price_per_unit&quot;: &lt;float&gt;} ordered ascending.
    &#39;upto&#39;=None means infinite.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    remaining = float(units)
    total = 0.0
    for t in tiers:
        upto = t.get(&quot;upto&quot;)
        p = float(t[&quot;price_per_unit&quot;])
        if upto is None:
            total += remaining * p
            remaining = 0
            break
        take = min(remaining, float(upto))
        total += take * p
        remaining -= take
        if remaining &lt;= 0:
            break
    return round(total, 6)

def apply_discount(subtotal: float, discount: Dict) -&gt; float:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    discount: {&quot;percent&quot;: float (0-100), &quot;flat&quot;: float}
    returns discount_amount
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    pct = float(discount.get(&quot;percent&quot;, 0)) / 100.0
    flat = float(discount.get(&quot;flat&quot;, 0.0))
    return round(subtotal * pct + flat, 6)

339 — write_evidence_bundle.py / assemble_evidence_bundle.py — Bundle invoice + audit -&gt; EVIDENCE_BUNDLES (staging + record). Sources:
# write_evidence_bundle.py (assemble + persist evidence bundle)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from datetime import datetime
import json
from hashlib import sha256

def assemble_evidence_bundle(session: Session, invoice_hash: str, org_id: str, created_by: str = None, target_stage: str = &quot;@AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_STAGE&quot;):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Collect rows related to invoice_hash (SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES, BILLING_LINE_ITEM, BILLING_RUN_AUDIT),
    assemble JSON bundle, compute bundle_hash, PUT to stage (via session.file.put or equivalent),
    and insert metadata into AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        # gather data
        inv = session.sql(&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES WHERE invoice_hash = %s&quot;, (invoice_hash,)).collect()
        lines = session.sql(&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM WHERE invoice_hash = %s&quot;, (invoice_hash,)).collect()
        audits = session.sql(&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RUN_AUDIT WHERE invoice_hash = %s&quot;, (invoice_hash,)).collect()

        bundle = {
            &quot;invoice&quot;: [dict(r.asDict()) for r in inv],
            &quot;lines&quot;: [dict(r.asDict()) for r in lines],
            &quot;audits&quot;: [dict(r.asDict()) for r in audits],
            &quot;assembled_at&quot;: datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
        }
        bundle_json = json.dumps(bundle, sort_keys=True)
        bundle_hash = sha256(bundle_json.encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;)).hexdigest()

        # stage the bundle JSON (PUT)
        stage_path = f&quot;{target_stage}/{bundle_hash}.json&quot;
        # Use session.file.put if available; fallback to INSERT into stage table or store metadata
        try:
            # write to local temp and PUT to stage (if runtime allows)
            with open(f&quot;/tmp/{bundle_hash}.json&quot;, &quot;w&quot;) as f:
                f.write(bundle_json)
            session.file.put(f&quot;/tmp/{bundle_hash}.json&quot;, stage_path, auto_compress=False)
            staged = True
        except Exception:
            staged = False

        # record evidence bundle metadata
        session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES (bundle_id, bundle_hash, invoice_hash, org_id, staged_path, staged, created_by, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (bundle_hash, invoice_hash, org_id, stage_path if staged else None, staged, created_by)).collect()

        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;, &quot;bundle_hash&quot;: bundle_hash, &quot;staged&quot;: staged, &quot;stage_path&quot;: stage_path}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

350 — billing_run_orchestrator.py — Orchestrates a billing run: calls the billing aggregation, optionally persists the preview, writes audit rows, and triggers evidence bundle assembly for committed invoices.
# billing_run_orchestrator.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, Any
import json
from hashlib import sha256

# Uses the canonical run_billing_enhanced, persist, and assemble evidence patterns.
def orchestrate_billing_run(session: Session, run_id: str, window_start: str, window_end: str, commit: bool = False, markup_pct: float = 0.10) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    1) Runs billing preview to produce invoice_hash and payload.
    2) If commit=True, persists invoice and line items idempotently and assembles evidence bundle.
    3) Always writes a run orchestration audit row.
    Returns the preview or commit result with orchestration metadata.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    started_at = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    # 1) Preview via run_billing_enhanced (assumes available in same repo / registered proc)
    try:
        # call the Python function in-session if available
        preview = None
        try:
            preview = session.call(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.RUN_BILLING_ENHANCED&quot;, [run_id, window_start, window_end, True, float(markup_pct)])
        except Exception:
            # fallback: import local helper if running outside stored proc registration
            from run_billing_enhanced import run_billing_enhanced
            preview = run_billing_enhanced(session, run_id, window_start, window_end, dry_run=True, markup_pct=markup_pct)
        invoice_hash = preview.get(&quot;invoice_hash&quot;) if isinstance(preview, dict) else None

        # write orchestration audit
        session.sql(
            &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_ORCHESTRATION_AUDIT (audit_id, run_id, invoice_hash, action, started_at, commit_requested, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, %s, &#39;ORCHESTRATE&#39;, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
            (run_id, invoice_hash, started_at, commit)
        ).collect()

        if not commit:
            return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;PREVIEW&quot;, &quot;preview&quot;: preview}
        # 2) Persist (call persist_billing_proc)
        try:
            persist_result = session.call(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.PERSIST_BILLING_PROC&quot;, [10, False])
        except Exception:
            from persist_billing_proc import persist_billing_proc
            persist_result = persist_billing_proc(session, older_than_minutes=0, preview_only=False)

        # 3) Assemble evidence for committed invoices (persist_result contains list of committed invoice_hashes)
        assembled = []
        for entry in (persist_result.get(&quot;results&quot;) or []):
            if entry.get(&quot;status&quot;) == &quot;COMMITTED&quot;:
                ih = entry.get(&quot;invoice_hash&quot;)
                try:
                    # call evidence assembler
                    try:
                        eb = session.call(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.ASSEMBLE_EVIDENCE_BUNDLE&quot;, [ih, None, None])
                    except Exception:
                        from write_evidence_bundle import assemble_evidence_bundle
                        eb = assemble_evidence_bundle(session, ih, org_id=None, created_by=&quot;orchestrator&quot;)
                    assembled.append({&quot;invoice_hash&quot;: ih, &quot;evidence&quot;: eb})
                except Exception as e:
                    assembled.append({&quot;invoice_hash&quot;: ih, &quot;evidence_error&quot;: str(e)})
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;COMMITTED&quot;, &quot;persist&quot;: persist_result, &quot;evidence&quot;: assembled}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}
