352 — entitlement_sync.py — Idempotent sync that upserts entitlements from an external JSON feed into AI_FEATURE_HUB.ENTITLEMENTS.
# entitlement_sync.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import List, Dict
import json

def sync_entitlements(session: Session, entitlements_json: str) -&gt; Dict[str, int]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    entitlements_json: JSON array of {account_id, feature_key, enabled, expires_at, metadata}
    Upserts each entitlement row and returns counts for upserted and errors.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        payload: List[Dict] = json.loads(entitlements_json)
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: f&quot;invalid_json: {e}&quot;}

    upserted = 0
    errors = 0
    for e in payload:
        try:
            account_id = e[&quot;account_id&quot;]
            feature_key = e[&quot;feature_key&quot;]
            enabled = bool(e.get(&quot;enabled&quot;, True))
            expires_at = e.get(&quot;expires_at&quot;)  # ISO string or None
            metadata = json.dumps(e.get(&quot;metadata&quot;, {}))
            session.sql(
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ENTITLEMENTS tgt
                USING (SELECT %s AS account_id, %s AS feature_key) src
                ON tgt.account_id = src.account_id AND tgt.feature_key = src.feature_key
                WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET enabled = %s, expires_at = TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s), metadata = PARSE_JSON(%s), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (entitlement_id, account_id, feature_key, enabled, expires_at, metadata, created_at)
                  VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, %s, %s, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s), PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                (account_id, feature_key, enabled, expires_at, metadata, account_id, feature_key, enabled, expires_at, metadata)
            ).collect()
            upserted += 1
        except Exception:
            errors += 1
            continue
    return {&quot;upserted&quot;: upserted, &quot;errors&quot;: errors}

353 — usage_batch_loader.py — Idempotent loader that reads staged usage rows (STAGED_USAGE) and MERGEs into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE; marks staged rows processed.
# usage_batch_loader.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def load_staged_usage(session: Session, batch_id: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Moves rows from AI_FEATURE_HUB.STAGED_USAGE where batch_id matches into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE idempotently.
    Deletes or marks staged rows as processed to avoid reprocessing.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        # Upsert into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE from STAGED_USAGE (idempotent by event_id)
        session.sql(
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
            USING (SELECT event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, usage_timestamp, raw_payload FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.STAGED_USAGE WHERE batch_id = %s AND processed = FALSE) src
            ON tgt.event_id = src.event_id
            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET account_id = src.account_id, feature_key = src.feature_key, units = src.units, usage_timestamp = src.usage_timestamp, raw_payload = src.raw_payload, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, usage_timestamp, raw_payload, created_at)
              VALUES (src.event_id, src.account_id, src.feature_key, src.units, src.usage_timestamp, src.raw_payload, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;,
            (batch_id,)
        ).collect()

        # Mark staged rows processed
        session.sql(
            &quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.STAGED_USAGE SET processed = TRUE, processed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE batch_id = %s AND processed = FALSE&quot;,
            (batch_id,)
        ).collect()

        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

354 — embedding_batch_exporter.py — Exports selected embeddings to an external location (e.g., S3) for FAISS snapshot build and writes a manifest row.
# embedding_batch_exporter.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import json
import time

def export_embeddings_for_faiss(session: Session, index_id: str, s3_stage_path: str, filter_sql: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Exports embeddings matching filter_sql (or all for index_id) to the provided stage path (COPY INTO &lt;stage&gt;).
    Writes a manifest row to AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_snapshots with snapshot metadata and returns snapshot_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        snapshot_id = f&quot;{index_id}-{int(time.time())}&quot;
        # Construct export query
        where_clause = f&quot;WHERE index_id = &#39;{index_id}&#39;&quot; + (f&quot; AND ({filter_sql})&quot; if filter_sql else &quot;&quot;)
        export_sql = f&quot;COPY INTO &#39;{s3_stage_path}/{snapshot_id}/&#39; FROM (SELECT embedding_id, document_id, vector, metadata FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS {where_clause}) FILE_FORMAT = (TYPE = &#39;JSON&#39;) OVERWRITE = TRUE&quot;
        session.sql(export_sql).collect()

        # Insert snapshot manifest
        manifest = {
            &quot;index_id&quot;: index_id,
            &quot;snapshot_id&quot;: snapshot_id,
            &quot;created_at&quot;: time.strftime(&quot;%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ&quot;, time.gmtime()),
        }
        session.sql(
            &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_snapshots (snapshot_id, index_id, manifest, created_at, status) VALUES (%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), &#39;CREATED&#39;)&quot;,
            (snapshot_id, index_id, json.dumps(manifest))
        ).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;, &quot;snapshot_id&quot;: snapshot_id}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

355 — faiss_snapshot_manifest_creator.py — Reads shard outputs and upserts shard manifest rows and the overall snapshot manifest.
# faiss_snapshot_manifest_creator.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import List, Dict, Any
import json
import time

def create_snapshot_manifest(session: Session, index_id: str, snapshot_id: str, shards: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    shards: list of {&quot;shard_id&quot;: str, &quot;shard_path&quot;: str, &quot;count&quot;: int}
    Writes/Upserts into index_shard_manifest and index_snapshots tables for discovery by similarity services.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        # Upsert snapshot
        manifest = {&quot;index_id&quot;: index_id, &quot;snapshot_id&quot;: snapshot_id, &quot;created_at&quot;: time.strftime(&quot;%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ&quot;, time.gmtime()), &quot;shards&quot;: shards}
        session.sql(
            &quot;MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_snapshots tgt USING (SELECT %s AS snapshot_id) src ON tgt.snapshot_id = src.snapshot_id WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET manifest = PARSE_JSON(%s), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (snapshot_id, index_id, manifest, created_at, status) VALUES (%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), &#39;READY&#39;)&quot;,
            (snapshot_id, json.dumps(manifest), snapshot_id, index_id, json.dumps(manifest))
        ).collect()

        # Upsert shards
        for s in shards:
            session.sql(
                &quot;MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.index_shard_manifest tgt USING (SELECT %s AS index_id, %s AS shard_id) src ON tgt.index_id = src.index_id AND tgt.shard_id = src.shard_id WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET shard_path = %s, records_count = %s, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (manifest_id, index_id, shard_id, shard_path, records_count, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
                (index_id, s[&quot;shard_id&quot;], s[&quot;shard_path&quot;], int(s.get(&quot;count&quot;, 0)), index_id, s[&quot;shard_id&quot;], s[&quot;shard_path&quot;], int(s.get(&quot;count&quot;, 0)))
            ).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;, &quot;snapshot_id&quot;: snapshot_id}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

356 — external_function_register_helper.py — Helper that creates API_INTEGRATION and EXTERNAL FUNCTION SQL programmatically; records registration rows.
# external_function_register_helper.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def register_external_function(session: Session, ef_name: str, api_integration_name: str, api_url: str, allowed_prefixes: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Creates an API_INTEGRATION (if not exists) and an EXTERNAL FUNCTION that points to the given api_url.
    Records a row in AI_FEATURE_HUB.external_functions_registry for discovery.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        # API_INTEGRATION creation (idempotent)
        api_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        CREATE OR REPLACE API_INTEGRATION {api_integration_name}
        API_PROVIDER = &#39;aws_api_gateway&#39;
        API_AWS_ROLE_ARN = &#39;REPLACE_WITH_ROLE_ARN&#39;
        ENABLE_OAUTH = FALSE
        ENABLED = TRUE
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        session.sql(api_sql).collect()

        # Create external function
        ef_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        CREATE OR REPLACE EXTERNAL FUNCTION {ef_name}(input VARIANT)
        RETURNS VARIANT
        API_INTEGRATION = {api_integration_name}
        AS &#39;{api_url}&#39;;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        session.sql(ef_sql).collect()

        # Registry row
        session.sql(
            &quot;MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.external_functions_registry tgt USING (SELECT %s AS ef_name) src ON tgt.ef_name = src.ef_name WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET api_url = %s, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (ef_id, ef_name, api_url, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
            (ef_name, api_url, ef_name, api_url)
        ).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;REGISTERED&quot;, &quot;ef_name&quot;: ef_name}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

357 — rate_rule_seeder.py — Consolidated seeder that promotes approved rules into RATE_RULES, with versioning and an audit row.
# rate_rule_seeder.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def seed_approved_rate_rules(session: Session, upsert_limit: int = 100) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Finds approved rows in RATE_RULES_STAGING and merges them into RATE_RULES with a version column.
    Writes an audit row per staging_id processed.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        approved = session.sql(&quot;SELECT staging_id, rule_key, rule_json FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING WHERE approved = TRUE AND processed = FALSE LIMIT %s&quot;, (upsert_limit,)).collect()
        processed = []
        for r in approved:
            staging_id = r[&quot;STAGING_ID&quot;]
            rule_key = r[&quot;RULE_KEY&quot;]
            rule_json = r[&quot;RULE_JSON&quot;]
            # Upsert into RATE_RULES; increment version if exists
            session.sql(
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES tgt
                USING (SELECT %s AS rule_key, %s AS rule_json) src
                ON tgt.rule_key = src.rule_key
                WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET rule_json = src.rule_json, version = COALESCE(tgt.version,0) + 1, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (rule_id, rule_key, rule_json, version, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), src.rule_key, src.rule_json, 1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                (rule_key, rule_json)
            ).collect()
            # mark staging processed
            session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING SET processed = TRUE, processed_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE staging_id = %s&quot;, (staging_id,)).collect()
            # audit
            session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (audit_id, staging_id, action, actor, acted_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, &#39;PROMOTE&#39;, &#39;seeder&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (staging_id,)).collect()
            processed.append(staging_id)
        return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

358 — invoice_hash_utils.py — Canonical, deterministic invoice hash utility (JSON canonicalization + sha256) used across preview/persist/compare flows.
# invoice_hash_utils.py
import json
from hashlib import sha256
from typing import Any, Dict

def canonical_invoice_hash(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Computes a deterministic SHA256 over a canonical JSON representation of the payload.
    Use this function anywhere invoice_hash must be computed to ensure idempotency across systems.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # sort keys, ensure separators to remove whitespace differences
    canonical = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    return sha256(canonical.encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;)).hexdigest()

359 — task_runner_helper.py — Idempotent helper to invoke and resume Snowflake TASKs safely (e.g., TASK_RUN_BILLING, TASK_EMBEDDING_INGEST).
# task_runner_helper.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict
import time

def run_task_now(session: Session, task_name: str, resume_if_suspended: bool = True, wait_seconds: int = 0) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Ensures task is resumed (if requested), triggers the task via SYSTEM$TASK_TRIGGER or ALTER TASK ... RESUME; returns task state.
    If wait_seconds &gt; 0, polls the task history for a short period and returns last run status.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        # Optionally resume
        if resume_if_suspended:
            session.sql(f&quot;ALTER TASK {task_name} RESUME&quot;).collect()
        # Trigger the task now
        try:
            session.sql(f&quot;CALL SYSTEM$TASK_TRIGGER(&#39;{task_name}&#39;)&quot;).collect()
        except Exception:
            # Fallback: alter schedule to run once via immediate run (best-effort)
            session.sql(f&quot;ALTER TASK {task_name} SET SCHEDULE = &#39;USING CRON * * * * * UTC&#39;&quot;).collect()
        if wait_seconds &gt; 0:
            deadline = time.time() + wait_seconds
            last_status = None
            while time.time() &lt; deadline:
                rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT LAST_ERROR, LAST_SUCCESS, STATE FROM TABLE(INFORMATION_SCHEMA.TASK_HISTORY()) WHERE NAME = %s ORDER BY COMPLETED_ON DESC LIMIT 1&quot;, (task_name,)).collect()
                if rows:
                    last_status = rows[0].asDict()
                    if last_status.get(&quot;STATE&quot;) in (&quot;SUCCEEDED&quot;, &quot;FAILED&quot;):
                        break
                time.sleep(1)
            return {&quot;triggered&quot;: True, &quot;last_status&quot;: last_status}
        return {&quot;triggered&quot;: True}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

360 — persist_billing_proc.py — Snowpark procedure that collects billing previews older than a threshold and persists invoice headers and line items idempotently using invoice_hash. Returns a summary of persisted invoices.
# persist_billing_proc.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session, Row
from typing import Dict, Any, List
import json
from invoice_hash_utils import canonical_invoice_hash

def persist_billing_runs(session: Session, older_than_minutes: int = 60, preview_only: bool = True) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Finds billing preview rows older than `older_than_minutes` in AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW,
    computes the deterministic invoice_hash for each preview payload, and persists header + lines
    into AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES and AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICE_LINES
    using idempotent MERGE semantics.
    If preview_only=True, returns preview results without persisting.
    Returns summary like {&quot;results&quot;:[{&quot;invoice_hash&quot;:..., &quot;status&quot;:&quot;COMMITTED&quot;|&quot;SKIPPED&quot;|&quot;PREVIEW&quot;}], ...}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    results: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    # select previews older than threshold
    previews = session.sql(
        &quot;SELECT preview_id, account_id, preview_payload FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW &quot;
        &quot;WHERE created_at &lt;= DATEADD(minute, -%s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (older_than_minutes,)
    ).collect()

    for p in previews:
        preview_id = p[&quot;PREVIEW_ID&quot;]
        account_id = p[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;]
        payload = p[&quot;PREVIEW_PAYLOAD&quot;]
        # ensure payload is dict
        if isinstance(payload, str):
            try:
                payload = json.loads(payload)
            except Exception:
                results.append({&quot;preview_id&quot;: preview_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;INVALID_PAYLOAD&quot;})
                continue

        invoice_hash = canonical_invoice_hash(payload)
        # PREVIEW path
        if preview_only:
            results.append({&quot;preview_id&quot;: preview_id, &quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;PREVIEW&quot;})
            continue

        # Persist header idempotently using MERGE on invoice_hash
        header_json = json.dumps(payload.get(&quot;header&quot;, {}))
        lines = payload.get(&quot;lines&quot;, [])
        try:
            session.sql(
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES tgt
                USING (SELECT %s AS invoice_hash, %s AS account_id) src
                ON tgt.invoice_hash = src.invoice_hash
                WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET account_id = src.account_id, header = PARSE_JSON(%s), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (invoice_id, invoice_hash, account_id, header, created_at)
                VALUES (UUID_STRING(), src.invoice_hash, src.account_id, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                (invoice_hash, account_id, header_json, header_json),
            ).collect()

            # Upsert each line (idempotent by invoice_hash + line_number)
            for idx, ln in enumerate(lines, start=1):
                line_json = json.dumps(ln)
                session.sql(
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;
                    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICE_LINES tgt
                    USING (SELECT %s AS invoice_hash, %s AS line_number) src
                    ON tgt.invoice_hash = src.invoice_hash AND tgt.line_number = src.line_number
                    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET line = PARSE_JSON(%s), amount = %s, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (line_id, invoice_hash, line_number, line, amount, created_at)
                    VALUES (UUID_STRING(), src.invoice_hash, src.line_number, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                    (invoice_hash, idx, line_json, float(ln.get(&quot;amount&quot;, 0.0)), line_json, float(ln.get(&quot;amount&quot;, 0.0))),
                ).collect()

            results.append({&quot;preview_id&quot;: preview_id, &quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;COMMITTED&quot;})
        except Exception as e:
            results.append({&quot;preview_id&quot;: preview_id, &quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)})

    return {&quot;results&quot;: results}

361 — reconcile_proc.py — Snowpark procedure to reconcile billed totals vs aggregated usage; writes reconciliation discrepancies to RECONCILIATION_AUDIT. Idempotent insertion on computed reconciliation_id.
# reconcile_proc.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import json
from hashlib import sha256

def reconcile_billing_vs_usage(session: Session, account_id: str = None, window_start: str = None, window_end: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregates usage from TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE and billing totals from BILLING_LINE_ITEM for the window.
    For each (account_id, feature_key) where abs(usage_total - billed_total) &gt; tolerance, insert into RECONCILIATION_AUDIT.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    tolerance = 0.01
    params = []
    where_usage = &quot;&quot;
    where_billing = &quot;&quot;
    if account_id:
        where_usage += &quot; AND account_id = %s&quot;
        where_billing += &quot; AND account_id = %s&quot;
        params.append(account_id)
    if window_start:
        where_usage += &quot; AND usage_timestamp &gt;= TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s)&quot;
        where_billing += &quot; AND billed_at &gt;= TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s)&quot;
        params.append(window_start)
    if window_end:
        where_usage += &quot; AND usage_timestamp &lt; TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s)&quot;
        where_billing += &quot; AND billed_at &lt; TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s)&quot;
        params.append(window_end)

    # aggregate usage
    usage_rows = session.sql(
        f&quot;SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(units) AS usage_total FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE WHERE 1=1 {where_usage} GROUP BY account_id, feature_key&quot;,
        tuple(params),
    ).collect()

    # aggregate billing
    billing_rows = session.sql(
        f&quot;SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(amount) AS billed_total FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM WHERE 1=1 {where_billing} GROUP BY account_id, feature_key&quot;,
        tuple(params),
    ).collect()

    # map for quick lookup
    billed_map = {(r[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;], r[&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;]): float(r[&quot;BILLED_TOTAL&quot;]) for r in billing_rows}

    inserted = []
    for r in usage_rows:
        key = (r[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;], r[&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;])
        usage_total = float(r[&quot;USAGE_TOTAL&quot;])
        billed_total = float(billed_map.get(key, 0.0))
        diff = usage_total - billed_total
        if abs(diff) &gt; tolerance:
            payload = {&quot;account_id&quot;: key[0], &quot;feature_key&quot;: key[1], &quot;usage_total&quot;: usage_total, &quot;billed_total&quot;: billed_total, &quot;diff&quot;: diff}
            reconciliation_id = sha256(json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
            # idempotent insert
            session.sql(
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RECONCILIATION_AUDIT tgt
                USING (SELECT %s AS reconciliation_id) src
                ON tgt.reconciliation_id = src.reconciliation_id
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (reconciliation_id, account_id, feature_key, usage_total, billed_total, diff, created_at)
                VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                (reconciliation_id, reconciliation_id, key[0], key[1], usage_total, billed_total, diff),
            ).collect()
            inserted.append(reconciliation_id)

    return {&quot;inserted&quot;: inserted}



363 — write_evidence_bundle.py — Exports invoice + supporting query results and line-item evidence into an evidence bundle table + stage; used post-commit to assemble archival artifact.
# write_evidence_bundle.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import json
import time

def assemble_evidence_bundle(session: Session, invoice_hash: str, org_id: str = None, created_by: str = &quot;system&quot;) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Collects invoice header, line items, relevant usage rows and query snapshots and writes a JSON bundle to a stage path,
    then records a manifest row in AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES (bundle_id, invoice_hash, stage_path, created_at).
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        header_row = session.sql(&quot;SELECT header FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES WHERE invoice_hash = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, (invoice_hash,)).collect()
        lines = session.sql(&quot;SELECT line_number, line FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICE_LINES WHERE invoice_hash = %s ORDER BY line_number&quot;, (invoice_hash,)).collect()
        # collect supporting usage and queries (example: fetch usage rows for accounts in the invoice)
        # For simplicity, bundle header + lines
        header = header_row[0][&quot;HEADER&quot;] if header_row else {}
        lines_json = [l[&quot;LINE&quot;].to_json() if hasattr(l[&quot;LINE&quot;], &quot;to_json&quot;) else json.dumps(l[&quot;LINE&quot;]) for l in lines]
        bundle = {&quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash, &quot;header&quot;: header, &quot;lines&quot;: [json.loads(x) if isinstance(x, str) else x for x in lines_json], &quot;assembled_at&quot;: time.strftime(&quot;%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ&quot;, time.gmtime())}
        bundle_str = json.dumps(bundle, ensure_ascii=False)
        stage_path = f&quot;@~/evidence_bundles/{invoice_hash}.json&quot;
        # put content to user stage as single file (use Snowflake PUT via SQL API; here we write record and assume external system fetches)
        session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES(bundle_id, invoice_hash, bundle_body, created_by, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (invoice_hash, bundle_str, created_by)).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;, &quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash}
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

364 — usageingest_sp.py — Idempotent Snowpark ingestion procedure for usage events (batch or single), validating and inserting to STAGED_USAGE or TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE.
# usageingest_sp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import json

def usage_ingest_sp(session: Session, events_json: str, staged: bool = True) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Accepts a JSON array of usage events. If staged=True, writes to AI_FEATURE_HUB.STAGED_USAGE for later batch processing.
    Otherwise MERGEs into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE idempotently on event_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        events = json.loads(events_json)
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: f&quot;invalid_json: {e}&quot;}

    inserted = 0
    for ev in events:
        event_id = ev.get(&quot;event_id&quot;)
        account_id = ev.get(&quot;account_id&quot;)
        feature_key = ev.get(&quot;feature_key&quot;)
        units = ev.get(&quot;units&quot;, 0)
        usage_ts = ev.get(&quot;usage_timestamp&quot;)  # ISO
        raw_payload = json.dumps(ev)
        if staged:
            session.sql(
                &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.STAGED_USAGE (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, usage_timestamp, raw_payload, batch_id, processed, created_at) VALUES (%s,%s,%s,%s,TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s),PARSE_JSON(%s), UUID_STRING(), FALSE, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
                (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, usage_ts, raw_payload),
            ).collect()
            inserted += 1
        else:
            session.sql(
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
                USING (SELECT %s AS event_id, %s AS account_id, %s AS feature_key, %s AS units, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s) AS usage_timestamp, PARSE_JSON(%s) AS raw_payload) src
                ON tgt.event_id = src.event_id
                WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET account_id = src.account_id, feature_key = src.feature_key, units = src.units, usage_timestamp = src.usage_timestamp, raw_payload = src.raw_payload, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, usage_timestamp, raw_payload, created_at) VALUES (src.event_id, src.account_id, src.feature_key, src.units, src.usage_timestamp, src.raw_payload, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, usage_ts, raw_payload),
            ).collect()
            inserted += 1

    return {&quot;inserted&quot;: inserted}

365 — embeddingingest_sp.py — Idempotent Snowpark procedure that ingests embedding vectors into DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS with vector normalization and metadata parse.
# embeddingingest_sp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import json

def embedding_ingest_sp(session: Session, embeddings_json: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    embeddings_json: JSON array of {embedding_id, document_id, index_id, vector (list), metadata}
    Upserts into AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS keyed by embedding_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        items = json.loads(embeddings_json)
    except Exception as e:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: f&quot;invalid_json: {e}&quot;}

    processed = 0
    for it in items:
        eid = it.get(&quot;embedding_id&quot;)
        docid = it.get(&quot;document_id&quot;)
        idx = it.get(&quot;index_id&quot;)
        vector = json.dumps(it.get(&quot;vector&quot;, []))
        metadata = json.dumps(it.get(&quot;metadata&quot;, {}))
        session.sql(
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS tgt
            USING (SELECT %s AS embedding_id, %s AS document_id, %s AS index_id, PARSE_JSON(%s) AS vector, PARSE_JSON(%s) AS metadata) src
            ON tgt.embedding_id = src.embedding_id
            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET document_id = src.document_id, index_id = src.index_id, vector = src.vector, metadata = src.metadata, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (embedding_id, document_id, index_id, vector, metadata, created_at) VALUES (src.embedding_id, src.document_id, src.index_id, src.vector, src.metadata, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;,
            (eid, docid, idx, vector, metadata),
        ).collect()
        processed += 1
    return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed}

367 — similarity_service.py — FastAPI FAISS similarity microservice that reads snapshot manifest from Snowflake (via simple REST config) and serves /query endpoint returning top-k ids and scores. Note: the Snowflake-to-service registration is handled separately; this service is the external ANN endpoint referenced by external function registrations.
# similarity_service.py
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
from typing import List, Dict, Any
import faiss
import numpy as np
import json
import os

app = FastAPI()

# Simple in-memory index loader; production uses shard manifests and paging
INDICES: Dict[str, Any] = {}

class QueryRequest(BaseModel):
    index_id: str
    vector: List[float]
    top_k: int = 10

@app.post(&quot;/load_snapshot&quot;)
def load_snapshot(payload: Dict[str, Any]):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    payload: {&quot;index_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;shard_paths&quot;: [...], &quot;dim&quot;: 768}
    For PoC we load a single numpy file per shard; this endpoint would be called by infra pipeline.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    index_id = payload.get(&quot;index_id&quot;)
    shards = payload.get(&quot;shard_paths&quot;, [])
    dim = int(payload.get(&quot;dim&quot;, 0))
    if not index_id or not shards or not dim:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=&quot;missing index_id/shards/dim&quot;)
    # build a flat index for PoC
    vectors = []
    ids = []
    for sp in shards:
        # expectation: shard files are JSON lines or numpy arrays accessible by local FS or mounted S3
        if sp.endswith(&quot;.npy&quot;):
            arr = np.load(sp)
            vectors.append(arr)
        else:
            # try JSON lines
            with open(sp, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
                for line in fh:
                    obj = json.loads(line)
                    vectors.append(np.array(obj[&quot;vector&quot;], dtype=np.float32).reshape(1, -1))
                    ids.append(obj.get(&quot;id&quot;))
    if vectors:
        data = np.vstack(vectors) if isinstance(vectors[0], np.ndarray) and len(vectors) &gt; 1 else vectors[0]
        idx = faiss.IndexFlatL2(dim)
        idx.add(np.asarray(data).astype(&#39;float32&#39;))
        INDICES[index_id] = {&quot;index&quot;: idx, &quot;ids&quot;: ids}
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;loaded&quot;, &quot;index_id&quot;: index_id, &quot;count&quot;: idx.ntotal}
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;no_data&quot;}

@app.post(&quot;/query&quot;)
def query(req: QueryRequest):
    if req.index_id not in INDICES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=&quot;index not loaded&quot;)
    idx_meta = INDICES[req.index_id]
    arr = np.asarray(req.vector, dtype=&#39;float32&#39;).reshape(1, -1)
    D, I = idx_meta[&quot;index&quot;].search(arr, req.top_k)
    results = []
    for score, i in zip(D[0], I[0]):
        id_val = idx_meta[&quot;ids&quot;][i] if i &lt; len(idx_meta[&quot;ids&quot;]) else str(i)
        results.append({&quot;id&quot;: id_val, &quot;score&quot;: float(score)})
    return {&quot;results&quot;: results}

368 — infra/registervia_connector.py — Python helper that attempts registration via connector API, falls back to direct SQL registration when connector fails. Used to register Snowpark procedures/registrations programmatically.
# registervia_connector.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import requests
import json

def register_via_connector(session: Session, registration_payload: Dict[str, Any], connector_url: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Tries to POST registration_payload to connector_url; if fails, falls back to executing provided SQL registration script in payload[&#39;sql&#39;].
    Records outcome in AI_FEATURE_HUB.registration_audit.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    try:
        if connector_url:
            resp = requests.post(connector_url, json=registration_payload, timeout=10)
            if resp.status_code in (200,201):
                session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.registration_audit(audit_id, payload, status, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), PARSE_JSON(%s), &#39;CONNECTOR_OK&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (json.dumps(registration_payload),)).collect()
                return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;CONNECTOR_OK&quot;, &quot;response&quot;: resp.json()}
        # fallback to SQL registration
        sql = registration_payload.get(&quot;sql&quot;)
        if not sql:
            raise RuntimeError(&quot;no_sql_for_fallback&quot;)
        session.sql(sql).collect()
        session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.registration_audit(audit_id, payload, status, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), PARSE_JSON(%s), &#39;SQL_OK&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (json.dumps(registration_payload),)).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;SQL_OK&quot;}
    except Exception as e:
        session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.registration_audit(audit_id, payload, status, error, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), PARSE_JSON(%s), &#39;ERROR&#39;, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (json.dumps(registration_payload), str(e))).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;error&quot;: str(e)}

369 — faiss/indexsnapshotloader.py — Utility that downloads snapshot shards from a stage/S3 to local paths and emits a manifest consumable by similarity_service loader.
# indexsnapshotloader.py
import boto3
import os
import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any

def download_shards_from_s3(s3_bucket: str, shard_keys: List[str], target_dir: str, aws_profile: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Downloads listed shard_keys from s3_bucket into target_dir and returns local shard paths.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session = boto3.Session(profile_name=aws_profile) if aws_profile else boto3.Session()
    s3 = session.client(&quot;s3&quot;)
    os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True)
    local_paths = []
    for key in shard_keys:
        filename = os.path.basename(key)
        local_path = os.path.join(target_dir, filename)
        s3.download_file(s3_bucket, key, local_path)
        local_paths.append(local_path)
    return {&quot;downloaded&quot;: local_paths}

def build_loader_manifest(index_id: str, shard_local_paths: List[str], dim: int) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    manifest = {&quot;index_id&quot;: index_id, &quot;shard_paths&quot;: shard_local_paths, &quot;dim&quot;: dim, &quot;generated_at&quot;: __import__(&quot;time&quot;).strftime(&quot;%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ&quot;, __import__(&quot;time&quot;).gmtime())}
    manifest_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(shard_local_paths[0]) if shard_local_paths else &quot;.&quot;, f&quot;{index_id}_manifest.json&quot;)
    with open(manifest_path, &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
        json.dump(manifest, fh, ensure_ascii=False)
    return {&quot;manifest_path&quot;: manifest_path, &quot;manifest&quot;: manifest}
370 — advanced_pricing_admin.py — Streamlit admin UI to list, create, edit, and promote RATE_RULE_TEMPLATES and RATE_RULES in Snowflake (uses Snowpark connector for PUT/REGISTER and MERGE into staging/promote workflows).
# advanced_pricing_admin.py
import streamlit as st
import json
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def get_session() -&gt; Session:
    # Expect STREAMLIT_SECRETS or env configured for Snowpark connector
    return Session.builder.configs(st.secrets[&quot;snowflake&quot;]).create()

def list_rate_rule_templates(session: Session):
    return session.sql(&quot;SELECT template_id, name, created_at FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULE_TEMPLATES ORDER BY created_at DESC&quot;).collect()

def get_template_payload(session: Session, template_id: str):
    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT template_body FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULE_TEMPLATES WHERE template_id = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, (template_id,)).collect()
    return rows[0][&quot;TEMPLATE_BODY&quot;] if rows else {}

def upsert_template(session: Session, name: str, template_body: dict, template_id: str = None):
    tb = json.dumps(template_body, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    if template_id:
        session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULE_TEMPLATES SET name=%s, template_body=PARSE_JSON(%s), updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE template_id=%s&quot;, (name, tb, template_id)).collect()
        return template_id
    else:
        session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULE_TEMPLATES(template_id, name, template_body, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (name, tb)).collect()
        return &quot;CREATED&quot;

def promote_staging(session: Session, staging_id: str, actor: str = &quot;ui&quot;):
    # Mark staging approved and call seeder/merge process or run MERGE directly
    session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING SET approved = TRUE, approved_by = %s, approved_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE staging_id = %s&quot;, (actor, staging_id)).collect()
    # Optionally call seeder SP
    try:
        session.call(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.SEED_APPROVED_RATE_RULES&quot;, [])
    except Exception:
        # fallback: run promotion SQL (idempotent MERGE handled by seeder)
        pass
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;PROMOTED&quot;, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id}

def main():
    st.title(&quot;Advanced Pricing Admin&quot;)
    session = get_session()

    st.sidebar.header(&quot;Actions&quot;)
    action = st.sidebar.selectbox(&quot;Action&quot;, [&quot;List templates&quot;, &quot;Create template&quot;, &quot;Edit template&quot;, &quot;Promote staging&quot;])

    if action == &quot;List templates&quot;:
        rows = list_rate_rule_templates(session)
        for r in rows:
            st.write(r[&quot;NAME&quot;], r[&quot;TEMPLATE_ID&quot;], r[&quot;CREATED_AT&quot;])
    elif action == &quot;Create template&quot;:
        name = st.text_input(&quot;Template name&quot;)
        body = st.text_area(&quot;Template JSON&quot;, height=300)
        if st.button(&quot;Create&quot;):
            try:
                payload = json.loads(body)
                res = upsert_template(session, name, payload, None)
                st.success(f&quot;Created {res}&quot;)
            except Exception as e:
                st.error(str(e))
    elif action == &quot;Edit template&quot;:
        template_id = st.text_input(&quot;Template ID&quot;)
        if st.button(&quot;Load&quot;):
            payload = get_template_payload(session, template_id)
            st.session_state[&quot;payload&quot;] = json.dumps(payload, indent=2)
        body = st.text_area(&quot;Template JSON&quot;, value=st.session_state.get(&quot;payload&quot;, &quot;{}&quot;), height=300)
        if st.button(&quot;Save&quot;):
            try:
                payload = json.loads(body)
                res = upsert_template(session, name=&quot;edited&quot;, template_body=payload, template_id=template_id)
                st.success(f&quot;Updated {res}&quot;)
            except Exception as e:
                st.error(str(e))
    elif action == &quot;Promote staging&quot;:
        staging_id = st.text_input(&quot;Staging ID&quot;)
        if st.button(&quot;Promote&quot;):
            res = promote_staging(session, staging_id, actor=st.secrets.get(&quot;user&quot;,&quot;ui&quot;))
            st.json(res)

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    main()

373 — rate_rule_migration.py — Script to consolidate multiple versions of an artifact (RATE_RULE_TEMPLATES or RATE_RULES) into a single canonical record (merges fields, preserves history into audit table). Useful for the user&#39;s &quot;merge different versions into one&quot; objective.
# rate_rule_migration.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json

def canonical_merge(rules):
    # simple rule: later updated_at wins for scalar fields; arrays concat unique
    merged = {}
    rules_sorted = sorted(rules, key=lambda r: r.get(&quot;UPDATED_AT&quot;) or r.get(&quot;CREATED_AT&quot;))
    for r in rules_sorted:
        body = r.get(&quot;RULE_JSON&quot;) or r.get(&quot;TEMPLATE_BODY&quot;) or {}
        for k, v in (body.items() if isinstance(body, dict) else []):
            merged[k] = v
    return merged

def consolidate_rules(session: Session, rule_key: str):
    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT rule_id, version, rule_json, created_at, updated_at FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES WHERE rule_key = %s ORDER BY created_at ASC&quot;, (rule_key,)).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;NOT_FOUND&quot;}
    rules = [r.asDict() for r in rows]
    merged = canonical_merge([{&quot;RULE_JSON&quot;: json.loads(r[&quot;RULE_JSON&quot;]) if isinstance(r[&quot;RULE_JSON&quot;], str) else r[&quot;RULE_JSON&quot;], &quot;UPDATED_AT&quot;: r.get(&quot;UPDATED_AT&quot;), &quot;CREATED_AT&quot;: r.get(&quot;CREATED_AT&quot;)} for r in rules])
    # write consolidated as new version
    session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_CONSOLIDATED(consolidated_id, rule_key, consolidated_json, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (rule_key, json.dumps(merged))).collect()
    # audit
    session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_MERGE_AUDIT(audit_id, rule_key, source_versions, created_at) VALUES (UUID_STRING(), %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (rule_key, json.dumps([r[&quot;VERSION&quot;] for r in rules]))).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;, &quot;merged_fields&quot;: list(merged.keys())}

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    s = Session.builder.configs({}).create()
    print(consolidate_rules(s, &quot;default.feature.rate.v1&quot;))

374 — rate_rule_validator.py — Validates rule JSONs against a minimal JSON schema and returns errors; used by admin UI and CI checks.
# rate_rule_validator.py
import jsonschema
import json

RATE_RULE_SCHEMA = {
    &quot;type&quot;: &quot;object&quot;,
    &quot;properties&quot;: {
        &quot;rule_key&quot;: {&quot;type&quot;: &quot;string&quot;},
        &quot;pricing&quot;: {
            &quot;type&quot;: &quot;object&quot;,
            &quot;properties&quot;: {
                &quot;unit_price&quot;: {&quot;type&quot;: &quot;number&quot;},
                &quot;tiers&quot;: {&quot;type&quot;: &quot;array&quot;}
            },
            &quot;required&quot;: [&quot;unit_price&quot;]
        }
    },
    &quot;required&quot;: [&quot;rule_key&quot;, &quot;pricing&quot;]
}

def validate_rule(rule_json_str: str):
    payload = json.loads(rule_json_str)
    try:
        jsonschema.validate(instance=payload, schema=RATE_RULE_SCHEMA)
        return {&quot;valid&quot;: True}
    except jsonschema.ValidationError as e:
        return {&quot;valid&quot;: False, &quot;error&quot;: e.message}

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    import sys
    with open(sys.argv[1], &quot;r&quot;) as fh:
        print(validate_rule(fh.read()))

376 — tests/test_deterministic_billing.py — Deterministic billing unit test for invoice_hash stability (matches deterministic hash algorithm).
# tests/test_deterministic_billing.py
import json
from invoice_hash_utils import canonical_invoice_hash

def test_invoice_hash_stability():
    payload = {&quot;header&quot;: {&quot;run_id&quot;:&quot;r1&quot;}, &quot;lines&quot;:[{&quot;feature_key&quot;:&quot;f1&quot;,&quot;units&quot;:10,&quot;unit_price&quot;:1.5}], &quot;subtotal&quot;:15.0}
    h1 = canonical_invoice_hash(payload)
    h2 = canonical_invoice_hash(payload)
    assert h1 == h2
    # slight reordering should not change hash when keys sorted in canonicalization
    payload2 = {&quot;lines&quot;:payload[&quot;lines&quot;], &quot;subtotal&quot;:15.0, &quot;header&quot;:payload[&quot;header&quot;]}
    assert canonical_invoice_hash(payload2) == h1

379 — db_schema_exporter.py — Exports DDL for schema/tables to files (helps to consolidate DDL variants across documents into canonical DDL files). Use to generate canonical SQL files before merging.
# db_schema_exporter.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import os

def get_session(): return Session.builder.configs({}).create()

def export_table_ddl(session: Session, database: str, schema: str, table: str, out_dir: str = &quot;./ddls&quot;):
    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)
    res = session.sql(&quot;SELECT GET_DDL(&#39;TABLE&#39;, %s || &#39;.&#39; || %s || &#39;.&#39; || %s) AS ddl&quot;, (database, schema, table)).collect()
    ddl = res[0][&quot;DDL&quot;] if res else &quot;&quot;
    path = os.path.join(out_dir, f&quot;{schema}__{table}.sql&quot;)
    with open(path, &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
        fh.write(ddl)
    return path

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:

    print(export_table_ddl(s, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;, &quot;PUBLIC&quot;, &quot;RATE_RULES&quot;))

380 — record_autoscale_action.py — Snowpark procedure that records recommended autoscale actions into an audit table; idempotent insert using generated UUID.
# record_autoscale_action.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def record_autoscale_action(session: Session, container_name: str, recommended_action: str, notes: Dict[str, Any] = None) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Inserts a recommended autoscale action into AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_autoscale_actions.
    Returns the action_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    action_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    notes_json = &quot;NULL&quot;
    if notes is not None:
        import json
        notes_json = json.dumps(notes, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_autoscale_actions
            (action_id, container_name, recommended_action, notes, created_at)
        VALUES (%s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (action_id, container_name, recommended_action, notes_json),
    ).collect()
    return action_id

381 — record_autoscale_approval.py — Marks an autoscale action approved (sets approved flag, approver, timestamp); idempotent update.
# record_autoscale_approval.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any

def record_autoscale_approval(session: Session, action_id: str, approver: str, approval_notes: Dict[str, Any] = None) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Marks an autoscale action approved. Returns action_id on success.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    notes_json = &quot;NULL&quot;
    if approval_notes is not None:
        import json
        notes_json = json.dumps(approval_notes, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)

    session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_autoscale_actions
        SET approved = TRUE,
            approved_by = %s,
            approved_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(),
            approval_notes = PARSE_JSON(%s)
        WHERE action_id = %s
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (approver, notes_json, action_id),
    ).collect()

    return action_id

383 — compute_similarity_costs.py — Charges per-query cost by reading recent similarity_query_logs and inserting usage events (idempotent by query_id).
# compute_similarity_costs.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Dict, Any
import uuid

def compute_similarity_costs(session: Session, price_per_query: float = 0.01, lookback_minutes: int = 60) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Reads similarity_query_logs from the last `lookback_minutes` and writes usage events into AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_events_detailed.
    Idempotency ensured by using query_id as unique key in a MERGE.
    Returns summary counts.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT query_id, org_id, index_id, returned_count
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.similarity_query_logs
        WHERE created_at &gt;= DATEADD(minute, -%s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (lookback_minutes,),
    ).collect()

    inserted = 0
    for r in rows:
        qid = r[&quot;QUERY_ID&quot;]
        org = r[&quot;ORG_ID&quot;]
        # Insert idempotently
        session.sql(
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.usage_events_detailed tgt
            USING (SELECT %s AS event_key) src
            ON tgt.event_key = src.event_key
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (event_id, event_key, org_id, feature_code, model_id, tokens, latency_ms, cost, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, src.event_key, %s, &#39;SIMILARITY_QUERY&#39;, &#39;ANN&#39;, 0, 0, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;,
            (qid, str(uuid.uuid4()), org, float(price_per_query)),
        ).collect()
        inserted += 1

    return {&quot;processed&quot;: len(rows), &quot;inserted&quot;: inserted}

384 — enforce_ann_rate_limit.py — Checks per-org QPS against configured ANN rate limits and records backpressure events when exceeded.
# enforce_ann_rate_limit.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def enforce_ann_rate_limit(session: Session, org_id: str) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Evaluates QPS over last minute against ANN rate limits table. If exceeded, inserts a backpressure event and returns &quot;THROTTLED&quot;.
    Otherwise returns &quot;OK&quot;.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    limit_row = session.sql(
        &quot;SELECT max_qps FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_rate_limits WHERE org_id = %s LIMIT 1&quot;,
        (org_id,),
    ).collect()

    if not limit_row:
        return &quot;NO_LIMIT&quot;

    max_qps = float(limit_row[0][&quot;MAX_QPS&quot;] or 0.0)
    qps_row = session.sql(
        &quot;SELECT (COUNT(*)/60.0) AS qps FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.similarity_query_logs WHERE org_id = %s AND created_at &gt;= DATEADD(minute,-1,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
        (org_id,),
    ).collect()
    qps = float(qps_row[0][&quot;QPS&quot;] or 0.0)

    if qps &gt; max_qps:
        # record backpressure event
        event_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        detail = {&quot;qps&quot;: qps, &quot;limit&quot;: max_qps}
        import json
        session.sql(
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ingestion_backpressure_events(event_id, org_id, event_type, details, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, &#39;ann_rate_limit&#39;, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;,
            (event_id, org_id, json.dumps(detail)),
        ).collect()
        return &quot;THROTTLED&quot;

    return &quot;OK&quot;

388 — update_retention_policy_and_audit.py — Updates retention policies for an org and writes an audit entry capturing previous vs new values.
# update_retention_policy_and_audit.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json
from typing import Dict, Any

def update_retention_policy_and_audit(session: Session, org_id: str, embedding_days: int, provenance_days: int, changed_by: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Upserts retention_policies for org_id and records an audit row with previous values.
    Returns audit_id and status.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    prev = session.sql(
        &quot;SELECT embedding_days, provenance_days FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.retention_policies WHERE org_id = %s LIMIT 1&quot;,
        (org_id,),
    ).collect()

    prev_obj = prev[0].asDict() if prev else None

    # Upsert retention policy
    session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.retention_policies tgt
        USING (SELECT %s AS org_id) src
        ON tgt.org_id = src.org_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET embedding_days = %s, provenance_days = %s, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (org_id, embedding_days, provenance_days, created_at) VALUES (src.org_id, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (org_id, embedding_days, provenance_days, embedding_days, provenance_days),
    ).collect()

    audit_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.retention_policy_audit (audit_id, org_id, previous_value, new_value, changed_by, created_at)
        VALUES (%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (
            audit_id,
            org_id,
            json.dumps(prev_obj or {}),
            json.dumps({&quot;embedding_days&quot;: embedding_days, &quot;provenance_days&quot;: provenance_days}),
            changed_by,
        ),
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;audit_id&quot;: audit_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;}

396 — compute_quality_metrics.py — computes dataset / ingestion quality metrics and writes to metrics table.
# compute_quality_metrics.py
# Computes simple quality metrics (completeness, uniqueness, avg_embed_norm) and writes to OPERATIONAL_METRICS.

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def compute_quality_metrics(session: Session, org_id: str, dataset_id: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Example quality checks:
      - completeness: fraction of sections with embeddings
      - duplicate_rate: fraction of sections with identical content hash
      - avg_embedding_norm: mean L2 norm of embeddings
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    completeness_row = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS total_sections,
               SUM(CASE WHEN embedding IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS with_embedding
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_SECTIONS
        WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND dataset_id = &#39;{dataset_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()[0]

    total = int(completeness_row[&#39;TOTAL_SECTIONS&#39;] or 0)
    with_emb = int(completeness_row[&#39;WITH_EMBEDDING&#39;] or 0)
    completeness = (with_emb / total) if total else 0.0

    # avg norm
    avg_norm_row = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT AVG(ARRAY_SUM(ARRAY_TRANSFORM(embedding, x -&gt; x*x))) as avg_norm_sq
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SECTION_EMBEDDINGS se
        JOIN AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_SECTIONS ds ON se.section_id = ds.section_id
        WHERE ds.org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND ds.dataset_id = &#39;{dataset_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    avg_norm_sq = float(avg_norm_row[0][&#39;AVG_NORM_SQ&#39;] or 0.0) if avg_norm_row else 0.0
    avg_norm = avg_norm_sq ** 0.5

    metrics = {&#39;completeness&#39;: completeness, &#39;avg_embedding_norm&#39;: avg_norm, &#39;total_sections&#39;: total, &#39;with_embedding&#39;: with_emb}
    metric_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.OPERATIONAL_METRICS (METRIC_ID, ORG_ID, DATASET_ID, METRICS_JSON, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{metric_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, &#39;{dataset_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(metrics)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    return {&#39;metric_id&#39;: metric_id, &#39;metrics&#39;: metrics}

398 — get_or_set_snippet_cache.py — snippet caching helper used by document generation/query flows.
# get_or_set_snippet_cache.py
# Retrieves cached snippet by key or sets it if missing. Uses CACHE table with TTL.

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def get_or_set_snippet_cache(session: Session, cache_key: str, compute_fn=None, ttl_seconds: int = 3600):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    If compute_fn is provided and cache miss, compute_fn() is executed to produce value (JSON-serializable).
    Returns the cached value (dict).
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT value, created_at FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SNIPPET_CACHE WHERE cache_key = &#39;{cache_key}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
    if rows:
        return rows[0][&#39;VALUE&#39;]

    if compute_fn is None:
        return None

    value = compute_fn()
    value_json = json.dumps(value, separators=(&#39;,&#39;, &#39;:&#39;), ensure_ascii=False)
    cache_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SNIPPET_CACHE (CACHE_ID, CACHE_KEY, VALUE, CREATED_AT, TTL_SECONDS)
        VALUES (&#39;{cache_id}&#39;, &#39;{cache_key}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{value_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), {ttl_seconds})
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return value
400 — entitlement_check.py
# entitlement_check.py
# Snowpark Python stored-procedure style handler to evaluate whether an account/org is entitled to a feature.
# Returns dict {&quot;allowed&quot;: bool, &quot;reason&quot;: str}
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

def entitlement_check(session: Session, account_id: str, feature_key: str, org_id: Optional[str] = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Checks entitlement tables and limits to determine if `account_id` may use `feature_key`.
    - Checks RATE_RULES (if feature gated), ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENTS, and optionally per-org suspensions.
    Returns {&#39;allowed&#39;: bool, &#39;reason&#39;: str}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Check explicit account entitlement
    q_ent = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT enabled, notes
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENTS
        WHERE account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39; AND feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39;
        LIMIT 1
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ent_rows = session.sql(q_ent).collect()
    if ent_rows:
        row = ent_rows[0]
        if not row[&quot;ENABLED&quot;]:
            return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;Account-level entitlement disabled&quot;}
        # allowed by account entitlement
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: True, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;Account entitlement present&quot;}

    # Check org-level suspensions
    if org_id:
        q_sus = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ORG_SUSPENSIONS
            WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39; AND NOW() BETWEEN suspended_from AND suspended_to
            LIMIT 1
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        sus = session.sql(q_sus).collect()
        if sus:
            return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;Feature suspended for organization&quot;}

    # Check RATE_RULES (if a rule denies access or requires quota)
    q_rule = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT rule_type, config
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES
        WHERE feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39; AND enabled = TRUE
        ORDER BY priority ASC
        LIMIT 1
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rule_rows = session.sql(q_rule).collect()
    if rule_rows:
        rt = rule_rows[0][&quot;RULE_TYPE&quot;]
        cfg = rule_rows[0][&quot;CONFIG&quot;]
        # Example simple policy: RULE_TYPE == &#39;ALLOW&#39; or &#39;DENY&#39;
        if rt.upper() == &quot;DENY&quot;:
            return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;Rate rule denies access&quot;}
        if rt.upper() == &quot;ALLOW&quot;:
            return {&quot;allowed&quot;: True, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;Rate rule allows access&quot;}

    # Fallback: allow
    return {&quot;allowed&quot;: True, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;No entitlement rule forbids access&quot;}

401 — usage_ingest_sp.py
# usage_ingest_sp.py
# Idempotent Snowpark handler to ingest usage events (from stage or function input).
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any, List

def usage_ingest_sp(session: Session, usage_events_json: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Expects usage_events_json as a JSON array of events:
      [{ &quot;event_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;account_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;feature_key&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;units&quot;: 1.0, &quot;unit_price&quot;: 0.01, &quot;usage_timestamp&quot;: &quot;...&quot; }, ...]
    Inserts/upserts events into AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE using MERGE on event_id for idempotency.
    Returns summary {ingested: n, upserted: n}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    events = json.loads(usage_events_json)
    ingested = 0
    for ev in events:
        eid = ev.get(&quot;event_id&quot;) or f&quot;evt-{uuid.uuid4().hex}&quot;
        account_id = ev.get(&quot;account_id&quot;)
        feature_key = ev.get(&quot;feature_key&quot;)
        units = float(ev.get(&quot;units&quot;) or 0)
        unit_price = float(ev.get(&quot;unit_price&quot;) or 0)
        ts = ev.get(&quot;usage_timestamp&quot;)
        # MERGE for idempotent insert/update
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
            USING (SELECT &#39;{eid}&#39; AS event_id) src
            ON tgt.event_id = src.event_id
            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39;, feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39;, units = {units}, unit_price = {unit_price}, usage_timestamp = TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{ts}&#39;)
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp, created_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{eid}&#39;, &#39;{account_id}&#39;, &#39;{feature_key}&#39;, {units}, {unit_price}, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{ts}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        ingested += 1
    return {&quot;ingested&quot;: ingested}

402 — embedding_ingest_sp.py
# embedding_ingest_sp.py
# Ingest embeddings for documents/sections in an idempotent way with provenance captured.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List

def embedding_ingest_sp(session: Session, embeddings_json: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    embeddings_json is a JSON array:
      [{ &quot;embedding_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;doc_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;section_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;org_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;embedding&quot;: [...], &quot;provenance&quot;: {...} }, ...]
    Uses MERGE to ensure idempotency keyed by embedding_id or section_id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = json.loads(embeddings_json)
    inserted = 0
    for r in rows:
        emb_id = r.get(&quot;embedding_id&quot;) or uuid.uuid4().hex
        doc_id = r.get(&quot;doc_id&quot;)
        section_id = r.get(&quot;section_id&quot;)
        org_id = r.get(&quot;org_id&quot;)
        emb_json = json.dumps(r.get(&quot;embedding&quot;) or [], separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
        prov_json = json.dumps(r.get(&quot;provenance&quot;) or {}, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
        # Upsert by embedding_id (if provided) else by section_id
        key_expr = f&quot;&#39;{emb_id}&#39;&quot;
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS tgt
            USING (SELECT {key_expr} AS embedding_id) src
            ON tgt.embedding_id = src.embedding_id
            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET doc_id = &#39;{doc_id}&#39;, section_id = &#39;{section_id}&#39;, org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;, embedding = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{emb_json}&#39;), provenance = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{prov_json}&#39;), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (embedding_id, doc_id, section_id, org_id, embedding, provenance, created_at)
            VALUES ({key_expr}, &#39;{doc_id}&#39;, &#39;{section_id}&#39;, &#39;{org_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{emb_json}&#39;), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{prov_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        inserted += 1
    return {&quot;processed&quot;: len(rows), &quot;inserted&quot;: inserted}

403 — write_evidence_bundle.py
# write_evidence_bundle.py
# Consolidated evidence bundle exporter: collects bundle parts and persists to archive table and optional stage file.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def write_evidence_bundle(session: Session, bundle_id: str, export_to_stage: bool = False, stage_path: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Gathers evidence bundle components and writes an archive record. If export_to_stage=True and stage_path provided,
    writes the JSON to the specified user stage path.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    meta_rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_METADATA WHERE BUNDLE_ID = &#39;{bundle_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
    if not meta_rows:
        raise ValueError(&quot;Bundle not found&quot;)
    docs = [dict(r.asDict()) for r in session.sql(f&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT WHERE BUNDLE_ID = &#39;{bundle_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()]
    attachments = [dict(r.asDict()) for r in session.sql(f&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_ATTACHMENTS WHERE BUNDLE_ID = &#39;{bundle_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()]
    bundle = {
        &quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id,
        &quot;metadata&quot;: dict(meta_rows[0].asDict()),
        &quot;documents&quot;: docs,
        &quot;attachments&quot;: attachments
    }
    bundle_json = json.dumps(bundle, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    archive_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_ARCHIVE (ARCHIVE_ID, BUNDLE_ID, BUNDLE_JSON, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{archive_id}&#39;, &#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{bundle_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    if export_to_stage and stage_path:
        # session.file.put_stream used in repo artifacts; attempt same pattern
        try:
            session.file.put_stream(bundle_json.encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;), stage_path, auto_compress=False)
        except Exception:
            # Best-effort: if environment doesn&#39;t support put_stream, ignore or write alternative
            pass
    return {&quot;archive_id&quot;: archive_id, &quot;bundle_size&quot;: len(bundle_json)}

404 — submit_and_approve_procs.py
# submit_and_approve_procs.py
# Consolidated Snowpark helpers for RATE_RULES staging workflow: submit, approve, reject.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def submit_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_payload_json: str, submitted_by: str = &quot;streamlit&quot;) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    payload = json.loads(staging_payload_json)
    staging_id = f&quot;stg-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    cfg = json.dumps(payload.get(&quot;config&quot;, {}), separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    priority = int(payload.get(&quot;priority&quot;, 100))
    feature_key = payload.get(&quot;feature_key&quot;, &quot;UNKNOWN&quot;)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING (STAGING_ID, FEATURE_KEY, RULE_TYPE, CONFIG, PRIORITY, SUBMITTED_BY, SUBMITTED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{staging_id}&#39;, &#39;{feature_key}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&quot;rule_type&quot;, &quot;CUSTOM&quot;)}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{cfg}&#39;), {priority}, &#39;{submitted_by}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    audit_id = f&quot;audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, ACTION_TS, DETAILS)
        VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{staging_id}&#39;, &#39;SUBMIT&#39;, &#39;{submitted_by}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(payload, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;))}&#39;))
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;submitted&quot;, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id}

def approve_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_id: str, approver: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    # Fetch staging row
    s = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING WHERE STAGING_ID = &#39;{staging_id}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
    if not s:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;not_found&quot;, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id}
    srow = s[0].asDict()
    rule_id = srow.get(&quot;RULE_ID&quot;) or f&quot;rule-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    feature_key = srow.get(&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;)
    cfg = json.dumps(srow.get(&quot;CONFIG&quot;) or {}, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    priority = int(srow.get(&quot;PRIORITY&quot;) or 100)
    # MERGE into RATE_RULES by RULE_ID (or create new)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{rule_id}&#39; AS rule_id) src
        ON tgt.rule_id = src.rule_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39;, rule_type = &#39;{srow.get(&#39;RULE_TYPE&#39;)}&#39;, config = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{cfg}&#39;), priority = {priority}, enabled = TRUE, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (rule_id, feature_key, rule_type, config, priority, enabled, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{rule_id}&#39;, &#39;{feature_key}&#39;, &#39;{srow.get(&#39;RULE_TYPE&#39;)}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{cfg}&#39;), {priority}, TRUE, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # Mark staging as approved
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING
        SET APPROVED = TRUE, APPROVED_BY = &#39;{approver}&#39;, APPROVED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHERE STAGING_ID = &#39;{staging_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # Audit
    audit_id = f&quot;audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, ACTION_TS)
        VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{staging_id}&#39;, &#39;APPROVE&#39;, &#39;{approver}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;approved&quot;, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id, &quot;rule_id&quot;: rule_id}

def reject_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_id: str, approver: str, reason: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING
        SET REJECTED = TRUE, REJECT_REASON = &#39;{reason}&#39;, APPROVED_BY = &#39;{approver}&#39;, APPROVED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHERE STAGING_ID = &#39;{staging_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    audit_id = f&quot;audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, ACTION_TS, DETAILS)
        VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{staging_id}&#39;, &#39;REJECT&#39;, &#39;{approver}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps({&quot;reason&quot;: reason})}&#39;))
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;rejected&quot;, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id}

405 — infra/register_via_connector.py
# register_via_connector.py
# Helper to register Snowpark procedures using the Python connector (fallback registrar).
import os
import glob
import snowflake.connector
from typing import List

def register_sql_files(conn_params: dict, sql_dir: str) -&gt; List[str]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Executes all .sql files in sql_dir against Snowflake using snowflake.connector.
    Returns list of filenames executed.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    executed = []
    ctx = snowflake.connector.connect(**conn_params)
    cur = ctx.cursor()
    try:
        for path in sorted(glob.glob(f&quot;{sql_dir}/*.sql&quot;)):
            with open(path, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
                sql = fh.read()
            # naive split; real registrars use snowsql or single-statement files
            for stmt in [s for s in sql.split(&quot;;&quot;) if s.strip()]:
                cur.execute(stmt)
            executed.append(path)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        ctx.close()
    return executed

# snowflake_provisioning.py
# Provision stages, integrations, roles deterministically via Snowpark connector.
import snowflake.connector
from typing import Dict

def provision_core(conn_params: Dict, stage_name: str = &quot;DOCGEN_STAGE&quot;, integration_name: str = &quot;AI_INTEGRATION&quot;):
    ctx = snowflake.connector.connect(**conn_params)
    cur = ctx.cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE STAGE {stage_name} FILE_FORMAT = (TYPE = &#39;JSON&#39;);&quot;)
        cur.execute(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE API_INTEGRATION {integration_name} TYPE = EXTERNAL_OAUTH ENABLED = TRUE;&quot;)
        # example role creation
        cur.execute(&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE ROLE AI_FEATURE_OPERATOR;&quot;)
        cur.execute(&quot;GRANT USAGE ON WAREHOUSE SYS_WH TO ROLE AI_FEATURE_OPERATOR;&quot;)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        ctx.close()
    return {&quot;stage&quot;: stage_name, &quot;integration&quot;: integration_name}

# test_harness_snowflake.py
# Simple harness used by CI to execute ingest, billing, persist, reconcile flows via connector.
import os
import time
import snowflake.connector

def conn():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(
        user=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;),
        password=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;),
        account=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;),
        warehouse=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_WAREHOUSE&quot;),
        database=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DB&quot;, &quot;AI_PLATFORM&quot;),
        schema=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    )

def run_all():
    c = conn()
    cur = c.cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(&quot;CALL AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_USAGE_TASK(100);&quot;)
        time.sleep(2)
        cur.execute(&quot;CALL AI_FEATURE_HUB.RUN_BILLING_ENHANCED(DATEADD(&#39;day&#39;, -1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())::STRING, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()::STRING, NULL, TRUE);&quot;)
        cur.execute(&quot;CALL AI_FEATURE_HUB.PERSIST_BILLING_RUNS(60, TRUE);&quot;)

        print(&#39;RECONCILE&#39;, res)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        c.close()

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    run_all()

408 — tests/test_deterministic_billing.py
# test_deterministic_billing.py
# Pytest-style check that running billing preview twice with same inputs yields same invoice_hash.
import os
import snowflake.connector

def conn():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(
        user=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;),
        password=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;),
        account=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;),
        warehouse=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_WAREHOUSE&quot;),
        database=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DB&quot;, &quot;AI_PLATFORM&quot;),
        schema=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    )

def get_preview_hash(cur, start, end):
    cur.execute(f&quot;SELECT (CALL AI_FEATURE_HUB.RUN_BILLING_ENHANCED(&#39;{start}&#39;,&#39;{end}&#39;, NULL, TRUE))&quot;)
    # Stored proc returns a VARIANT/OBJECT in some implementations; here we fetch via SELECT wrapper if required.
    row = cur.fetchone()
    if not row:
        return None
    # If return is stringified JSON, parse; else adapt as needed.
    import json
    val = row[0]
    if isinstance(val, str):
        obj = json.loads(val)
        return obj.get(&quot;invoice_hash&quot;)
    # else if VARIANT, Snowflake connector returns dict-like
    try:
        return val.get(&quot;invoice_hash&quot;)
    except Exception:
        return None

def test_invoice_hash_determinism():
    import datetime
    start = (datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()
    end = datetime.datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    c = conn()
    cur = c.cursor()
    try:
        h1 = get_preview_hash(cur, start, end)
        h2 = get_preview_hash(cur, start, end)
        assert h1 == h2
    finally:
        cur.close()
        c.close()

409 — faiss/index_snapshot_loader.py
# index_snapshot_loader.py
# Loads a FAISS index snapshot from a Snowflake stage to local filesystem and opens it with faiss (if available).
import os
import tempfile
from typing import Optional

def load_index_from_stage(session, stage_path: str, local_filename: Optional[str] = None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    stage_path example: @~/faiss_snapshots/index_2025_10_17.faiss
    Downloads the file from user stage to local filesystem and returns local path.
    Note: session.file.get or session.file.get_stream usage depends on environment; fallback uses GET via SQL if needed.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    if local_filename is None:
        fd, local_filename = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=&quot;faiss_&quot;, suffix=&quot;.idx&quot;)
        os.close(fd)
    try:
        # Attempt to use session.file.get if available
        try:
            session.file.get(stage_path, local_filename)
            return local_filename
        except Exception:
            # Fallback: use GET via SQL (requires stage access and network) - write to local via client tool in real deploy
            raise
    except Exception as e:
        raise RuntimeError(f&quot;Failed to fetch index snapshot: {e}&quot;)

def open_faiss_index(local_path: str):
    try:
        import faiss
    except ImportError:
        raise RuntimeError(&quot;faiss not installed in runtime&quot;)
    idx = faiss.read_index(local_path)
    return idx
