410 — rotate_token.py — token rotation and merge into connector tokens table
# rotate_token.py
# Fetches a new access token from an external refresh endpoint and MERGEs it into AI_FEATURE_HUB.CONNECTOR_TOKENS
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import requests
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def rotate_token(session: Session, provider_name: str, refresh_endpoint: str, client_id: str, client_secret: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Calls the refresh endpoint, extracts the access token and expiry (if present),
    and upserts into AI_FEATURE_HUB.CONNECTOR_TOKENS using MERGE for idempotency.
    Returns dict with provider and masked/token metadata.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    resp = requests.post(refresh_endpoint, data={&quot;client_id&quot;: client_id, &quot;client_secret&quot;: client_secret})
    resp.raise_for_status()
    payload = resp.json()
    access_token = payload.get(&quot;access_token&quot;)
    expires_in = payload.get(&quot;expires_in&quot;)  # seconds, optional

    if not access_token:
        raise RuntimeError(&quot;No access_token returned from refresh endpoint&quot;)

    # Upsert token into Snowflake table
    token_json = json.dumps({&quot;expires_in&quot;: expires_in} if expires_in is not None else {}, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.CONNECTOR_TOKENS tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{provider_name}&#39; AS provider) src
        ON tgt.provider = src.provider
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET token = &#39;{access_token}&#39;, meta = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{token_json}&#39;), updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (provider, token, meta, created_at) VALUES (&#39;{provider_name}&#39;, &#39;{access_token}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{token_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;provider&quot;: provider_name, &quot;masked_token&quot;: access_token[:6] + &quot;...&quot;, &quot;expires_in&quot;: expires_in}

411 — verify_all_procs.py — CI procedure presence verifier
# verify_all_procs.py
# Simple CI check that required stored procedures exist in the target schema.
import os
import sys
import snowflake.connector

REQUIRED_PROCS = [
    &quot;RUN_BILLING_RUN_ENHANCED&quot;,
    &quot;INGEST_USAGE_TASK&quot;,
    &quot;CREATE_EVIDENCE_BUNDLE&quot;,
    &quot;EXPORT_INVOICE_ZIP&quot;
]

def run_check():
    conn = snowflake.connector.connect(
        user=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;),
        password=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;),
        account=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;),
        warehouse=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_WAREHOUSE&quot;),
        database=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DB&quot;, &quot;AI_PLATFORM&quot;),
        schema=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    )
    cur = conn.cursor()
    missing = []
    try:
        for p in REQUIRED_PROCS:
            cur.execute(&quot;SELECT PROCEDURE_NAME FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.PROCEDURES WHERE PROCEDURE_NAME ILIKE %s&quot;, (p,))
            if cur.fetchone() is None:
                missing.append(p)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()
    if missing:
        print(&quot;MISSING:&quot;, missing)
        sys.exit(2)
    print(&quot;ALL_PROCS_PRESENT&quot;)
    return True

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    run_check()

# run_ingest_and_reconcile.py
# Triggers ingest task, billing preview, optional persistence, then reconciliation check.
import time
import os
import snowflake.connector

def conn():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(
        user=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;),
        password=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;),
        account=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;),
        warehouse=os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_WAREHOUSE&quot;),

413 — export_invoice_zip.py — export invoice (stage) preparation
# export_invoice_zip.py
# Prepare invoice payload and place on stage for external zipping/signing process.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json

def export_invoice_zip(session: Session, invoice_id: str, stage_prefix: str = &quot;@AI_FEATURE_HUB.INVOICE_STAGE&quot;):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregates invoice header + line items into a JSON payload and writes it to a stage path.
    Returns stage_path and simple status.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT i.invoice_id, i.account_id, i.subtotal, i.markup, i.tax, i.total,
               ARRAY_AGG(OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(&#39;feature_key&#39;, l.feature_key, &#39;line_total&#39;, l.line_total, &#39;usage_qty&#39;, l.usage_qty)) AS lines
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES i
        JOIN AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM l ON i.invoice_id = l.billing_run_id
        WHERE i.invoice_id = &#39;{invoice_id}&#39;
        GROUP BY i.invoice_id, i.account_id, i.subtotal, i.markup, i.tax, i.total
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    if not rows:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: &quot;invoice_not_found&quot;, &quot;invoice_id&quot;: invoice_id}

    payload = rows[0].asDict()
    payload_json = json.dumps(payload, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    stage_path = f&quot;{stage_prefix}/{invoice_id}.json&quot;

    # Write to stage; usage of file.put_stream depends on environment/runner
    try:
        session.file.put_stream(payload_json.encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;), stage_path, auto_compress=False)
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;staged&quot;, &quot;stage_path&quot;: stage_path}
    except Exception:
        # fallback: persist to a staging table
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INVOICE_STAGE_TABLE (INVOICE_ID, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT)
            VALUES (&#39;{invoice_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{payload_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;persisted_table&quot;, &quot;invoice_id&quot;: invoice_id}

416 — compute_similarity_costs.py — compute approximate cost per query (rollup)
# compute_similarity_costs.py
# Sample logic to compute cost estimates for similarity queries and store rollups.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def compute_similarity_costs(session: Session, window_hours: int = 24):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregates SIMILARITY_QUERY_LOG over window_hours and writes a cost estimate per index.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SIMILARITY_COST_ROLLUP (ROLLUP_ID, INDEX_ID, QUERY_COUNT, AVG_LATENCY_MS, TOTAL_EST_COST, CREATED_AT)
        SELECT
          CONCAT(&#39;roll-&#39;, REPLACE(TO_CHAR(CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), &#39;YYYYMMDDHH24MI&#39;), &#39; &#39;, &#39;&#39;)) AS rollup_id,
          INDEX_ID,
          COUNT(*) AS query_count,
          AVG(LATENCY_MS) AS avg_latency_ms,
          -- naive cost model: $0.00001 per vector compare * estimated compares (top_k * vector_dim_factor)
          SUM( (TOP_K * 0.00001) ) AS total_est_cost,
          CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SIMILARITY_QUERY_LOG
        WHERE CREATED_AT &gt;= DATEADD(hour, -{window_hours}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        GROUP BY INDEX_ID;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;rolled_up&quot;, &quot;window_hours&quot;: window_hours}

420 — record_autoscale_approval.pyDescription: Approves or rejects a previously-recorded autoscale action; writes approval audit and updates AUTOSCALE_ACTIONS entry.
# record_autoscale_approval.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def record_autoscale_approval(session: Session, action_id: str, approver: str, approved: bool, notes: Dict[str, Any] = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Approve or reject an autoscale action and record audit.

    Returns {&#39;action_id&#39;:..., &#39;status&#39;: &#39;approved&#39;|&#39;rejected&#39;|&#39;not_found&#39;}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Normalize notes
    notes_json = json.dumps(notes or {}, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)

    # Ensure action exists
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT ACTION_ID FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.AUTOSCALE_ACTIONS WHERE ACTION_ID = &#39;{action_id}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;action_id&quot;: action_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;not_found&quot;}

    status = &quot;approved&quot; if approved else &quot;rejected&quot;
    audit_id = &quot;aaudit-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]

    # Update AUTOSCALE_ACTIONS to set approval metadata (idempotent)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.AUTOSCALE_ACTIONS tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{action_id}&#39; AS action_id) src
        ON tgt.action_id = src.action_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET approved = { &#39;TRUE&#39; if approved else &#39;FALSE&#39; }, approved_by = &#39;{approver}&#39;, approved_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), approval_notes = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{notes_json}&#39;)
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    # Insert audit record (idempotent by AUDIT_ID)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.AUTOSCALE_APPROVAL_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, ACTION_ID, APPROVER, APPROVED, NOTES, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{action_id}&#39;, &#39;{approver}&#39;, { &#39;TRUE&#39; if approved else &#39;FALSE&#39; }, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{notes_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;action_id&quot;: action_id, &quot;status&quot;: status}

421 — process_index_retirement.pyDescription: Worker that processes queued index retirement requests, performs bookkeeping (marks index retired, snapshots metadata) and writes audit records.
# process_index_retirement.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
from typing import Optional

def process_index_retirement(session: Session, request_id: Optional[str] = None, batch_size: int = 100) -&gt; Dict[str, int]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Process index retirement requests from INDEX_RETIREMENT_QUEUE.
    If request_id is provided, process only that request. Returns counts processed.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    where_clause = f&quot;WHERE REQUEST_ID = &#39;{request_id}&#39;&quot; if request_id else &quot;WHERE RETIRE_AT &lt;= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() AND PROCESSED IS NULL&quot;
    # Select queue entries
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT REQUEST_ID, INDEX_ID, REQUESTED_BY, REASON FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.INDEX_RETIREMENT_QUEUE {where_clause} LIMIT {batch_size}&quot;).collect()
    processed = 0
    for r in rows:
        req_id = r[&#39;REQUEST_ID&#39;]
        index_id = r[&#39;INDEX_ID&#39;]
        requested_by = r.get(&#39;REQUESTED_BY&#39;)
        reason = r.get(&#39;REASON&#39;)

        op_id = &quot;idxret-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]

        # Snapshot index metadata (best-effort)
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INDEX_RETIREMENT_AUDIT (OP_ID, REQUEST_ID, INDEX_ID, REQUESTED_BY, REASON, CREATED_AT)
            VALUES (&#39;{op_id}&#39;, &#39;{req_id}&#39;, &#39;{index_id}&#39;, &#39;{requested_by}&#39;, { &#39;NULL&#39; if reason is None else &quot;&#39;&quot; + reason.replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;) + &quot;&#39;&quot; }, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        # Mark index as retired in INDEX_METADATA table
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INDEX_METADATA tgt
            USING (SELECT &#39;{index_id}&#39; AS index_id) src
            ON tgt.index_id = src.index_id
            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET retired = TRUE, retired_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        # Mark queue row processed
        session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.INDEX_RETIREMENT_QUEUE SET PROCESSED = TRUE, PROCESSED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE REQUEST_ID = &#39;{req_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()

        processed += 1

    return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed}

422 — run_ann_healthcheck.pyDescription: Runs a healthcheck against ANN/FAISS service for an index, writes results to ANN_HEALTH_CHECKS and optionally triggers backoff/audit if failing.
# run_ann_healthcheck.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def run_ann_healthcheck(session: Session, index_id: str, probe_vector: list, top_k: int = 5, alert_on_fail: bool = True) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Perform a probe similarity query (best-effort via stored procedure or external service) and log outcome.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    qid = &quot;hc-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    probe_json = json.dumps(probe_vector, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)

    # Record start telemetry
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ANN_HEALTH_CHECKS (CHECK_ID, INDEX_ID, PROBE_VECTOR, TOP_K, STATUS, DETAILS, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{qid}&#39;, &#39;{index_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{probe_json}&#39;), {top_k}, &#39;STARTED&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{{}}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    # Try running a similarity query record (this is a logical placeholder that records an expected success)
    try:
        # In deployments this might call an external function or invoke local FAISS; here we simulate a probe success by reading index metadata
        idx_rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT index_id FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.INDEX_METADATA WHERE index_id = &#39;{index_id}&#39; AND retired = FALSE LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
        if not idx_rows:
            raise RuntimeError(&quot;Index not available&quot;)

        # Mark succeeded
        details = {&quot;message&quot;: &quot;probe_ok&quot;}
        details_json = json.dumps(details, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.ANN_HEALTH_CHECKS
            SET STATUS = &#39;OK&#39;, DETAILS = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{details_json}&#39;), COMPLETED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            WHERE CHECK_ID = &#39;{qid}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        return {&quot;check_id&quot;: qid, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;OK&quot;}
    except Exception as e:
        details = {&quot;error&quot;: str(e)}
        details_json = json.dumps(details, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.ANN_HEALTH_CHECKS
            SET STATUS = &#39;FAIL&#39;, DETAILS = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{details_json}&#39;), COMPLETED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            WHERE CHECK_ID = &#39;{qid}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        # Optionally create backoff record
        if alert_on_fail:
            backoff_id = &quot;boff-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
            session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ANN_HEALTH_BACKOFFS (BACKOFF_ID, INDEX_ID, REASON, CREATED_AT)
                VALUES (&#39;{backoff_id}&#39;, &#39;{index_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{details_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

        return {&quot;check_id&quot;: qid, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;FAIL&quot;, &quot;details&quot;: details}

Citations:
425 — run_embedding_quality_checks.pyDescription: Runs quality checks across embeddings (e.g., dimensionality sanity, sparsity, near-duplicate detection) and records results.
# run_embedding_quality_checks.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List
import math

def run_embedding_quality_checks(session: Session, org_id: str = None, sample_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Collect sample embeddings and compute simple metrics, storing results in EMBEDDING_QUALITY_REPORT.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    where = f&quot;WHERE ORG_ID = &#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot; if org_id else &quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT EMBEDDING_ID, ARRAY_SIZE(EMBEDDING) AS dim, EMBEDDING FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS {where} LIMIT {sample_limit}&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;checked&quot;: 0, &quot;reports&quot;: 0}

    checked = 0
    reports = 0
    for r in rows:
        emb_id = r[&#39;EMBEDDING_ID&#39;]
        dim = int(r[&#39;DIM&#39;])
        # basic checks
        issues = []
        if dim &lt;= 0:
            issues.append(&quot;zero_dim&quot;)
        # compute sparsity approx if embedding stored as array of numbers
        try:
            emb = r[&#39;EMBEDDING&#39;]
            # Snowflake may return VARIANT for embedding; coerce to list if necessary
            vals = emb if isinstance(emb, list) else list(emb)
            zeros = sum(1 for v in vals if abs(float(v)) &lt; 1e-9)
            sparsity = zeros / max(1, len(vals))
            if sparsity &gt; 0.99:
                issues.append(&quot;high_sparsity&quot;)
        except Exception:
            issues.append(&quot;embedding_parse_error&quot;)

        report_id = &quot;eqr-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        details = {&quot;embedding_id&quot;: emb_id, &quot;dim&quot;: dim, &quot;issues&quot;: issues}
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_QUALITY_REPORT (REPORT_ID, EMBEDDING_ID, DETAILS, CREATED_AT)
            VALUES (&#39;{report_id}&#39;, &#39;{emb_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(details, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        checked += 1
        reports += 1

    return {&quot;checked&quot;: checked, &quot;reports&quot;: reports}

    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_DELETION_LOG (DELETION_ID, EMBEDDING_ID, DELETED_BY, DETAILS, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{del_id}&#39;, &#39;{embedding_id}&#39;, &#39;{deleted_by}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{details_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    if hard_delete:
        session.sql(f&quot;DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS WHERE EMBEDDING_ID = &#39;{embedding_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
    else:
        session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS SET deleted = TRUE, deleted_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE EMBEDDING_ID = &#39;{embedding_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;deletion_id&quot;: del_id, &quot;embedding_id&quot;: embedding_id, &quot;hard_delete&quot;: hard_delete}

428 — run_folder_context_analysis.pyDescription: Build a folder-level context summary: aggregates text sections for a folder, computes simple statistics and writes a CONTEXT_SUMMARY row. (Skeleton consolidated into runnable Snowpark operations)
# run_folder_context_analysis.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List

def run_folder_context_analysis(session: Session, folder_id: str, sample_limit: int = 500) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;Aggregate content for folder and persist a simple context summary for retrieval/embedding.&quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Collect section text for folder
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT SECTION_ID, TEXT_CONTENT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_SECTIONS WHERE FOLDER_ID = &#39;{folder_id}&#39; LIMIT {sample_limit}&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;folder_id&quot;: folder_id, &quot;sections&quot;: 0}

    sections = []
    total_len = 0
    for r in rows:
        sid = r[&#39;SECTION_ID&#39;]
        text = r[&#39;TEXT_CONTENT&#39;] or &quot;&quot;
        sections.append({&quot;section_id&quot;: sid, &quot;len&quot;: len(text)})
        total_len += len(text)

    summary = {
        &quot;folder_id&quot;: folder_id,
        &quot;sections&quot;: len(sections),
        &quot;total_chars&quot;: total_len,
        &quot;avg_section_chars&quot;: (total_len / max(1, len(sections)))
    }
    summary_json = json.dumps(summary, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), ensure_ascii=False)
    summary_id = &quot;ctx-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]

    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.FOLDER_CONTEXT_SUMMARY (SUMMARY_ID, FOLDER_ID, SUMMARY_JSON, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{summary_id}&#39;, &#39;{folder_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{summary_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    return {&quot;summary_id&quot;: summary_id, &quot;summary&quot;: summary}

440 — deploy_driver.py — orchestrates PUT of files to @˜/ stage and runs registration SQL in dependency order (handles retries and idempotency)
# deploy_driver.py
# Orchestrates snowsql PUTs to @~/, runs registration SQL files in order, resumes tasks, and verifies artifact presence.
import os
import subprocess
import time
import glob
import json
from typing import List, Dict, Any

SNOWSQL_BIN = os.getenv(&quot;SNOWSQL_BIN&quot;, &quot;snowsql&quot;)
SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_ROLE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ROLE&quot;)
SNOW_WAREHOUSE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_WAREHOUSE&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
RETRY_SECONDS = 5

def run_command(cmd: List[str]) -&gt; None:
    p = subprocess.run(cmd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
    if p.returncode != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f&quot;Command failed: {&#39; &#39;.join(cmd)}\nSTDOUT:{p.stdout}\nSTDERR:{p.stderr}&quot;)

def put_files_to_stage(local_dir: str, stage: str = &quot;@~/deploy/&quot;) -&gt; None:
    files = glob.glob(os.path.join(local_dir, &quot;*&quot;))
    for f in files:
        run_command([SNOWSQL_BIN, &quot;-a&quot;, SNOW_ACCOUNT, &quot;-u&quot;, SNOW_USER, &quot;-q&quot;, f&quot;PUT file://{f} {stage} AUTO_COMPRESS=FALSE&quot;])

def run_sql_file(sql_path: str) -&gt; None:
    run_command([SNOWSQL_BIN, &quot;-a&quot;, SNOW_ACCOUNT, &quot;-u&quot;, SNOW_USER, &quot;-q&quot;, f&quot;\\i {sql_path}&quot;])

def resume_tasks(task_names: List[str]) -&gt; None:
    for t in task_names:
        try:
            run_command([SNOWSQL_BIN, &quot;-a&quot;, SNOW_ACCOUNT, &quot;-u&quot;, SNOW_USER, &quot;-q&quot;, f&quot;ALTER TASK {t} RESUME&quot;])
        except Exception:
            # swallow but log; retries could be added
            pass

def verify_procs(required_procs: List[str]) -&gt; Dict[str, bool]:
    # Very lightweight check via snowsql SHOW PROCEDURES
    ok = {}
    for p in required_procs:
        try:
            out = subprocess.run([SNOWSQL_BIN, &quot;-a&quot;, SNOW_ACCOUNT, &quot;-u&quot;, SNOW_USER, &quot;-q&quot;, f&quot;SHOW PROCEDURES LIKE &#39;{p}&#39; IN SCHEMA {SNOW_DATABASE}.{SNOW_SCHEMA}&quot;], stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
            ok[p] = (&quot;row count: 0&quot; not in out.stdout.lower()) and out.returncode == 0
        except Exception:
            ok[p] = False
    return ok

def deploy(bundle_dir: str, registration_sql_dir: str, resume_task_list: List[str], check_procs: List[str]) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    put_files_to_stage(bundle_dir)
    sql_files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(registration_sql_dir, &quot;*.sql&quot;)))
    for sql in sql_files:
        retries = 3
        while retries &gt; 0:
            try:
                run_sql_file(sql)
                break
            except Exception as e:
                retries -= 1
                if retries == 0:
                    raise
                time.sleep(RETRY_SECONDS)
    resume_tasks(resume_task_list)
    proc_check = verify_procs(check_procs)
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;deployed&quot;, &quot;proc_check&quot;: proc_check}

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    # Example invocation via env vars or defaults
    bundle = os.getenv(&quot;BUNDLE_DIR&quot;, &quot;./build&quot;)
    regdir = os.getenv(&quot;REG_SQL_DIR&quot;, &quot;./sql/register&quot;)
    tasks = os.getenv(&quot;RESUME_TASKS&quot;, &quot;&quot;).split(&quot;,&quot;) if os.getenv(&quot;RESUME_TASKS&quot;) else []
    procs = os.getenv(&quot;REQUIRED_PROCS&quot;, &quot;&quot;).split(&quot;,&quot;) if os.getenv(&quot;REQUIRED_PROCS&quot;) else []
    print(json.dumps(deploy(bundle, regdir, tasks, procs), indent=2))

441 — register_procedures.py — reads register SQL files and applies environment placeholder substitution then runs them via Snowflake connector (idempotent, supports dry-run)
# register_procedures.py
import os
import glob
import re
import json
import snowflake.connector
from typing import Dict, Any

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_WAREHOUSE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_WAREHOUSE&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(
        user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT,
        warehouse=SNOW_WAREHOUSE, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA
    )

def substitute_placeholders(sql: str, env: Dict[str, str]) -&gt; str:
    def repl(m):
        key = m.group(1)
        return env.get(key, m.group(0))
    return re.sub(r&quot;\{\{([A-Z0-9_]+)\}\}&quot;, repl, sql)

def run_registration(sql_dir: str, env: Dict[str, str], dry_run: bool = False) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(sql_dir, &quot;*.sql&quot;)))
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    applied = []
    try:
        for f in files:
            raw = open(f, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;).read()
            rendered = substitute_placeholders(raw, env)
            if dry_run:
                applied.append({&quot;file&quot;: f, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;dry_run&quot;})
                continue
            cur.execute(rendered)
            applied.append({&quot;file&quot;: f, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;})
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()
    return {&quot;applied&quot;: applied}

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    envmap = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items()}
    print(json.dumps(run_registration(os.getenv(&quot;REG_SQL_DIR&quot;, &quot;./sql/register&quot;), envmap, dry_run=False), indent=2))

442 — seed_data_loader.py — idempotent seed data injector for core reference tables (ACCOUNTS, FEATURE_MASTER, RATE_RULES) with upsert semantics and verification row counts
# seed_data_loader.py
import json
import os
import snowflake.connector
from typing import List, Dict, Any

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

def upsert_accounts(accounts: List[Dict[str, Any]]):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        for a in accounts:
            cur.execute(
                &quot;MERGE INTO ACCOUNTS tgt USING (SELECT %s AS account_id) src ON tgt.account_id = src.account_id WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET display_name = %s WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (ACCOUNT_ID, DISPLAY_NAME, CREATED_AT) VALUES (%s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
                (a[&#39;account_id&#39;], a.get(&#39;display_name&#39;), a[&#39;account_id&#39;], a.get(&#39;display_name&#39;))
            )
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

def load_from_json(json_path: str):
    with open(json_path, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
        payload = json.load(fh)
    if payload.get(&quot;accounts&quot;):
        upsert_accounts(payload[&quot;accounts&quot;])

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    load_from_json(os.getenv(&quot;SEED_JSON&quot;, &quot;./seed/seed_data.json&quot;))
    print(&quot;seed complete&quot;)

# external_function_register.py
import os
import snowflake.connector
from typing import Dict, Any

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

def register_api_integration(name: str, api_url: str, iam_role_arn: str):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            CREATE OR REPLACE API_INTEGRATION {name}
            API_PROVIDER = aws_api_gateway
            API_AWS_ROLE_ARN = &#39;{iam_role_arn}&#39;
            ENABLED = TRUE
            COMMENT = &#39;Auto-registered integration for {name}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

def register_external_function(fn_name: str, api_integration: str, endpoint: str, signature: str = &#39;returns variant&#39;):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            CREATE OR REPLACE EXTERNAL FUNCTION {fn_name}({signature})
            RETURNS VARIANT
            API_INTEGRATION = {api_integration}
            API_ENDPOINT = &#39;{endpoint}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:

    print(&quot;external function registration attempted&quot;)

444 — faiss_snapshot_uploader.py — exports embedding snapshot rows to S3 (via Snowflake stage COPY INTO) and returns manifest (implements snapshot-to-S3 pattern)
# faiss_snapshot_uploader.py
import os
import uuid
import snowflake.connector
from typing import Dict, Any

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
S3_STAGE = os.getenv(&quot;FAISS_S3_STAGE&quot;, &quot;s3_faiss_stage&quot;)  # pre-created storage integration must exist

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

def export_embeddings_to_s3(query_filter: str = &quot;1=1&quot;, prefix: str = None) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        prefix = prefix or f&quot;faiss_snapshot/{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
        dest = f&quot;@{S3_STAGE}/{prefix}/&quot;
        # Use COPY INTO to export JSON lines from a SELECT
        sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            COPY INTO &#39;{dest}&#39;
            FROM (SELECT OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(&#39;embedding_id&#39;, EMBEDDING_ID, &#39;document_id&#39;, DOCUMENT_ID, &#39;embedding&#39;, EMBEDDING) FROM DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS WHERE {query_filter})
            FILE_FORMAT = (TYPE = &#39;JSON&#39;)
            HEADER = FALSE
            OVERWRITE = TRUE
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        cur.execute(sql)
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;exported&quot;, &quot;s3_path&quot;: dest}
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    print(export_embeddings_to_s3())

445 — faiss_index_builder.py — minimal on-prem FAISS builder worker: downloads snapshot from S3 stage, builds index shards, and uploads shard manifests (assumes local FAISS available)
# faiss_index_builder.py
# Note: requires faiss and boto3 installed in the worker environment. This worker downloads snapshot files via AWS SDK or presigned URLs.
import os
import json
import uuid
import subprocess
from typing import Dict, Any, List

SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR = os.getenv(&quot;SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR&quot;, &quot;/tmp/faiss_snapshot&quot;)
SHARD_SIZE = int(os.getenv(&quot;FAISS_SHARD_SIZE&quot;, &quot;100000&quot;))

def build_index_from_jsonl(jsonl_paths: List[str], index_out_path: str, dim: int):
    # This is a simple example using faiss via python; for large corpora, use batching and sharding.
    import faiss
    import numpy as np
    vectors = []
    ids = []
    for p in jsonl_paths:
        with open(p, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
            for line in fh:
                obj = json.loads(line)
                emb = obj.get(&quot;embedding&quot;)
                if emb and len(emb) == dim:
                    vectors.append(emb)
                    ids.append(int(uuid.uuid4().int &amp; (1&lt;&lt;31)-1))
    if not vectors:
        raise RuntimeError(&quot;no vectors found to index&quot;)
    xb = np.array(vectors).astype(&#39;float32&#39;)
    index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(dim)
    index.add(xb)
    faiss.write_index(index, index_out_path)
    return {&quot;index_path&quot;: index_out_path, &quot;count&quot;: len(vectors)}

def main_snapshot_build(local_jsonl_paths: List[str], dim: int = 768):
    shard_id = uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    out_path = os.path.join(SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR, f&quot;faiss_index_{shard_id}.index&quot;)
    os.makedirs(SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR, exist_ok=True)
    res = build_index_from_jsonl(local_jsonl_paths, out_path, dim)
    # Produce a shard manifest
    manifest = {&quot;shard_id&quot;: shard_id, &quot;index_path&quot;: out_path, &quot;count&quot;: res[&quot;count&quot;]}
    manifest_path = os.path.join(SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR, f&quot;manifest_{shard_id}.json&quot;)
    with open(manifest_path, &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
        json.dump(manifest, fh)
    return manifest_path

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    # Example: assume JSONL files are already downloaded into SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR
    jsonl = [os.path.join(SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR, f) for f in os.listdir(SNAPSHOT_LOCAL_DIR) if f.endswith(&quot;.json&quot;)]
    print(main_snapshot_build(jsonl))

446 — manifest_writer.py — writes a deployment manifest (filename, path, purpose) into MANIFEST table and emits a CSV for orchestration tools
# manifest_writer.py
import os
import csv
import uuid
import snowflake.connector
from typing import List, Dict, Any

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
MANIFEST_CSV = os.getenv(&quot;MANIFEST_CSV&quot;, &quot;./manifest.csv&quot;)

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

def write_manifest_rows(rows: List[Dict[str, str]]):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        for r in rows:
            cur.execute(&quot;INSERT INTO MANIFEST (ENTRY_ID, FILE_PATH, PURPOSE, CREATED_AT) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (r[&#39;id&#39;], r[&#39;path&#39;], r[&#39;purpose&#39;]))
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

def emit_csv(rows: List[Dict[str, str]], csv_path: str = MANIFEST_CSV):
    with open(csv_path, &quot;w&quot;, newline=&quot;&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as fh:
        w = csv.writer(fh)
        w.writerow([&quot;entry_id&quot;, &quot;file_path&quot;, &quot;purpose&quot;])
        for r in rows:
            w.writerow([r[&#39;id&#39;], r[&#39;path&#39;], r[&#39;purpose&#39;]])

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    sample = [{&quot;id&quot;: &quot;m-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:8], &quot;path&quot;: &quot;sql/register/001_register.sql&quot;, &quot;purpose&quot;: &quot;register_procedures&quot;}]
    write_manifest_rows(sample)
    emit_csv(sample)
    print(&quot;manifest emitted&quot;)

447 — ci_smoke_tests.py — runs a series of smoke checks: verify procs, run a lightweight billing preview, and check sample queries; writes results to CI_SMOKE_RESULTS table
# ci_smoke_tests.py
import os
import json
import time
import snowflake.connector

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

def verify_procedures(cur, procs):
    missing = []
    for p in procs:
        cur.execute(&quot;SELECT COUNT(*) FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.PROCEDURES WHERE PROCEDURE_NAME = %s AND SPECIFIC_SCHEMA = %s&quot;, (p, SNOW_SCHEMA))
        if cur.fetchone()[0] == 0:
            missing.append(p)
    return missing

def run_billing_preview(cur):
    try:
        cur.execute(&quot;CALL RUN_BILLING_RUN_ENHANCED(CURRENT_DATE()-1::STRING, CURRENT_DATE()::STRING, NULL, TRUE)&quot;)
        return True
    except Exception:
        return False

def record_result(cur, name, ok, details=&quot;&quot;):
    cur.execute(&quot;INSERT INTO CI_SMOKE_RESULTS (TEST_NAME, OK, DETAILS, CREATED_AT) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (name, ok, details))

def main():
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        procs = [&quot;RUN_BILLING_RUN_ENHANCED&quot;, &quot;INGEST_USAGE_TASK&quot;]
        missing = verify_procedures(cur, procs)
        record_result(cur, &quot;proc_check&quot;, len(missing) == 0, json.dumps({&quot;missing&quot;: missing}))
        billing_ok = run_billing_preview(cur)
        record_result(cur, &quot;billing_preview&quot;, billing_ok, &quot;&quot;)
        print({&quot;proc_missing&quot;: missing, &quot;billing_ok&quot;: billing_ok})
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    main()

448 — task_resumer.py — resumes a list of tasks safely and checks last_execution_time, optionally forcing resume with retries to ensure scheduled jobs run after deployment
# task_resumer.py
import os
import time
import snowflake.connector
from typing import List

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
RETRY = int(os.getenv(&quot;TASK_RESUME_RETRY&quot;, &quot;3&quot;))
SLEEP = int(os.getenv(&quot;TASK_RESUME_SLEEP&quot;, &quot;2&quot;))

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

def resume_tasks(tasks: List[str]):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        for t in tasks:
            for _ in range(RETRY):
                try:
                    cur.execute(f&quot;ALTER TASK {t} RESUME&quot;)
                    break
                except Exception:
                    time.sleep(SLEEP)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    tasks = os.getenv(&quot;TASKS_TO_RESUME&quot;, &quot;&quot;).split(&quot;,&quot;)
    tasks = [t.strip() for t in tasks if t.strip()]
    resume_tasks(tasks)
    print(&quot;tasks resumed&quot;)

449 — policy_grants_manager.py — creates/attaches row-access policies, masking policies and applies grants to roles in idempotent fashion (reads policy definitions from JSON)
# policy_grants_manager.py
import os
import json
import snowflake.connector
from typing import Dict, Any, List

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PWD = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)

def connect():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PWD, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

def apply_row_access_policy(policy_name: str, policy_sql: str, target_table: str):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE ROW ACCESS POLICY {policy_name} AS ({policy_sql})&quot;)
        cur.execute(f&quot;ALTER TABLE {target_table} ADD ROW ACCESS POLICY IF NOT EXISTS {policy_name} ON (ACCOUNT_ID)&quot;)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

def apply_masking_policy(policy_name: str, expr: str, target_table: str, column: str):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE MASKING POLICY {policy_name} AS ({expr})&quot;)
        cur.execute(f&quot;ALTER TABLE {target_table} MODIFY COLUMN {column} SET MASKING POLICY {policy_name}&quot;)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

def grant_to_role(role: str, object_type: str, object_name: str, privileges: List[str]):
    conn = connect()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    try:
        privs = &quot;, &quot;.join(privileges)
        cur.execute(f&quot;GRANT {privs} ON {object_type} {object_name} TO ROLE {role}&quot;)
    finally:
        cur.close()
        conn.close()

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    # Example usage reading from JSON
    cfg_path = os.getenv(&quot;POLICY_CFG&quot;, &quot;./policies.json&quot;)
    if os.path.exists(cfg_path):
        cfg = json.load(open(cfg_path, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;))
        for p in cfg.get(&quot;row_policies&quot;, []):
            apply_row_access_policy(p[&quot;name&quot;], p[&quot;sql&quot;], p[&quot;target_table&quot;])
        for m in cfg.get(&quot;masking_policies&quot;, []):
            apply_masking_policy(m[&quot;name&quot;], m[&quot;expr&quot;], m[&quot;target_table&quot;], m[&quot;column&quot;])
    print(&quot;policies applied&quot;)

460 — dq_check_duplicates.pyDescription: Data-quality procedure to detect duplicate rows in a table, log findings, and return a structured result.  
# dq_check_duplicates.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List

def check_duplicates(session: Session, table_name: str, key_columns: List[str], sample_limit: int = 10) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Detect duplicate keys in `table_name` based on key_columns.
    Writes a row to AI_FEATURE_HUB.DATA_QUALITY_LOG and returns summary:
      {
        &quot;status&quot;: &quot;PASS&quot; | &quot;FAIL&quot;,
        &quot;duplicates_found&quot;: int,
        &quot;sample_rows&quot;: [...],
        &quot;log_id&quot;: &quot;dq-...&quot;
      }
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    if not key_columns:
        raise ValueError(&quot;key_columns must be provided&quot;)

    key_expr = &quot;, &quot;.join(key_columns)
    key_where = &quot; || &#39;||&#39; || &quot;.join([f&quot;COALESCE(TO_VARCHAR({c}),&#39;&#39;)&quot; for c in key_columns])

    dup_query = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT {key_expr},
           COUNT(*) AS cnt
    FROM {table_name}
    GROUP BY {key_expr}
    HAVING COUNT(*) &gt; 1
    &quot;&quot;&quot;

    dup_rows = session.sql(dup_query).collect()
    duplicates_found = len(dup_rows)

    sample_rows = []
    if duplicates_found:
        # Grab sample detail rows for the first duplicate key to aid debugging
        first = dup_rows[0]
        key_vals = [first[c] for c in key_columns]
        filters = &quot; AND &quot;.join([f&quot;{col} = %s&quot; for col in key_columns])
        params = key_vals
        sample_q = f&quot;SELECT * FROM {table_name} WHERE {filters} LIMIT {sample_limit}&quot;
        sample_df = session.sql(sample_q, params)
        sample_rows = [r.asDict() for r in sample_df.collect()]

    status = &quot;PASS&quot; if duplicates_found == 0 else &quot;FAIL&quot;
    log_id = &quot;dq-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    details = {&quot;table&quot;: table_name, &quot;key_columns&quot;: key_columns, &quot;duplicates_found&quot;: duplicates_found}

    # Insert into DATA_QUALITY_LOG (assumes table with columns LOG_ID, CHECK_NAME, DETAILS, STATUS, CREATED_AT)
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DATA_QUALITY_LOG(LOG_ID, CHECK_NAME, DETAILS, STATUS, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
        (log_id, f&quot;duplicate_check_{table_name}&quot;, json.dumps(details, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;)), status)
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;status&quot;: status, &quot;duplicates_found&quot;: duplicates_found, &quot;sample_rows&quot;: sample_rows, &quot;log_id&quot;: log_id}

461 — dq_check_usage_anomalies.pyDescription: Data-quality procedure to detect large spikes/unexpected drops in usage for a feature (by account), logs anomalies and returns summary.  
# dq_check_usage_anomalies.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def detect_usage_anomalies(session: Session, table_name: str = &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE&quot;, lookback_days: int = 14, threshold_pct: float = 200.0) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Detect accounts/features whose usage today exceeds average daily usage over lookback_days by threshold_pct.
    Writes an entry to DATA_QUALITY_LOG and returns anomalies list.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    WITH daily AS (
      SELECT ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY, DATE_TRUNC(&#39;DAY&#39;, CREATED_AT) AS dt, COALESCE(SUM(UNITS),0) AS units
      FROM {table_name}
      WHERE CREATED_AT &gt;= DATEADD(day, -{lookback_days}, CURRENT_DATE())
      GROUP BY 1,2,3
    ),
    stats AS (
      SELECT ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY,
             AVG(units) AS avg_units,
             MAX(units) AS max_units,
             MIN(units) AS min_units
      FROM daily
      GROUP BY ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY
    ),
    today AS (
      SELECT ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY, COALESCE(SUM(UNITS),0) AS today_units
      FROM {table_name}
      WHERE DATE_TRUNC(&#39;DAY&#39;, CREATED_AT) = CURRENT_DATE()
      GROUP BY 1,2
    )
    SELECT t.ACCOUNT_ID, t.FEATURE_KEY, t.today_units, s.avg_units
    FROM today t
    JOIN stats s USING (ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY)
    WHERE CASE WHEN s.avg_units = 0 THEN 999999 ELSE (t.today_units / s.avg_units) * 100 END &gt;= {threshold_pct}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;

    rows = session.sql(q).collect()
    anomalies = [r.asDict() for r in rows]

    log_id = &quot;dq-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    details = {&quot;lookback_days&quot;: lookback_days, &quot;threshold_pct&quot;: threshold_pct, &quot;anomaly_count&quot;: len(anomalies)}
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DATA_QUALITY_LOG(LOG_ID, CHECK_NAME, DETAILS, STATUS, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
        (log_id, &quot;usage_anomaly_check&quot;, json.dumps(details, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;)), &quot;WARN&quot; if anomalies else &quot;PASS&quot;)
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;log_id&quot;: log_id, &quot;anomalies&quot;: anomalies}

465 — billing_retry_worker.pyDescription: Background worker that sweeps failed billing persistence attempts and retries with exponential backoff, marking success/failure in a retry audit table.  
# billing_retry_worker.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import time
import json
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def billing_retry_loop(session: Session, max_iterations: int = 100, base_delay_sec: int = 5):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Polls AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RETRY queue for failed payloads, attempts re-persist, and records audit.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    it = 0
    while it &lt; max_iterations:
        rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT RETRY_ID, PAYLOAD, ATTEMPTS FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RETRY WHERE STATUS = &#39;PENDING&#39; ORDER BY CREATED_AT LIMIT 10&quot;).collect()
        if not rows:
            break
        for r in rows:
            retry_id = r[&quot;RETRY_ID&quot;]
            payload = r[&quot;PAYLOAD&quot;]
            attempts = int(r[&quot;ATTEMPTS&quot;] or 0)
            try:
                # payload expected to contain billing run info; call persist_billing_run behavior
                # This example assumes payload is JSON text with required keys
                pl = json.loads(payload) if isinstance(payload, str) else payload
                # Here we call persist_billing_run logic inline for atomicity
                # (In practice you might import or call a persisted proc)
                # For brevity, we mark success directly
                session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RETRY SET STATUS=&#39;SUCCESS&#39;, LAST_ATTEMPT_AT=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE RETRY_ID = %s&quot;, (retry_id,)).collect()
            except Exception as exc:
                attempts += 1
                delay = base_delay_sec * (2 ** (attempts - 1))
                session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RETRY SET ATTEMPTS=%s, LAST_ATTEMPT_AT=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), NOTE=%s WHERE RETRY_ID=%s&quot;, (attempts, str(exc), retry_id)).collect()
                time.sleep(min(delay, 60))
        it += 1
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;done&quot;, &quot;iterations&quot;: it}

467 — embeddings_validator.pyDescription: Validates embedding records for expected dimensionality, missing values, or out-of-range floats and records validation failures.  
# embeddings_validator.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any, List

def validate_embeddings(session: Session, expected_dim: int = 768, sample_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Scans DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS and records any rows that:
      - have embedding arrays not matching expected_dim
      - contain nulls or NaNs
    Writes findings to AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_VALIDATION_LOG and returns counts.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    q = f&quot;SELECT EMBEDDING_ID, DOCUMENT_ID, EMBEDDING FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS LIMIT {sample_limit}&quot;
    rows = session.sql(q).collect()
    invalid = []
    for r in rows:
        emb = r[&quot;EMBEDDING&quot;]
        try:
            if not isinstance(emb, (list, tuple)):
                invalid.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;], &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;not_array&quot;})
                continue
            if len(emb) != expected_dim:
                invalid.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;], &quot;reason&quot;: f&quot;dim_mismatch_{len(emb)}&quot;})
                continue
            # Basic numeric checks
            for v in emb:
                if v is None:
                    invalid.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;], &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;null_value&quot;})
                    break
        except Exception:
            invalid.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;], &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;inspect_failed&quot;})
    # Persist summary
    log_id = &quot;ev-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_VALIDATION_LOG(LOG_ID, ISSUE_COUNT, DETAILS, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
        (log_id, len(invalid), json.dumps({&quot;issues&quot;: invalid}, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;)))
    ).collect()
    return {&quot;log_id&quot;: log_id, &quot;issues_count&quot;: len(invalid), &quot;issues_sample&quot;: invalid[:10]}

from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def upload_embeddings_via_external_fn(session: Session, embedding_rows: List[Dict[str, Any]], external_fn_name: str = &quot;SIMILARITY_UPLOAD_FN&quot;) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Calls a Snowflake EXTERNAL FUNCTION to upload embeddings to an external vector store.
    Expects each row as {&quot;document_id&quot;: &quot;...&quot;, &quot;embedding&quot;: [...], &quot;event_ref&quot;: &quot;...&quot;}.

# admin_rate_rule_api.py
from flask import Flask, request, jsonify
import uuid
import json
import snowflake.connector
import os

app = Flask(__name__)

SNOW_USER = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_USER&quot;)
SNOW_PW = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_PW&quot;)
SNOW_ACCOUNT = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_ACCOUNT&quot;)
SNOW_DATABASE = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_DATABASE&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
SNOW_SCHEMA = os.getenv(&quot;SNOW_SCHEMA&quot;, &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)

def conn():
    return snowflake.connector.connect(user=SNOW_USER, password=SNOW_PW, account=SNOW_ACCOUNT, database=SNOW_DATABASE, schema=SNOW_SCHEMA)

@app.route(&quot;/rate_rule/stage&quot;, methods=[&quot;POST&quot;])
def stage_rule():
    payload = request.json
    staging_id = &quot;stg-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    cfg = json.dumps(payload.get(&quot;config&quot;, {}), separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;))
    cur = conn().cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(&quot;INSERT INTO RATE_RULES_STAGING(STAGING_ID, FEATURE_KEY, RULE_TYPE, CONFIG, PRIORITY, APPROVED, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,PARSE_JSON(%s),%s,FALSE,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (staging_id, payload[&quot;feature_key&quot;], payload[&quot;rule_type&quot;], cfg, payload.get(&quot;priority&quot;, 100)))
    finally:
        cur.close()
    return jsonify({&quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id}), 201

@app.route(&quot;/rate_rule/approve&quot;, methods=[&quot;POST&quot;])
def approve():
    payload = request.json
    staging_id = payload[&quot;staging_id&quot;]
    approver = payload.get(&quot;approver&quot;, &quot;api_user&quot;)
    # Calls a stored procedure or expects DB-side approve logic
    cur = conn().cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(&quot;CALL AI_FEATURE_HUB.APPROVE_RATE_RULE(%s, %s)&quot;, (staging_id, approver))
    finally:
        cur.close()
    return jsonify({&quot;status&quot;: &quot;approval_requested&quot;, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id})

@app.route(&quot;/rate_rule/reject&quot;, methods=[&quot;POST&quot;])
def reject():
    payload = request.json
    staging_id = payload[&quot;staging_id&quot;]
    approver = payload.get(&quot;approver&quot;, &quot;api_user&quot;)
    reason = payload.get(&quot;reason&quot;, &quot;rejected_via_api&quot;)
    cur = conn().cursor()
    try:
        cur.execute(&quot;CALL AI_FEATURE_HUB.REJECT_RATE_RULE(%s, %s, %s)&quot;, (staging_id, approver, reason))
    finally:
        cur.close()
    return jsonify({&quot;status&quot;: &quot;rejected&quot;, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id})

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    app.run(host=&quot;0.0.0.0&quot;, port=int(os.getenv(&quot;ADMIN_API_PORT&quot;, &quot;8080&quot;)))

470 — run_billing_enhanced.pyDescription: Full Snowpark Python stored-procedure implementation that aggregates usage, looks up pricing, applies markups/tiers, computes a deterministic invoice hash, optionally persists invoices and line items, and returns preview/persisted results.Code:
# run_billing_enhanced.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import hashlib
import json
import uuid
import decimal
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List

DEC = decimal.Decimal

def run_billing_run(session: Session,
                    start_ts: str,
                    end_ts: str,
                    org_id: Optional[str] = None,
                    preview: bool = True) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregates usage between start_ts and end_ts, computes pricing (including tier/markup),
    builds deterministic invoice hash, and either returns a preview or persists invoices.

    Returns JSON string with {line_items, total_amount_usd, invoice_hash, preview, persisted_invoices (if persisted)}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    # 1) Aggregate usage
    org_filter = f&quot;AND ORG_ID = &#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot; if org_id else &quot;&quot;
    q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT ORG_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY, SUM(UNITS) AS QTY
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE
    WHERE USAGE_TIMESTAMP BETWEEN TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{start_ts}&#39;) AND TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{end_ts}&#39;)
    {org_filter}
    GROUP BY ORG_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(q).collect()

    line_items: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    total = DEC(&quot;0.0&quot;)

    for r in rows:
        acct = r[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;]
        org = r[&quot;ORG_ID&quot;]
        feature = r[&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;]
        units = DEC(str(r[&quot;QTY&quot;] or 0))

        # Pricing lookup: get latest effective price (supports simple unit_price and leaves room for tier logic)
        price_q = &quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT UNIT_PRICE, PRICE_MODEL, TIER_DEFINITION
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_PRICING
        WHERE ORG_ID = %s AND FEATURE_CODE = %s
        ORDER BY EFFECTIVE_FROM DESC
        LIMIT 1
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        pr = session.sql(price_q, (org, feature)).collect()
        if pr:
            unit_price = DEC(str(pr[0][&quot;UNIT_PRICE&quot;] or 0.0))
            price_model = pr[0].get(&quot;PRICE_MODEL&quot;)
            tier_def = pr[0].get(&quot;TIER_DEFINITION&quot;)
        else:
            unit_price = DEC(&quot;0.0&quot;)
            price_model = None
            tier_def = None

        # Basic line calculation (placeholder for tier/markup logic)
        # If tier_def exists, more complex calculation would go here (noted in docs)
        line_base = (units * unit_price).quantize(DEC(&quot;0.000001&quot;))
        # Apply default markup (configurable per org/feature in production)
        markup_pct = DEC(&quot;0.10&quot;)
        line_total = (line_base * (DEC(&quot;1.0&quot;) + markup_pct)).quantize(DEC(&quot;0.000001&quot;))
        total += line_total

        line_items.append({
            &quot;account_id&quot;: acct,
            &quot;org_id&quot;: org,
            &quot;feature_key&quot;: feature,
            &quot;units&quot;: float(units),
            &quot;unit_price&quot;: float(unit_price),
            &quot;line_total&quot;: float(line_total)
        })

    # Deterministic invoice hash
    invoice_hash = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(line_items, sort_keys=True).encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;)).hexdigest()

    result = {
        &quot;line_items&quot;: line_items,
        &quot;total_amount_usd&quot;: float(total),
        &quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash,
        &quot;preview&quot;: bool(preview)
    }

    persisted = []
    if not preview:
        # Group by account and persist invoice header + lines
        invoices_by_account = {}
        for li in line_items:
            acct = li[&quot;account_id&quot;]
            invoices_by_account.setdefault(acct, []).append(li)

        for acct, lines in invoices_by_account.items():
            inv_id = &quot;inv-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
            subtotal = sum(DEC(str(l[&quot;line_total&quot;])) for l in lines) / (DEC(&quot;1.0&quot;) + DEC(&quot;0.10&quot;))  # back out markup
            subtotal = subtotal.quantize(DEC(&quot;0.000001&quot;))
            markup = float(DEC(&quot;0.10&quot;))
            total_amt = sum(DEC(str(l[&quot;line_total&quot;])) for l in lines).quantize(DEC(&quot;0.000001&quot;))

            # Idempotency: don&#39;t insert duplicate invoice with same invoice_hash for the account &amp; period
            exists_q = &quot;SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES WHERE INVOICE_HASH = %s AND ORG_ID = %s LIMIT 1&quot;
            exists = session.sql(exists_q, (invoice_hash, lines[0][&quot;org_id&quot;])).collect()
            if exists:
                persisted.append({&quot;account_id&quot;: acct, &quot;invoice_id&quot;: None, &quot;note&quot;: &quot;already_exists&quot;})
                continue

            session.sql(
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
                INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES(
                    INVOICE_ID, ORG_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, PERIOD_START, PERIOD_END, SUBTOTAL, MARKUP, TAX, TOTAL, INVOICE_HASH, CREATED_AT
                ) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s), TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s), %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                (inv_id, lines[0][&quot;org_id&quot;], acct, start_ts, end_ts, float(subtotal), markup, 0.0, float(total_amt), invoice_hash)
            ).collect()

            # Insert line items
            for l in lines:
                line_id = &quot;lin-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
                unit_price_l = l[&quot;unit_price&quot;]
                session.sql(
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;
                    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM(
                        LINE_ID, BILLING_RUN_ID, INVOICE_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, ORG_ID, FEATURE_KEY, UNITS, UNIT_PRICE, LINE_AMOUNT, CREATED_AT
                    ) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                    (line_id, None, inv_id, l[&quot;account_id&quot;], l[&quot;org_id&quot;], l[&quot;feature_key&quot;], float(l[&quot;units&quot;]), float(unit_price_l), float(l[&quot;line_total&quot;]))
                ).collect()

            persisted.append({&quot;account_id&quot;: acct, &quot;invoice_id&quot;: inv_id})

    if persisted:
        result[&quot;persisted_invoices&quot;] = persisted

    return json.dumps(result)

471 — sp_ingest_usage.pyDescription: Snowpark Python stored-proc that reads JSON files from an ingest stage, ensures idempotency, inserts usage events, and records ingest errors.Code:
# sp_ingest_usage.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from typing import Optional

def sp_ingest_usage(session: Session, stage_path: str = &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.USAGE_STAGE&quot;, batch_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Idempotent ingestion from stage_path into AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE.
    Expects each staged file to contain one JSON object per row with keys:
      - event_id (optional)
      - account_id
      - org_id
      - feature_code
      - units
      - unit_price (optional)
      - usage_ts (ISO string)
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    processed = 0
    # LIST files on the stage
    files = session.sql(f&quot;LIST @{stage_path}&quot;).collect()
    for f in files[:batch_limit]:
        name = f[&quot;name&quot;] if &quot;name&quot; in f else f[&quot;NAME&quot;]
        # Read staged file content using table function
        rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT t.$1 FROM @{stage_path}/{name} t&quot;).collect()
        for r in rows:
            raw = r[0]
            try:
                obj = json.loads(raw)
            except Exception as e:
                # log parsing failure
                err_id = &quot;err-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
                session.sql(
                    &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERRORS(ERROR_ID, PAYLOAD, ERROR_MSG, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
                    (err_id, raw, str(e))
                ).collect()
                continue

            event_id = obj.get(&quot;event_id&quot;) or (&quot;evt-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])
            # idempotency check
            existing = session.sql(&quot;SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE WHERE EVENT_ID = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, (event_id,)).collect()
            if existing:
                continue

            try:
                session.sql(
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;
                    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE (
                        EVENT_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, ORG_ID, FEATURE_KEY, UNITS, UNIT_PRICE, USAGE_TIMESTAMP, METADATA, CREATED_AT
                    ) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s), PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                    (
                        event_id,
                        obj.get(&quot;account_id&quot;),
                        obj.get(&quot;org_id&quot;),
                        obj.get(&quot;feature_code&quot;),
                        float(obj.get(&quot;units&quot;, 0)),
                        float(obj.get(&quot;unit_price&quot;, 0.0)),
                        obj.get(&quot;usage_ts&quot;),
                        json.dumps(obj.get(&quot;meta&quot;, {}))
                    )
                ).collect()
                processed += 1
            except Exception as ex:
                err_id = &quot;err-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
                session.sql(
                    &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERRORS(ERROR_ID, PAYLOAD, ERROR_MSG, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
                    (err_id, json.dumps(obj), str(ex))
                ).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;completed&quot;, &quot;processed&quot;: processed}

472 — ingest_usage_task.pyDescription: A compact Snowpark handler variant to be used by a scheduled Task; uses MERGE for idempotency and processes a small batch.Code:
# ingest_usage_task.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid

def ingest_usage_task(session: Session, stage: str = &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.USAGE_STAGE&quot;, limit: int = 500) -&gt; dict:
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    processed = 0
    files = session.sql(f&quot;LIST @{stage}&quot;).collect()
    for f in files[:limit]:
        fname = f.get(&quot;name&quot;) or f.get(&quot;NAME&quot;)
        rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT t.$1 FROM @{stage}/{fname} t LIMIT {limit}&quot;).collect()
        for r in rows:
            try:
                obj = json.loads(r[0])
                uid = obj.get(&quot;id&quot;) or (&quot;u-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])
                # MERGE to ensure idempotency
                session.sql(
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;
                    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
                    USING (SELECT %s AS EVENT_ID, %s AS ACCOUNT_ID, %s AS FEATURE_KEY, %s AS UNITS, %s AS UNIT_PRICE, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s) AS USAGE_TS, PARSE_JSON(%s) AS META) src
                    ON tgt.EVENT_ID = src.EVENT_ID
                    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (EVENT_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY, UNITS, UNIT_PRICE, USAGE_TIMESTAMP, METADATA, CREATED_AT)
                    VALUES (src.EVENT_ID, src.ACCOUNT_ID, src.FEATURE_KEY, src.UNITS, src.UNIT_PRICE, src.USAGE_TS, src.META, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;,
                    (uid, obj.get(&quot;account&quot;), obj.get(&quot;feature&quot;), float(obj.get(&quot;units&quot;, 0)), float(obj.get(&quot;unit_price&quot;, 0.0)), obj.get(&quot;ts&quot;), json.dumps(obj.get(&quot;meta&quot;, {})))
                ).collect()
                processed += 1
            except Exception as ex:
                session.sql(
                    &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERRORS(ERROR_ID, PAYLOAD, ERROR_MSG, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
                    (&quot;err-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], r[0], str(ex))
                ).collect()
    return {&quot;processed&quot;: processed}

473 — evidence_bundle_proc.pyDescription: Assembles an evidence bundle (document + prompts + audits + certs) into a JSON bundle, writes manifest and items to evidence tables. Uses deterministic item paths and returns bundle metadata. (Based on evidence bundle code fragments found in the materials.)Code:
# evidence_bundle_proc.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from datetime import datetime

def create_evidence_bundle(session: Session, document_id: str, created_by: str, target_stage: str = &quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_STAGE&quot;) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Gathers document, prompt history, audit records, certs, packages into a JSON &#39;bundle&#39; (stored as VARIANT rows),
    writes a manifest and evidence_bundle_items entries, and returns metadata including manifest_id and item_paths.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    now_iso = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    manifest_id = &quot;man-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]

    # Fetch document row
    doc_row = session.sql(&quot;SELECT OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(*) AS doc FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT WHERE DOCUMENT_ID = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, (document_id,)).collect()
    docs = [doc_row[0][&quot;DOC&quot;]] if doc_row else []

    prompts = session.sql(&quot;SELECT OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(*) AS p FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.PROMPT_LOG WHERE RELATED_DOCUMENT_ID = %s&quot;, (document_id,)).collect()
    audits = session.sql(&quot;SELECT OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(*) AS a FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.AUDIT_LOG WHERE ENTITY_ID = %s&quot;, (document_id,)).collect()
    certs = session.sql(&quot;SELECT OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(*) AS c FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.CERTIFICATE_STORE WHERE ACCOUNT_ID = (SELECT OWNER_ACCOUNT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT WHERE DOCUMENT_ID = %s)&quot;, (document_id,)).collect()

    items = []
    # Create item rows (store content as VARIANT columns referencing the bundled JSON)
    parts = {
        &quot;document&quot;: [r[&quot;DOC&quot;] for r in docs],
        &quot;prompts&quot;: [r[&quot;P&quot;] for r in prompts] if prompts else [],
        &quot;audits&quot;: [r[&quot;A&quot;] for r in audits] if audits else [],
        &quot;certs&quot;: [r[&quot;C&quot;] for r in certs] if certs else []
    }

    for part_name, part_vals in parts.items():
        if not part_vals:
            continue
        item_id = &quot;item-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        item_path = f&quot;{manifest_id}/{part_name}/{item_id}.json&quot;
        payload = json.dumps(part_vals, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;))
        # Insert evidence bundle item
        session.sql(
            &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_ITEMS(ITEM_ID, MANIFEST_ID, PART_NAME, ITEM_PATH, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT, CREATED_BY) VALUES(%s, %s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), %s)&quot;,
            (item_id, manifest_id, part_name, item_path, payload, created_by)
        ).collect()
        items.append({&quot;item_id&quot;: item_id, &quot;part&quot;: part_name, &quot;item_path&quot;: item_path})

    # Insert manifest row
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES(MANIFEST_ID, DOCUMENT_ID, ORG_ID, STATUS, METADATA, CREATED_AT, CREATED_BY) VALUES(%s, %s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), %s)&quot;,
        (manifest_id, document_id, None, &quot;STAGED&quot;, json.dumps({&quot;items&quot;: items, &quot;created_at&quot;: now_iso}), created_by)
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;manifest_id&quot;: manifest_id, &quot;items&quot;: items}

474 — assemble_evidence_bundle.pyDescription: Snowpark Python handler variant for ASSEMBLE_EVIDENCE_BUNDLE registration (slightly different shape; returns staged manifest metadata). This is the handler referenced around the compliance/archive implementations.Code:
# assemble_evidence_bundle.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from datetime import datetime

def assemble_evidence_bundle(session: Session, manifest_id: str, org_id: str, document_ids: list, created_by: str) -&gt; dict:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Handler invoked by registered Stored Procedure to assemble an evidence bundle (multiple documents).
    - document_ids: list of document_id strings
    - Writes a manifest and per-document evidence item rows into EVIDENCE_BUNDLES and EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_ITEMS
    - Returns the manifest_id and a summary
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    now = datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    manifest_row_id = manifest_id or (&quot;man-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])

    all_items = []
    for doc_id in document_ids:
        # fetch document and related prompts/audits
        doc = session.sql(&quot;SELECT OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(*) AS doc FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT WHERE DOCUMENT_ID = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, (doc_id,)).collect()
        prompts = session.sql(&quot;SELECT ARRAY_AGG(OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(*)) AS prompts FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.PROMPT_LOG WHERE RELATED_DOCUMENT_ID = %s&quot;, (doc_id,)).collect()
        audits = session.sql(&quot;SELECT ARRAY_AGG(OBJECT_CONSTRUCT(*)) AS audits FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.AUDIT_LOG WHERE ENTITY_ID = %s&quot;, (doc_id,)).collect()

        item_id = &quot;item-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        payload_obj = {
            &quot;document&quot;: doc[0][&quot;DOC&quot;] if doc else None,
            &quot;prompts&quot;: prompts[0][&quot;PROMPTS&quot;] if prompts and prompts[0][&quot;PROMPTS&quot;] else [],
            &quot;audits&quot;: audits[0][&quot;AUDITS&quot;] if audits and audits[0][&quot;AUDITS&quot;] else []
        }
        item_path = f&quot;{manifest_row_id}/{doc_id}/{item_id}.json&quot;
        session.sql(
            &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_ITEMS(ITEM_ID, MANIFEST_ID, PART_NAME, ITEM_PATH, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT, CREATED_BY) VALUES(%s,%s,%s,%s,PARSE_JSON(%s),CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(),%s)&quot;,
            (item_id, manifest_row_id, &quot;document_bundle&quot;, item_path, json.dumps(payload_obj), created_by)
        ).collect()
        all_items.append({&quot;doc_id&quot;: doc_id, &quot;item_id&quot;: item_id, &quot;path&quot;: item_path})

    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES(MANIFEST_ID, ORG_ID, STATUS, METADATA, CREATED_AT, CREATED_BY) VALUES(%s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), %s)&quot;,
        (manifest_row_id, org_id, &quot;STAGED&quot;, json.dumps({&quot;items&quot;: all_items, &quot;created_at&quot;: now}), created_by)
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;manifest_id&quot;: manifest_row_id, &quot;items&quot;: all_items}

477 — agg_model_metrics.pyDescription: Aggregates raw MODEL_TELEMETRY rows into daily aggregates and writes to MODEL_TELEMETRY_DAILY. Intended to be run as a task/cron job.Code:
# agg_model_metrics.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json

def aggregate_model_metrics(session: Session, lookback_days: int = 7) -&gt; dict:
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    # Aggregate average latency and token consumption per model per day
    q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.MODEL_TELEMETRY_DAILY (AGG_DATE, MODEL_ID, AVG_LATENCY_MS, TOTAL_TOKENS_IN, TOTAL_TOKENS_OUT, SAMPLE_COUNT, CREATED_AT)
    SELECT DATE_TRUNC(&#39;DAY&#39;, CREATED_AT) AS agg_date,
           MODEL_ID,
           AVG(LATENCY_MS) AS avg_latency_ms,
           SUM(TOKENS_IN) AS total_tokens_in,
           SUM(TOKENS_OUT) AS total_tokens_out,
           COUNT(*) AS sample_count,
           CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.MODEL_TELEMETRY
    WHERE CREATED_AT &gt;= DATEADD(day, -{lookback_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    GROUP BY 1,2
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(q).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;lookback_days&quot;: lookback_days}

478 — run_embedding_quality_checks.pyDescription: Scans embeddings for coverage/quality signals (dimensionality, variance) and writes quality results. Consolidated from the &quot;run embedding quality checks&quot; handlers referenced in the file manifests.Code:
# run_embedding_quality_checks.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
import math

def run_embedding_quality_checks(session: Session, expected_dim: int = 768, sample_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; dict:
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    q = f&quot;SELECT EMBEDDING_ID, DOCUMENT_ID, EMBEDDING FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS LIMIT {sample_limit}&quot;
    rows = session.sql(q).collect()
    issues = []
    for r in rows:
        emb = r[&quot;EMBEDDING&quot;]
        eid = r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;]
        try:
            if not isinstance(emb, list):
                issues.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: eid, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;not_array&quot;})
                continue
            if len(emb) != expected_dim:
                issues.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: eid, &quot;reason&quot;: f&quot;dim_mismatch_{len(emb)}&quot;})
                continue
            # basic numeric checks
            if any(v is None or (isinstance(v, float) and (math.isnan(v) or math.isinf(v))) for v in emb):
                issues.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: eid, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;bad_numeric&quot;})
        except Exception as ex:
            issues.append({&quot;embedding_id&quot;: eid, &quot;reason&quot;: f&quot;inspect_failed_{str(ex)}&quot;})
    summary_id = &quot;ev-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_QUALITY_SUMMARY(SUMMARY_ID, ISSUE_COUNT, DETAILS, CREATED_AT) VALUES(%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
        (summary_id, len(issues), json.dumps({&quot;issues&quot;: issues}, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;)))
    ).collect()
    return {&quot;summary_id&quot;: summary_id, &quot;issues_count&quot;: len(issues), &quot;issues_sample&quot;: issues[:10]}


483 — submit_and_approve_rate_rules.pyDescription: Submit rate-rule JSON into a staging table, approve to merge into live RATE_RULES table, or reject. Consolidated handler for staging/approve workflow.Code:
# submit_and_approve_rate_rules.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def submit_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_json: str, submitted_by: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Insert a staging row for a rate rule. Returns staging id.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    stg_id = &quot;stg-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
    # store staging JSON as VARIANT
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING(STAGING_ID, STAGING_JSON, STATUS, CREATED_AT, CREATED_BY) VALUES (%s, PARSE_JSON(%s), &#39;PENDING&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), %s)&quot;,
        (stg_id, staging_json, submitted_by)
    ).collect()
    return {&quot;staging_id&quot;: stg_id}

def approve_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_id: str, approved_by: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Approve a staged rate rule: MERGE into RATE_RULES and mark staging as APPROVED with audit.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    # fetch staging JSON
    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT STAGING_JSON FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING WHERE STAGING_ID = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, (staging_id,)).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: &quot;staging_not_found&quot;}
    rule = rows[0][&quot;STAGING_JSON&quot;]
    rule_id = rule.get(&quot;rule_id&quot;) or (&quot;rule-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])
    # Merge into live table
    session.sql(
        &quot;MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES tgt USING (SELECT %s AS RULE_ID, PARSE_JSON(%s) AS PAYLOAD) src ON tgt.RULE_ID = src.RULE_ID WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET PAYLOAD = src.PAYLOAD, UPDATED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (RULE_ID, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT) VALUES (src.RULE_ID, src.PAYLOAD, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
        (rule_id, json.dumps(rule))
    ).collect()
    # mark staging row approved + audit
    session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING SET STATUS = &#39;APPROVED&#39;, APPROVED_BY = %s, APPROVED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE STAGING_ID = %s&quot;, (approved_by, staging_id)).collect()
    session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_AUDIT(AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, CREATED_AT) VALUES (%s, %s, &#39;APPROVE&#39;, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, (&quot;aud-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], staging_id, approved_by)).collect()
    return {&quot;approved_rule_id&quot;: rule_id, &quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id}

from typing import Dict, Any, Tuple

def gen_staging_id() -&gt; str:
