def validate_rate_rule_json(j: str) -&gt; Tuple[bool, Dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        obj = json.loads(j)
    except Exception as e:
        return False, {&quot;error&quot;: &quot;invalid_json&quot;, &quot;msg&quot;: str(e)}
    # minimal validation: must have a rules list or rule_id + actions
    if not isinstance(obj, dict) or (&quot;rules&quot; not in obj and &quot;rule_id&quot; not in obj):
        return False, {&quot;error&quot;: &quot;invalid_shape&quot;}
    return True, obj



493 — usageingestsp.pyDescription: Idempotent usage ingestion Snowpark stored-proc: validates input, writes to TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE and ingestion audit. Consolidated from ingestion SP artifacts in the inventoryCode:
# usageingestsp.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def usage_ingest_sp(session: Session, payload_variant) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    payload_variant expected to be VARIANT/JSON with keys: org_id, account_id, feature_key, units, event_ts
    Idempotently writes into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE using an ingestion_id derived from payload if present.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    try:
        if isinstance(payload_variant, str):
            payload = json.loads(payload_variant)
        else:
            payload = payload_variant
    except Exception:
        return {&quot;error&quot;: &quot;invalid_payload&quot;}

    ingestion_id = payload.get(&quot;ingestion_id&quot;) or (&quot;ing-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12])
    org_id = payload.get(&quot;org_id&quot;)
    account_id = payload.get(&quot;account_id&quot;)
    feature_key = payload.get(&quot;feature_key&quot;)
    units = float(payload.get(&quot;units&quot;, 0))
    event_ts = payload.get(&quot;event_ts&quot;)

    session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
        USING (SELECT %s AS INGESTION_ID) src
        ON tgt.INGESTION_ID = src.INGESTION_ID
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
          INSERT (INGESTION_ID, ORG_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY, UNITS, USAGE_TIMESTAMP, CREATED_AT)
          VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (ingestion_id, ingestion_id, org_id, account_id, feature_key, units, event_ts)
    ).collect()

    # Audit
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_AUDIT(AUDIT_ID, INGESTION_ID, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT) VALUES (%s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,
        (&quot;aud-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12], ingestion_id, json.dumps(payload))
    ).collect()

    return {&quot;ingestion_id&quot;: ingestion_id}


494 — writeevidencebundle.pyDescription: Export and archive evidence bundles to a stage, create archive manifest and item rows. Consolidated from the evidence bundle exporter artifact in the inventoryCode:
# writeevidencebundle.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from typing import Dict, Any

def write_evidence_bundle(session: Session, manifest_id: str, org_id: str, items: list, target_stage_path: str) -&gt; Dict[str, Any]:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Given manifest_id and a list of items (each {document_id, content_base64, filename}), upload to stage path and record manifest/items.
    This implementation assumes caller takes care of binary-&gt;stage PUT using available Snowpark/container mechanism; here we record metadata.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    session.sql(
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCGEN_ARCHIVE_MANIFEST tgt
        USING (SELECT %s AS MANIFEST_ID) src
        ON tgt.MANIFEST_ID = src.MANIFEST_ID
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
          INSERT (MANIFEST_ID, ORG_ID, PACKAGE_PATH, EXPORT_STATUS, CREATED_AT)
          VALUES (%s, %s, %s, &#39;STAGED&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        WHEN MATCHED THEN
          UPDATE SET PACKAGE_PATH = %s, EXPORT_STATUS = &#39;STAGED&#39;, UPDATED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (manifest_id, manifest_id, org_id, target_stage_path, target_stage_path)
    ).collect()

    inserted = []
    for it in items or []:
        item_id = &quot;item-&quot; + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]
        document_id = it.get(&quot;document_id&quot;)
        filename = it.get(&quot;filename&quot;) or f&quot;{document_id}.pdf&quot;
        item_path = f&quot;{target_stage_path.rstrip(&#39;/&#39;)}/{org_id}/{filename}&quot;

        session.sql(
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_ITEMS(ITEM_ID, MANIFEST_ID, ORG_ID, DOCUMENT_ID, ITEM_TYPE, ITEM_PATH, CREATED_AT)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
            &quot;&quot;&quot;,
            (item_id, manifest_id, org_id, document_id, &quot;PDF&quot;, item_path)
        ).collect()
        inserted.append({&quot;item_id&quot;: item_id, &quot;document_id&quot;: document_id, &quot;item_path&quot;: item_path})

    return {&quot;manifest_id&quot;: manifest_id, &quot;items_written&quot;: len(inserted)}


495 — similarity_service.pyDescription: FAISS-based similarity service (FastAPI skeleton) used in PoC; loader/lookup helpers included. Consolidated from FAISS PoC artifacts inventoryCode:
# similarity_service.py
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
import faiss
import numpy as np
import json
from typing import List

app = FastAPI()

# In-memory placeholder for loaded index snapshot
INDEX = None
ID_MAP = []

class QueryReq(BaseModel):
    vectors: List[List[float]]
    top_k: int = 5

@app.post(&quot;/load_index&quot;)
def load_index_from_path(path: str):
    global INDEX, ID_MAP
    try:
        INDEX = faiss.read_index(path)
        # ID_MAP should be loaded from sidecar metadata; here we assume trivial mapping
        ID_MAP = list(range(INDEX.ntotal))
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;loaded&quot;, &quot;n&quot;: INDEX.ntotal}
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=str(e))

@app.post(&quot;/query&quot;)
def query(req: QueryReq):
    global INDEX, ID_MAP
    if INDEX is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=&quot;index_not_loaded&quot;)
    np_q = np.array(req.vectors).astype(&#39;float32&#39;)
    D, I = INDEX.search(np_q, req.top_k)
    results = []
    for qi in range(len(req.vectors)):
        hits = []
        for idx_pos, iid in enumerate(I[qi].tolist()):
            if iid &lt; 0:
                continue
            hits.append({&quot;id&quot;: ID_MAP[iid], &quot;score&quot;: float(D[qi][idx_pos])})
        results.append({&quot;query&quot;: qi, &quot;hits&quot;: hits})
    return {&quot;results&quot;: results}



497 — ingest_embedding_with_provenance.pyDescription: Validate vector schema, record vector with provenance metadata into DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS or DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS_V2; idempotent by embedding_id. Consolidated from the ingestion handler references in the registration streamCode:

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
from typing import Dict, Any

def ingest_embedding(session: Session, embedding_id: str, org_id: str, document_id: str, section_id: str, model_id: str, vector: list, provenance: Dict[str, Any]) -&gt; str:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Basic validation + idempotent insert into DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS_V2.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)

    # Minimal validation
    if not isinstance(vector, list) or len(vector) == 0:
        return &quot;invalid_vector&quot;

    # Idempotent MERGE on embedding_id

        USING (SELECT %s AS EMBEDDING_ID) src

          UPDATE SET ORG_ID = %s, DOCUMENT_ID = %s, SECTION_ID = %s, MODEL_ID = %s, VECTOR = PARSE_JSON(%s), PROVENANCE = PARSE_JSON(%s), UPDATED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
          INSERT (EMBEDDING_ID, ORG_ID, DOCUMENT_ID, SECTION_ID, MODEL_ID, VECTOR, PROVENANCE, CREATED_AT)
          VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;,
        (embedding_id, org_id, document_id, section_id, model_id, json.dumps(vector), json.dumps(provenance),
         embedding_id, org_id, document_id, section_id, model_id, json.dumps(vector), json.dumps(provenance))
    ).collect()

    return &quot;OK&quot;

530 — faiss/index_loader_prod.pyDescription: Production FAISS index shard builder — downloads JSONL/.faiss inputs from S3, builds shard(s), writes index and id-map back to S3, and emits a manifest.json describing shards.Code:
# faiss/index_loader_prod.py
import os
import json
import tempfile
import logging
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
import boto3
import faiss
import numpy as np
from typing import List, Dict, Any

logger = logging.getLogger(&quot;faiss.index_loader&quot;)
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)

s3 = boto3.client(&quot;s3&quot;)

def parse_s3_uri(uri: str):
    assert uri.startswith(&quot;s3://&quot;)
    _, rest = uri.split(&quot;s3://&quot;, 1)
    bucket, key = rest.split(&quot;/&quot;, 1)
    return bucket, key

def download_to_local(s3_uri: str, local_path: str):
    b, k = parse_s3_uri(s3_uri)
    s3.download_file(b, k, local_path)
    return local_path

def read_jsonl_vectors(path: str):
    vecs = []
    ids = []
    with open(path, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as f:
        for line in f:
            doc = json.loads(line)
            ids.append(doc[&quot;id&quot;])
            vecs.append(doc[&quot;vector&quot;])
    return ids, np.array(vecs, dtype=&quot;float32&quot;)

def build_index_from_vectors(vectors: np.ndarray, index_factory: str = &quot;Flat&quot;):
    # Simple flat index; change to IVFFlat/IVFPQ for large data
    d = vectors.shape[1]
    if index_factory.lower() == &quot;flat&quot;:
        ix = faiss.IndexFlatL2(d)
    else:
        ix = faiss.index_factory(d, index_factory)
    ix.add(vectors)
    return ix

def upload_file(local_path: str, s3_uri: str):
    b, k = parse_s3_uri(s3_uri)
    s3.upload_file(local_path, b, k)
    return s3_uri

def build_shard(s3_inputs: List[str], output_prefix: str, index_name: str, index_factory: str = &quot;Flat&quot;):
    tmpdir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
    all_ids = []
    all_vecs = []
    # download and read
    for uri in s3_inputs:
        local = os.path.join(tmpdir, os.path.basename(uri))
        download_to_local(uri, local)
        ids, vecs = read_jsonl_vectors(local)
        all_ids.extend(ids)
        all_vecs.append(vecs)
    if not all_vecs:
        raise RuntimeError(&quot;No vectors found&quot;)
    vectors = np.vstack(all_vecs)
    index = build_index_from_vectors(vectors, index_factory=index_factory)
    idx_local = os.path.join(tmpdir, f&quot;{index_name}.faiss&quot;)
    faiss.write_index(index, idx_local)
    idmap_local = os.path.join(tmpdir, f&quot;{index_name}.idmap.json&quot;)
    with open(idmap_local, &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as f:
        json.dump(all_ids, f)
    # upload
    s3_index_uri = os.path.join(output_prefix, f&quot;{index_name}.faiss&quot;)
    s3_idmap_uri = os.path.join(output_prefix, f&quot;{index_name}.idmap.json&quot;)
    upload_file(idx_local, s3_index_uri)
    upload_file(idmap_local, s3_idmap_uri)
    return {&quot;index_uri&quot;: s3_index_uri, &quot;idmap_uri&quot;: s3_idmap_uri, &quot;num_vectors&quot;: len(all_ids)}

def create_manifest(shards: List[Dict[str, Any]], manifest_uri: str):
    manifest = {&quot;shards&quot;: shards, &quot;generated_at&quot;: None}
    manifest[&quot;generated_at&quot;] = json.dumps({&quot;ts&quot;: None})
    tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False, suffix=&quot;.json&quot;)
    tmp.write(json.dumps(shards).encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;))
    tmp.flush()
    tmp.close()
    upload_file(tmp.name, manifest_uri)
    return manifest_uri

def main(inputs_per_shard: List[List[str]], output_prefix: str, manifest_uri: str, index_factory: str = &quot;Flat&quot;):
    shards_meta = []
    for i, s3_list in enumerate(inputs_per_shard):
        name = f&quot;shard_{i:03d}&quot;
        meta = build_shard(s3_list, output_prefix, name, index_factory=index_factory)
        shards_meta.append(meta)
    create_manifest(shards_meta, manifest_uri)
    return shards_meta

if __name__ == &quot;__main__&quot;:
    # simple CLI glue — environment variables expected in production orchestration
    pass

531 — faiss/service/app.pyDescription: FastAPI-based FAISS query service that loads manifest, downloads shards, loads FAISS indexes and id-maps, and serves /search (kNN) requests across shards.Code:
# faiss/service/app.py
import os
import json
import tempfile
import logging
from typing import List, Dict, Any
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
import boto3
import faiss
import numpy as np
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

logger = logging.getLogger(&quot;faiss.service&quot;)
logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
s3 = boto3.client(&quot;s3&quot;)

app = FastAPI(title=&quot;FAISS Query Service&quot;)

class QueryRequest(BaseModel):
    vector: List[float]
    k: int = 10

shards = []

def parse_s3_uri(uri: str):
    assert uri.startswith(&quot;s3://&quot;)
    _, rest = uri.split(&quot;s3://&quot;, 1)
    bucket, key = rest.split(&quot;/&quot;, 1)
    return bucket, key

def download_to_local(s3_uri: str):
    b, k = parse_s3_uri(s3_uri)
    tmp = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False)
    s3.download_file(b, k, tmp.name)
    return tmp.name

def load_index_and_idmap(index_uri: str, idmap_uri: str):
    idx_local = download_to_local(index_uri)
    id_local = download_to_local(idmap_uri)
    index = faiss.read_index(idx_local)
    with open(id_local, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as f:
        idmap = json.load(f)
    return {&quot;index&quot;: index, &quot;idmap&quot;: idmap}

def load_manifest(manifest_uri: str):
    mlocal = download_to_local(manifest_uri)
    with open(mlocal, &quot;r&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf-8&quot;) as f:
        manifest = json.load(f)
    return manifest

@app.on_event(&quot;startup&quot;)
def startup():
    manifest_uri = os.environ.get(&quot;FAISS_INITIAL_MANIFEST_URI&quot;)
    if not manifest_uri:
        logger.warning(&quot;No FAISS_INITIAL_MANIFEST_URI&quot;)
        return
    manifest = load_manifest(manifest_uri)
    # manifest expected: list of dicts with index_uri and idmap_uri
    for shard in manifest:
        entry = load_index_and_idmap(shard[&quot;index_uri&quot;], shard[&quot;idmap_uri&quot;])
        shards.append(entry)
    logger.info(f&quot;Loaded {len(shards)} shards&quot;)

@app.post(&quot;/search&quot;)
def search(req: QueryRequest):
    if not shards:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=&quot;No shards loaded&quot;)
    qvec = np.array(req.vector, dtype=&quot;float32&quot;).reshape(1, -1)
    results = []
    for s in shards:
        D, I = s[&quot;index&quot;].search(qvec, req.k)
        idmap = s[&quot;idmap&quot;]
        hits = []
        for dist, idx in zip(D[0], I[0]):
            if idx &lt; 0:
                continue
            hits.append({&quot;id&quot;: idmap[idx], &quot;score&quot;: float(dist)})
        results.extend(hits)
    # simple cross-shard top-k
    results = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x[&quot;score&quot;])[: req.k]
    return {&quot;hits&quot;: results}

532 — src/run/billing.py (Snowpark Python stored-proc — consolidated)Description: Snowpark Python stored-proc implementing billing run (preview and final). Idempotent, writes billing_runs and invoice_lines.Code:
# src/run/billing.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session, functions as F
import json, uuid
def run_billing(session: Session, run_id: str = None, preview: bool = True, tenant_scope: str = None):
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    if not run_id:
        run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    # Example: aggregate usage_events_detailed grouped by org and feature
    q = session.table(&quot;USAGE_EVENTS_DETAILED&quot;)
    if tenant_scope:
        q = q.filter(F.col(&quot;ORG_ID&quot;) == tenant_scope)
    aggregates = q.group_by(&quot;ORG_ID&quot;).agg(
        F.sum(&quot;COST&quot;).as_(&quot;total_cost&quot;),
        F.count(&quot;USAGE_ID&quot;).as_(&quot;event_count&quot;)
    ).collect()
    # write billing_run record
    session.table(&quot;BILLING_RUNS&quot;).insert([
        {&quot;RUN_ID&quot;: run_id, &quot;PREVIEW&quot;: preview, &quot;STATUS&quot;: &quot;COMPLETED&quot; if preview else &quot;FINALIZED&quot;}
    ])
    # write invoice lines
    for row in aggregates:
        session.table(&quot;INVOICE_LINES&quot;).insert([
            {
                &quot;LINE_ID&quot;: str(uuid.uuid4()),
                &quot;RUN_ID&quot;: run_id,
                &quot;ORG_ID&quot;: row[&quot;ORG_ID&quot;],
                &quot;AMOUNT&quot;: float(row[&quot;TOTAL_COST&quot;]),
                &quot;METADATA&quot;: None
            }
        ])
    return {&quot;run_id&quot;: run_id, &quot;preview&quot;: preview, &quot;rows&quot;: len(aggregates)}

def retry_failed_ingests(session: Session, max_rows: int = 100):
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    rows = session.sql(
        &quot;SELECT metadata:original_file::STRING AS file FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.USAGE_RAW WHERE metadata:status = &#39;FAILED&#39; LIMIT %s&quot; % max_rows
    ).collect()

def log_inference(session: Session, request_payload: dict):

    prov_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    # Expect request_payload to include: request_id, model_id, input, output, tokens, confidence, ms
    session.sql(

            request_payload.get(&quot;tokens&quot;, 0),
            request_payload.get(&quot;confidence&quot;, None),
            request_payload.get(&quot;ms&quot;, None)
        )
    ).collect()

# sql/ops/300b_028_key_rotate_min.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, hashlib

def rotate_api_key(session: Session, org_id: str):
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    new_key = str(uuid.uuid4())
    key_hash = hashlib.sha256(new_key.encode()).hexdigest()
    session.sql(
        &quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.API_KEYS(KEY_ID, ORG_ID, KEY_HASH, CREATED_AT) VALUES (%s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;,

    ).collect()
    # Caller must deliver new_key securely to consumer
    return {&quot;key_id&quot;: new_key}

RETURNS VARIANT
LANGUAGE SQL
AS $$
INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ALERTS(ALERT_ID, NAME, DETAILS, LEVEL, CREATED_AT)
VALUES (alert_id, name, parse_json(details), level, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
$$;

572 — sql/ops/322d_015_proc_cost_optimization_advisor.py (Cost optimization advisor)Description: Analyze warehouse metering and platform signals to recommend rightsizing or QAS enablement.Code:
# sql/ops/322d_015_proc_cost_optimization_advisor.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def analyze_costs(session: Session):
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    recs = []
    rows = session.sql(
        &quot;SELECT WAREHOUSE_NAME, SUM(CREDITS_USED) AS CREDITS FROM SNOWFLAKE.ACCOUNT_USAGE.WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY &quot;
        &quot;WHERE START_TIME &gt;= DATEADD(day, -30, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()) GROUP BY WAREHOUSE_NAME ORDER BY CREDITS DESC LIMIT 5&quot;
    ).collect()
    if rows and rows[0][&quot;CREDITS&quot;] &gt; 1000:
        recs.append(f&quot;Review top warehouse {rows[0][&#39;WAREHOUSE_NAME&#39;]} for rightsizing&quot;)
    qas_count = session.sql(&quot;SELECT COUNT(*) AS CNT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.V_QAS_ELIGIBILITY WHERE ELIGIBLE = TRUE&quot;).collect()
    if qas_count and qas_count[0][&quot;CNT&quot;] &gt; 100:
        recs.append(&quot;Consider enabling Query Acceleration Service on candidate warehouses&quot;)
    return {&quot;recommendations&quot;: recs}

575 — sql/ops/322e_018_proc_reconcile_dynamic_tasks.py (Reconcile dynamic tasks)Description: Snowpark procedure to reconcile &quot;DYNAMIC_TASKS&quot; configuration table with actual Snowflake tasks (create/resume as needed).Code:
# sql/ops/322e_018_proc_reconcile_dynamic_tasks.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def reconcile_tasks(session: Session):
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    config_rows = session.table(&quot;DYNAMIC_TASKS&quot;).collect()
    existing = {r[&quot;NAME&quot;] for r in session.sql(&quot;SHOW TASKS IN SCHEMA AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;).collect()}
    for cfg in config_rows:
        tname = cfg[&quot;TASK_NAME&quot;]
        if tname not in existing:
            # Build basic CREATE TASK statement - config must provide SQL_TEXT and WAREHOUSE
            session.sql(
                f&quot;CREATE TASK {tname} WAREHOUSE = &#39;{cfg[&#39;WAREHOUSE&#39;]}&#39; SCHEDULE = &#39;{cfg[&#39;SCHEDULE&#39;]}&#39; AS {cfg[&#39;SQL_TEXT&#39;]}&quot;
            ).collect()
        if cfg.get(&quot;IS_ENABLED&quot;):
            session.sql(f&quot;ALTER TASK {tname} RESUME&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;reconciled&quot;: True}

576 — tests/testdeterministicbilling.py (Pytest for deterministic billing)Description: Unit test used in CI to validate deterministic invoice hashing/outputs (consolidated test).Code:
# tests/testdeterministicbilling.py
import pytest
import hashlib, json

def deterministic_invoice_hash(invoice):
    s = json.dumps(invoice, sort_keys=True).encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;)
    return hashlib.sha256(s).hexdigest()

def test_invoice_hash_stability():
    invoice = {&quot;invoice_id&quot;: &quot;inv-1&quot;, &quot;lines&quot;: [{&quot;amt&quot;: 10}, {&quot;amt&quot;: 20}], &quot;meta&quot;: {&quot;period&quot;: &quot;2025-01&quot;}}
    h1 = deterministic_invoice_hash(invoice)
    h2 = deterministic_invoice_hash(invoice)
    assert h1 == h2

from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def batch_forward_prov(session: Session, prov_list: list, batch_size: int = 100):
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    for i in range(0, len(prov_list), batch_size):

        # Call stored proc or insert in batch; simple loop here for portability
        for p in batch:
            session.call(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.FORWARD_PROVENANCE_SP&quot;, p)
    return {&quot;batches&quot;: (len(prov_list) + batch_size - 1) // batch_size}

579 — sql/ops/300b_017_model_p95_notifier.py (Model p95 notifier action)Description: Evaluates model p95 and inserts ALERT via INSERT_ALERT proc when threshold breached.Code:
# sql/ops/300b_017_model_p95_notifier.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, uuid

def notify_model_p95(session: Session, threshold_ms: int = 2000):
    session.use_schema(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB&quot;)
    rows = session.sql(
        &quot;SELECT MODEL_ID, PERCENTILE_CONT(0.95) WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY PROCESSING_TIME_MS) AS P95 &quot;
        &quot;FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.AI_PROCESSING_LOG GROUP BY MODEL_ID HAVING P95 &gt; %s&quot; % threshold_ms
    ).collect()
    alerts = []
    for r in rows:
        aid = str(uuid.uuid4())
        details = json.dumps({&quot;model_id&quot;: r[&quot;MODEL_ID&quot;], &quot;p95&quot;: r[&quot;P95&quot;], &quot;threshold_ms&quot;: threshold_ms})
        # call the SQL INSERT_ALERT procedure
        session.call(&quot;AI_FEATURE_HUB.INSERT_ALERT&quot;, aid, f&quot;Model {r[&#39;MODEL_ID&#39;]} p95 breach&quot;, details, &quot;WARN&quot;)
        alerts.append({&quot;alert_id&quot;: aid, &quot;model_id&quot;: r[&quot;MODEL_ID&quot;], &quot;p95&quot;: r[&quot;P95&quot;]})
    return {&quot;alerts_created&quot;: alerts}

# run_embedding_quality_checks.py
# Snowpark stored-proc to run quality checks on recently ingested embeddings:
# - sample embeddings per model/index
# - compute avg cosine similarity within sample (for collapse/dup detection)
# - compute coverage (percent docs with embeddings)
# - persist results to ANN_EMBEDDING_QUALITY_HISTORY
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import numpy as np
import json, uuid

def _cosine(a, b):
    a = np.array(a, dtype=float)
    b = np.array(b, dtype=float)
    na = np.linalg.norm(a)
    nb = np.linalg.norm(b)
    if na == 0 or nb == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.dot(a, b) / (na * nb))

def run_embedding_quality_checks(session: Session, index_snapshot_id: str, sample_size: int = 100) -&gt; dict:
    # sample embeddings for this snapshot
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT SECTION_ID, EMBEDDING, MODEL_ID
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS
        WHERE INDEX_SNAPSHOT_ID = &#39;{index_snapshot_id}&#39;
        SAMPLE ({sample_size})
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;no_data&quot;, &quot;index_snapshot_id&quot;: index_snapshot_id}

    embeddings = [r[&#39;EMBEDDING&#39;] for r in rows]
    # compute pairwise average cosine similarity on a random subset (bounded)
    n = min(len(embeddings), 50)
    sims = []
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i+1, n):
            sims.append(_cosine(embeddings[i], embeddings[j]))
    avg_sim = float(np.mean(sims)) if sims else 0.0

    # compute coverage: percent of sections in snapshot with embeddings
    total_sections_row = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT COUNT(*) AS CNT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SNAPSHOT_SECTIONS WHERE INDEX_SNAPSHOT_ID = &#39;{index_snapshot_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    total_sections = int(total_sections_row[0][&#39;CNT&#39;]) if total_sections_row else 0
    embedded_sections_row = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT SECTION_ID) AS CNT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS WHERE INDEX_SNAPSHOT_ID = &#39;{index_snapshot_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    embedded_sections = int(embedded_sections_row[0][&#39;CNT&#39;]) if embedded_sections_row else 0
    coverage = (embedded_sections / total_sections) if total_sections &gt; 0 else None

    # persist results
    run_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    details = json.dumps({&quot;avg_cosine&quot;: avg_sim, &quot;sample_count&quot;: len(embeddings), &quot;coverage&quot;: coverage})
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ANN_EMBEDDING_QUALITY_HISTORY (RUN_ID, INDEX_SNAPSHOT_ID, AVG_COSINE, SAMPLE_COUNT, COVERAGE, DETAILS, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{run_id}&#39;, &#39;{index_snapshot_id}&#39;, {avg_sim}, {len(embeddings)}, {(&#39;NULL&#39; if coverage is None else coverage)}, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{details.replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;run_id&quot;: run_id, &quot;avg_cosine&quot;: avg_sim, &quot;coverage&quot;: coverage}

# upsert_producer_config.py
# Idempotent upsert of producer configuration for ingestion pipelines.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json

def upsert_producer_config(session: Session, producer_id: str, config: dict, enabled: bool = True) -&gt; dict:
    cfg = json.dumps(config).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.PRODUCER_CONFIG tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{producer_id}&#39; AS PRODUCER_ID) src
        ON tgt.PRODUCER_ID = src.PRODUCER_ID
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET CONFIG = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{cfg}&#39;), ENABLED = {&#39;TRUE&#39; if enabled else &#39;FALSE&#39;}, UPDATED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (PRODUCER_ID, CONFIG, ENABLED, CREATED_AT)
            VALUES (&#39;{producer_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{cfg}&#39;), {&#39;TRUE&#39; if enabled else &#39;FALSE&#39;}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;producer_id&quot;: producer_id}

# purge_old_embeddings.py
# Removes embeddings older than retention threshold, records deletions to embedding_deletion_audit.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def purge_old_embeddings(session: Session, older_than_days: int, batch_limit: int = 1000) -&gt; dict:
    cutoff = f&quot;DATEADD(day, -{older_than_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    # select ids to delete
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT IDEMPOTENCY_KEY, DOC_ID, SECTION_ID
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS
        WHERE CREATED_AT &lt; {cutoff}
        LIMIT {batch_limit}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;deleted&quot;: 0}
    deleted = 0
    for r in rows:
        key = r[&#39;IDEMPOTENCY_KEY&#39;]
        doc = r[&#39;DOC_ID&#39;]
        sec = r[&#39;SECTION_ID&#39;]
        audit_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS WHERE IDEMPOTENCY_KEY = &#39;{key}&#39;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_DELETION_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, IDEMPOTENCY_KEY, DOC_ID, SECTION_ID, DELETED_AT)
            VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{key}&#39;, &#39;{doc}&#39;, &#39;{sec}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
        deleted += 1
    return {&quot;deleted&quot;: deleted}

# record_embedding_deletion.py
# Records a manual deletion event for auditing (does not remove embedding).
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json

def record_embedding_deletion(session: Session, idep_key: str, deleted_by: str, reason: str = None) -&gt; dict:
    audit_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    details = {&quot;reason&quot;: reason} if reason else {}
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_DELETION_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, IDEMPOTENCY_KEY, DELETED_BY, DETAILS, DELETED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{idep_key}&#39;, &#39;{deleted_by}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(details).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;audit_id&quot;: audit_id}

# report_producer_backlog.py
# Reports the current backlog per producer (unprocessed messages / lag).
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def report_producer_backlog(session: Session, producer_id: str = None) -&gt; dict:
    where = f&quot;WHERE PRODUCER_ID = &#39;{producer_id}&#39;&quot; if producer_id else &quot;&quot;
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT PRODUCER_ID, COUNT(*) AS PENDING_COUNT
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.PRODUCER_INBOX
        {where}
        GROUP BY PRODUCER_ID
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    result = [{&quot;producer_id&quot;: r[&#39;PRODUCER_ID&#39;], &quot;pending&quot;: int(r[&#39;PENDING_COUNT&#39;])} for r in rows]
    return {&quot;backlog&quot;: result}

# compute_quality_metrics.py
# Aggregates multiple quality metrics (embedding quality, query success, latency) into a daily rollup.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def compute_quality_metrics(session: Session, day_ts: str) -&gt; dict:
    # day_ts expected ISO date string (YYYY-MM-DD)
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT
            AVG(AVG_COSINE) AS AVG_COSINE,
            AVG(P95_LATENCY_MS) AS AVG_P95_LATENCY,
            SUM(CASE WHEN IS_SUCCESS THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) / NULLIF(COUNT(*),0) AS SUCCESS_RATE
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DAILY_METRICS_SOURCE
        WHERE METRIC_DAY = TO_DATE(&#39;{day_ts}&#39;)
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;no_data&quot;}
    r = rows[0]
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.QUALITY_METRICS_DAILY (METRIC_DAY, AVG_COSINE, AVG_P95_LATENCY_MS, SUCCESS_RATE, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (TO_DATE(&#39;{day_ts}&#39;), {r[&#39;AVG_COSINE&#39;]}, {r[&#39;AVG_P95_LATENCY&#39;]}, {r[&#39;SUCCESS_RATE&#39;]}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;day&quot;: day_ts}

# compute_ingest_error_summary.py
# Scans ingest error logs and produces a summary (counts by error type, top failing producers).
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def compute_ingest_error_summary(session: Session, window_hours: int = 24) -&gt; dict:
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT ERROR_CODE, COUNT(*) AS CNT
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERROR_LOG
        WHERE CREATED_AT &gt;= DATEADD(hour, -{window_hours}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        GROUP BY ERROR_CODE
        ORDER BY CNT DESC
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    summary = [{&quot;error_code&quot;: r[&#39;ERROR_CODE&#39;], &quot;count&quot;: int(r[&#39;CNT&#39;])} for r in rows]
    top_producers = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT PRODUCER_ID, COUNT(*) AS CNT
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERROR_LOG
        WHERE CREATED_AT &gt;= DATEADD(hour, -{window_hours}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        GROUP BY PRODUCER_ID
        ORDER BY CNT DESC
        LIMIT 10
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    producers = [{&quot;producer_id&quot;: r[&#39;PRODUCER_ID&#39;], &quot;count&quot;: int(r[&#39;CNT&#39;])} for r in top_producers]
    return {&quot;summary&quot;: summary, &quot;top_producers&quot;: producers}

# ingest_embedding_with_provenance.py
# Higher-level ingest that accepts document text, extracts embeddings (via external model call), and writes embedding + provenance.
# For consolidation we accept embedding vector as input (Snowpark SP should not call external network in many deployments).
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json, hashlib

def ingest_embedding_with_provenance(session: Session, doc_id: str, section_id: str, embedding: list, model_id: str, provenance: dict = None) -&gt; dict:
    idempotency_key = hashlib.sha256(f&quot;{doc_id}:{section_id}:{model_id}&quot;.encode()).hexdigest()
    emb_json = json.dumps(embedding).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    prov_json = json.dumps(provenance or {}).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS t
        USING (SELECT &#39;{idempotency_key}&#39; AS IDEMP, &#39;{doc_id}&#39; AS DOC_ID, &#39;{section_id}&#39; AS SECTION_ID, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{emb_json}&#39;) AS EMB, &#39;{model_id}&#39; AS MODEL_ID) s
        ON t.IDEMPOTENCY_KEY = s.IDEMP
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET EMBEDDING = s.EMB, MODEL_ID = s.MODEL_ID, UPDATED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (IDEMPOTENCY_KEY, DOC_ID, SECTION_ID, EMBEDDING, MODEL_ID, CREATED_AT)
            VALUES (s.IDEMP, s.DOC_ID, s.SECTION_ID, s.EMB, s.MODEL_ID, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    prov_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_METADATA (PROV_ID, IDEMPOTENCY_KEY, DOC_ID, SECTION_ID, MODEL_ID, PROVENANCE_PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{prov_id}&#39;, &#39;{idempotency_key}&#39;, &#39;{doc_id}&#39;, &#39;{section_id}&#39;, &#39;{model_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{prov_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;idempotency_key&quot;: idempotency_key, &quot;prov_id&quot;: prov_id}

# ingest_section_embedding.py
# Convenience wrapper to ingest multiple section embeddings in one call.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def ingest_section_embedding(session: Session, items: list) -&gt; dict:
    # items: list of dicts {doc_id, section_id, embedding, model_id, provenance}
    results = []
    for it in items:
        res = ingest_embedding_with_provenance(session, it[&#39;doc_id&#39;], it[&#39;section_id&#39;], it[&#39;embedding&#39;], it[&#39;model_id&#39;], it.get(&#39;provenance&#39;))
        results.append(res)
    return {&quot;ingested&quot;: len(results), &quot;results&quot;: results}

# get_or_set_snippet_cache.py
# Simple key/value snippet cache using SNIPPET_CACHE table (get or set with TTL).
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, datetime

def get_or_set_snippet_cache(session: Session, key: str, fetcher_callable=None, ttl_seconds: int = 3600) -&gt; dict:
    row = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT VALUE, CREATED_AT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.SNIPPET_CACHE WHERE KEY = &#39;{key}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
    if row:
        created_at = row[0][&#39;CREATED_AT&#39;]
        # TTL check in SQL time; returned as Snowflake TIMESTAMP, but here we rely on DB-side TTL cleanup. Return cached value.
        return {&quot;cached&quot;: True, &quot;value&quot;: row[0][&#39;VALUE&#39;]}
    if not fetcher_callable:
        return {&quot;cached&quot;: False, &quot;value&quot;: None}
    value = fetcher_callable()
    val_json = json.dumps(value).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SNIPPET_CACHE (KEY, VALUE, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{key}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{val_json}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;cached&quot;: False, &quot;value&quot;: value}

# assemble_evidence_bundle.py
# Builds and returns a structured evidence bundle from document ids; writes bundle record and optional archive to stage path.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json

def assemble_evidence_bundle(session: Session, bundle_name: str, document_ids: list, created_by: str, archive_stage: str = None) -&gt; dict:
    bundle_id = f&quot;eb-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    payload = {&quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id, &quot;documents&quot;: document_ids, &quot;created_by&quot;: created_by}
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLE (BUNDLE_ID, NAME, DOCUMENT_IDS, CREATED_BY, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, &#39;{bundle_name}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(document_ids).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), &#39;{created_by}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # Optionally write to stage as JSON file (if archive_stage provided)
    if archive_stage:
        # For simplicity, write bundle payload to a named stage via PUT is infra-specific; here we create a file record
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_ARCHIVE (BUNDLE_ID, STAGE_NAME, PAYLOAD, ARCHIVED_AT)
            VALUES (&#39;{bundle_id}&#39;, &#39;{archive_stage}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(payload).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id, &quot;name&quot;: bundle_name}

# run_folder_context_analysis.py
# Runs context analysis across a folder (e.g., compute topic vectors / term aggregates) and stores results for assistant context retrieval.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json

def run_folder_context_analysis(session: Session, folder_id: str) -&gt; dict:
    # Example: aggregate sections in folder and compute simple term frequency map
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT SECTION_ID, TEXT
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_SECTIONS
        WHERE FOLDER_ID = &#39;{folder_id}&#39;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;no_sections&quot;}
    term_counts = {}
    for r in rows:
        text = (r[&#39;TEXT&#39;] or &quot;&quot;).lower()
        for tok in text.split():
            term_counts[tok] = term_counts.get(tok, 0) + 1
    analysis_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.FOLDER_CONTEXT_ANALYSIS (ANALYSIS_ID, FOLDER_ID, METADATA, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{analysis_id}&#39;, &#39;{folder_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps({&quot;term_counts&quot;: term_counts}).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;analysis_id&quot;: analysis_id, &quot;terms&quot;: len(term_counts)}

# check_quota_and_reserve.py
# Checks tenant quota for a feature and optionally reserve units atomically.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def check_quota_and_reserve(session: Session, org_id: str, feature_key: str, units: float = 1.0, reserve: bool = False) -&gt; dict:
    r = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT QUOTA_LIMIT, COALESCE(QUOTA_USED,0) AS QUOTA_USED
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.FEATURE_ENTITLEMENTS
        WHERE ORG_ID = &#39;{org_id}&#39; AND FEATURE_KEY = &#39;{feature_key}&#39;
        LIMIT 1
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    if not r:
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;no_entitlement&quot;}
    quota = float(r[0][&#39;QUOTA_LIMIT&#39;]) if r[0][&#39;QUOTA_LIMIT&#39;] is not None else None
    used = float(r[0][&#39;QUOTA_USED&#39;])
    if quota is not None and used + units &gt; quota:
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;quota_exceeded&quot;, &quot;quota&quot;: quota, &quot;used&quot;: used}
    if reserve:
        # atomic increment via MERGE or UPDATE
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.FEATURE_ENTITLEMENTS tgt
            USING (SELECT &#39;{org_id}&#39; AS ORG_ID, &#39;{feature_key}&#39; AS FEATURE_KEY) src
            ON tgt.ORG_ID = src.ORG_ID AND tgt.FEATURE_KEY = src.FEATURE_KEY
            WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET QUOTA_USED = COALESCE(QUOTA_USED,0) + {units}, UPDATED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;allowed&quot;: True, &quot;quota&quot;: quota, &quot;used&quot;: used + (units if reserve else 0)}

# compute_ingest_error_summary_alt.py
# Alternate summarization that writes a snapshot to a reporting table.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def compute_ingest_error_summary_alt(session: Session, window_hours: int = 24) -&gt; dict:
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        CREATE OR REPLACE TABLE AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERROR_SUMMARY AS
        SELECT ERROR_CODE, COUNT(*) AS COUNT, MAX(LAST_SEEN) AS LAST_SEEN
        FROM (
            SELECT ERROR_CODE, CREATED_AT AS LAST_SEEN
            FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERROR_LOG
            WHERE CREATED_AT &gt;= DATEADD(hour, -{window_hours}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        )
        GROUP BY ERROR_CODE;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.INGEST_ERROR_SUMMARY&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;rows&quot;: len(rows)}

# run_producer_backlog_report.py
# Runs a scheduled detailed backlog report and writes to REPORTS table.
from snowflake.snowpark import Session, functions as F
import json, uuid

def run_producer_backlog_report(session: Session) -&gt; dict:
    rows = session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT p.PRODUCER_ID, COALESCE(SUM(p.BACKLOG),0) AS BACKLOG
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.PRODUCER_METRICS p
        GROUP BY p.PRODUCER_ID
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    report_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    payload = [{&quot;producer_id&quot;: r[&#39;PRODUCER_ID&#39;], &quot;backlog&quot;: int(r[&#39;BACKLOG&#39;])} for r in rows]
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SYSTEM_REPORTS (REPORT_ID, REPORT_TYPE, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{report_id}&#39;, &#39;producer_backlog&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(payload).replace(&quot;&#39;&quot;, &quot;&#39;&#39;&quot;)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;report_id&quot;: report_id, &quot;rows&quot;: len(payload)}


I analyzed the provided files and consolidated the Python artifacts that had full, mergeable implementations in the materials you supplied. Below are the merged, working Python artifacts (first batch). Each code block is drawn from and attributed to the intermediate materials listed after the block.

run_billing_enhanced (consolidated Snowpark Python stored-procedure)

# run_billing_enhanced.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import hashlib
import json
import uuid
from typing import Optional

def run_billing_enhanced(session: Session, window_start: str, window_end: str,
                         account_id: Optional[str] = None, dry_run: bool = True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Generate invoice preview or persist invoices.
    - window_start, window_end: ISO timestamps or Snowflake-compatible timestamp strings
    - account_id: optional org/account filter
    - dry_run: if True, returns preview JSON without persisting
    Returns: dict with invoice lines and totals
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Build query filtering by time window and optional account
    account_clause = f&quot; AND u.account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39;&quot; if account_id else &quot;&quot;
    q = (
        &quot;SELECT u.account_id, u.feature_key, SUM(u.units) AS units, &quot;
        &quot;MAX(u.unit_price) AS unit_price &quot;
        &quot;FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE u &quot;
        f&quot;WHERE u.usage_timestamp BETWEEN to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_start}&#39;) AND to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_end}&#39;)&quot;
        + account_clause +
        &quot; GROUP BY u.account_id, u.feature_key&quot;
    )

    rows = session.sql(q).collect()

    invoices = []
    for r in rows:
        acct = r[&#39;ACCOUNT_ID&#39;]
        fk = r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]
        units = float(r[&#39;UNITS&#39;] or 0)
        unit_price = float(r[&#39;UNIT_PRICE&#39;] or 0)
        line_total = round(units * unit_price, 6)
        invoices.append({
            &#39;account_id&#39;: acct,
            &#39;feature_key&#39;: fk,
            &#39;units&#39;: units,
            &#39;unit_price&#39;: unit_price,
            &#39;line_total&#39;: line_total
        })

    subtotal = round(sum([i[&#39;line_total&#39;] for i in invoices]), 6)
    # Example markup/tax logic (can be replaced by pricing engine)
    markup = round(subtotal * 0.085, 2)
    tax = round((subtotal + markup) * 0.0, 2)
    total = round(subtotal + markup + tax, 2)
    invoice_hash = hashlib.sha256((str(uuid.uuid4()) + str(total)).encode()).hexdigest()

    out = {
        &#39;lines&#39;: invoices,
        &#39;subtotal&#39;: subtotal,
        &#39;markup&#39;: markup,
        &#39;tax&#39;: tax,
        &#39;total&#39;: total,
        &#39;invoice_hash&#39;: invoice_hash,
        &#39;dry_run&#39;: dry_run
    }

    if not dry_run:
        # Idempotent invoice header insert by invoice_hash
        inv_account = account_id or &#39;MULTI&#39;
        session.sql(
            f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES t
            USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS invoice_id) s
            ON t.invoice_id = s.invoice_id
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (
                invoice_id, account_id, period_start, period_end, subtotal, markup, tax, total, created_at
            ) VALUES (
                &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{inv_account}&#39;, to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_start}&#39;),
                to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_end}&#39;), {subtotal}, {markup}, {tax}, {total}, current_timestamp()
            );
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
        ).collect()

        # Insert/merge line items idempotently
        for ln in invoices:
            session.sql(
                f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM t
                USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS billing_run_id, &#39;{ln[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39; AS feature_key) s
                ON t.billing_run_id = s.billing_run_id AND t.feature_key = s.feature_key
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (
                    billing_run_id, invoice_id, account_id, feature_key, usage_qty, unit_price, line_total, created_at
                ) VALUES (
                    &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{ln[&#39;account_id&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{ln[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39;, {ln[&#39;units&#39;]},
                    {ln[&#39;unit_price&#39;]}, {ln[&#39;line_total&#39;]}, current_timestamp()
                );
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
            ).collect()

        # Write audit log
        session.sql(
            f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RUN_AUDIT (
                RUN_ID, INVOICE_HASH, ACCOUNT_ID, PREVIEW_JSON, CREATED_AT
            ) VALUES (
                &#39;run-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&#39;, &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{inv_account}&#39;,
                PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(out)}&#39;), current_timestamp()
            );
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
        ).collect()

    return out



persist_billing_runs (persist previews into invoices)

# persist_billing_runs.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid
from typing import List

def persist_billing_runs(session: Session, older_than_minutes: int = 60, preview_only: bool = True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Persist billing previews older than `older_than_minutes` into final invoice tables.
    - If preview_only True: does not insert final invoice rows, returns count of previews considered.
    - If preview_only False: inserts invoices and line items idempotently.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    q = (
        &quot;SELECT PREVIEW_ID, PREVIEW_JSON FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEW &quot;
        f&quot;WHERE PREVIEW_TS &lt;= DATEADD(minute, -{older_than_minutes}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    )
    rows = session.sql(q).collect()
    persisted: List[str] = []

    for r in rows:
        preview_id = r[&#39;PREVIEW_ID&#39;]
        preview_json = r[&#39;PREVIEW_JSON&#39;]
        data = json.loads(preview_json) if isinstance(preview_json, (str, bytes)) else preview_json

        if not preview_only:
            # Example persistence - insert invoice header + line items idempotently
            invoice_hash = data.get(&#39;invoice_hash&#39;) or f&quot;inv-{uuid.uuid4().hex}&quot;
            account = data.get(&#39;account_id&#39;, &#39;MULTI&#39;)
            subtotal = data.get(&#39;subtotal&#39;, 0)
            markup = data.get(&#39;markup&#39;, 0)
            tax = data.get(&#39;tax&#39;, 0)
            total = data.get(&#39;total&#39;, 0)

            session.sql(
                f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES t
                USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS invoice_id) s
                ON t.invoice_id = s.invoice_id
                WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (
                    invoice_id, account_id, period_start, period_end, subtotal, markup, tax, total, created_at
                ) VALUES (
                    &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{account}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), {subtotal}, {markup}, {tax}, {total}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                );
                &quot;&quot;&quot;
            ).collect()

            for ln in data.get(&#39;lines&#39;, []):
                session.sql(
                    f&quot;&quot;&quot;
                    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM t
                    USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS billing_run_id, &#39;{ln.get(&#39;feature_key&#39;)}&#39; AS feature_key) s
                    ON t.billing_run_id = s.billing_run_id AND t.feature_key = s.feature_key
                    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (
                        billing_run_id, invoice_id, account_id, feature_key, usage_qty, unit_price, line_total, created_at
                    ) VALUES (
                        &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, &#39;{ln.get(&#39;account_id&#39;, account)}&#39;, &#39;{ln.get(&#39;feature_key&#39;)}&#39;,
                        {ln.get(&#39;units&#39;, 0)}, {ln.get(&#39;unit_price&#39;, 0)}, {ln.get(&#39;line_total&#39;, 0)}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
                    );
                    &quot;&quot;&quot;
                ).collect()

        persisted.append(preview_id)

    return {&#39;persisted_count&#39;: len(persisted), &#39;preview_only&#39;: preview_only}



reconcile_billing_vs_usage (reconciliation between usage and billed line items)

# reconcile_billing_vs_usage.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid

def reconcile_billing_vs_usage(session: Session, account_id: str = None, window_start: str = None, window_end: str = None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Compare usage aggregates against billed amounts and record mismatches into RECONCILIATION_AUDIT.
    - account_id optional filter
    - window_start/window_end optional timestamp strings
    Returns: dict with number of issues recorded
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    conds = []
    if account_id:
        conds.append(f&quot;account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39;&quot;)
    if window_start and window_end:
        conds.append(f&quot;usage_timestamp BETWEEN to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_start}&#39;) AND to_timestamp_ltz(&#39;{window_end}&#39;)&quot;)
    where = &#39; AND &#39;.join(conds) if conds else &#39;1=1&#39;

    usage_q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(units) AS units
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE
        WHERE {where}
        GROUP BY account_id, feature_key
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    billing_q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(line_total) AS billed
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM
        WHERE {where}
        GROUP BY account_id, feature_key
    &quot;&quot;&quot;

    usage_rows = session.sql(usage_q).collect()
    billing_rows = session.sql(billing_q).collect()

    u_map = {(r[&#39;ACCOUNT_ID&#39;], r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]): float(r[&#39;UNITS&#39;] or 0) for r in usage_rows}
    b_map = {(r[&#39;ACCOUNT_ID&#39;], r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]): float(r[&#39;BILLED&#39;] or 0) for r in billing_rows}

    issues = []
    keys = set(list(u_map.keys()) + list(b_map.keys()))
    for k in keys:
        u = u_map.get(k, 0.0)
        b = b_map.get(k, 0.0)
        if abs(u - b) &gt; 0.01:
            issues.append({
                &#39;account_id&#39;: k[0],
                &#39;feature_key&#39;: k[1],
                &#39;usage&#39;: u,
                &#39;billed&#39;: b,
                &#39;diff&#39;: u - b
            })

    for i in issues:
        audit_id = f&quot;rec-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
        session.sql(
            f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RECONCILIATION_AUDIT(
                AUDIT_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, FEATURE_KEY, USAGE_SUM, BILL_SUM, MISMATCH, RUN_AT
            ) VALUES (
                &#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{i[&#39;account_id&#39;]}&#39;, &#39;{i[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39;, {i[&#39;usage&#39;]}, {i[&#39;billed&#39;]}, {i[&#39;diff&#39;]}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
            );
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
        ).collect()

    return {&#39;issues_count&#39;: len(issues)}



pricing_engine helpers (apply_tiers, apply_discount)

# pricing_engine.py
from typing import List, Dict, Optional

def apply_tiers(amount: float, tiers: List[Dict]) -&gt; float:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    tiers: list of dicts with keys:
      - &#39;upto&#39;: optional numeric upper bound for the tier (None for infinity)
      - &#39;unit_price&#39;: price per unit within this tier
    Returns total cost applying tiered pricing to `amount`.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    remaining = amount
    cost = 0.0
    prev = 0.0
    for t in tiers:
        upto = t.get(&#39;upto&#39;)
        price = float(t[&#39;unit_price&#39;])
        if upto is None:
            qty = remaining
        else:
            qty = max(0.0, min(remaining, float(upto) - prev))
        cost += qty * price
        prev = float(upto) if upto is not None else prev
        remaining -= qty
        if remaining &lt;= 0:
            break
    return round(cost, 6)

def apply_discount(subtotal: float, percent: Optional[float] = 0.0, flat: Optional[float] = 0.0) -&gt; float:
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Apply percentage and flat discounts to subtotal.
    percent: e.g., 10.0 for 10%
    flat: flat amount to subtract
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    percent = float(percent or 0.0)
    flat = float(flat or 0.0)
    discounted = max(0.0, subtotal * (1.0 - percent / 100.0) - flat)
    return round(discounted, 6)



approve_rate_rule / reject_rate_rule (consolidated approval handlers)

# rate_rule_approval.py
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json
import uuid

def approve_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_id: str, approver: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Approve a staged rate rule and upsert into active RATE_RULES table.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # Fetch staging row
    rows = session.sql(
        f&quot;SELECT FEATURE_KEY, RULE_TYPE, CONFIG, PRIORITY FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING &quot;
        f&quot;WHERE STAGING_ID = &#39;{staging_id}&#39; AND (APPROVED = FALSE OR APPROVED IS NULL) AND (REJECTED = FALSE OR REJECTED IS NULL)&quot;
    ).collect()

    if not rows:
        return {&#39;status&#39;: &#39;not_found_or_already_processed&#39;, &#39;staging_id&#39;: staging_id}

    r = rows[0].as_dict()
    rule_id = f&quot;rule-{r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]}-{r[&#39;RULE_TYPE&#39;]}-{staging_id}&quot;

    # Upsert into RATE_RULES
    cfg = json.dumps(r[&#39;CONFIG&#39;]) if r.get(&#39;CONFIG&#39;) is not None else &#39;null&#39;
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES t
        USING (SELECT &#39;{rule_id}&#39; AS rule_id, &#39;{r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]}&#39; AS feature_key, &#39;{r[&#39;RULE_TYPE&#39;]}&#39; AS rule_type, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{cfg}&#39;) AS config, {r.get(&#39;PRIORITY&#39;, 100)} AS priority) s
        ON t.rule_id = s.rule_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET
            feature_key = s.feature_key,
            rule_type = s.rule_type,
            config = s.config,
            priority = s.priority,
            active = TRUE,
            effective_from = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (
            rule_id, feature_key, rule_type, config, priority, effective_from, active, created_at
        ) VALUES (
            s.rule_id, s.feature_key, s.rule_type, s.config, s.priority, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), TRUE, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        );
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    # Mark staging row approved
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING
        SET APPROVED = TRUE, APPROVED_BY = &#39;{approver}&#39;, APPROVED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHERE STAGING_ID = &#39;{staging_id}&#39;;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    # Audit
    audit_id = f&quot;audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (
            AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, ACTION_TS
        ) VALUES (
            &#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{staging_id}&#39;, &#39;APPROVE&#39;, &#39;{approver}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        );
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    return {&#39;status&#39;: &#39;approved&#39;, &#39;staging_id&#39;: staging_id, &#39;rule_id&#39;: rule_id}

def reject_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_id: str, approver: str, reason: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Reject a staged rate rule with reason; mark staging row and create audit entry.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING
        SET REJECTED = TRUE, REJECT_REASON = PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps({&#39;reason&#39;: reason})}&#39;),
            APPROVED_BY = &#39;{approver}&#39;, APPROVED_AT = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHERE STAGING_ID = &#39;{staging_id}&#39;;
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    audit_id = f&quot;audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    session.sql(
        f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (
            AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, ACTION_TS, DETAILS
        ) VALUES (
            &#39;{audit_id}&#39;, &#39;{staging_id}&#39;, &#39;REJECT&#39;, &#39;{approver}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps({&#39;reason&#39;: reason})}&#39;)
        );
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ).collect()

    return {&#39;status&#39;: &#39;rejected&#39;, &#39;staging_id&#39;: staging_id}



reject_rate_rule.py — mark staging row rejected and log reason to audit.

import jsonfrom snowflake.snowpark import Session
def handler(session: Session, staging_id: str, approver: str, reason: str = None):    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rules_staging WHERE staging_id = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, [staging_id]).collect()    if not rows:        return json.dumps({&quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;not_found&quot;})    session.sql(&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rules_staging SET rejected = TRUE, rejected_by = %s, rejected_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), rejection_reason = PARSE_JSON(%s) WHERE staging_id = %s&quot;, [approver, json.dumps({&quot;reason&quot;: reason}), staging_id]).collect()    session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rules_approval_audit(audit_id, staging_id, action, actor, details, acted_at) VALUES (SYSTEM$UUID(), %s, &#39;REJECT&#39;, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, [staging_id, approver, json.dumps({&quot;reason&quot;: reason})]).collect()    return json.dumps({&quot;staging_id&quot;: staging_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;rejected&quot;})


advanced_pricing_admin.upsert_rule (function) — used by Streamlit admin and by approval flows; performs MERGE into RATE_RULES and ensures metadata fields are set.

def upsert_rule(session: Session, rule_id: str = None, feature_key: str = None, rule_type: str = None, config: dict = None, priority: int = 100, active: bool = True, created_by: str = None):    &quot;&quot;&quot;    If rule_id provided, update; otherwise insert new rule_id.    Returns rule_id.    &quot;&quot;&quot;    rule_id = rule_id or str(uuid.uuid4())    session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rules tgt    USING (SELECT %s AS rule_id) src    ON tgt.rule_id = src.rule_id    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET feature_key = %s, rule_type = %s, config = PARSE_JSON(%s), priority = %s, active = %s, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (rule_id, feature_key, rule_type, config, priority, active, created_by, created_at)      VALUES (%s, %s, %s, PARSE_JSON(%s), %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())    &quot;&quot;&quot;, [rule_id, feature_key, rule_type, json.dumps(config or {}), priority, active,         rule_id, feature_key, rule_type, json.dumps(config or {}), priority, active, created_by]    ).collect()    return rule_id

billing_invoice_hash_util.py — small util to produce deterministic invoice_hash (shared across billing code).

import hashlibimport json
def invoice_hash(payload: dict) -&gt; str:    s = json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(&quot;,&quot;, &quot;:&quot;), default=str)    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;)).hexdigest()
Used by run_billing_enhanced for idempotency

reconciliation_report_exporter.py — exports reconciliation audit results to a JSON payload or stage for downstream reporting.

def export_reconciliation(session: Session, window_start: str, window_end: str, stage_path: str = None):    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.reconciliation_audit WHERE window_start = TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s) AND window_end = TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s)&quot;, [window_start, window_end]).collect()    out = [dict(r) for r in rows]    payload = json.dumps(out, default=str)    if stage_path:        session.file.put_stream(payload.encode(&quot;utf-8&quot;), f&quot;@{stage_path}/reconciliation_{window_start}_{window_end}.json.gz&quot;, auto_compress=True)    return payload


pricing_rule_evaluator.py — loads active RATE_RULES, evaluates rules against usage rows to compute effective price per unit. Supports tier, percentage and flat rules.

from decimal import Decimal
def evaluate_rate_rules(rules: list, usage_units: Decimal) -&gt; Decimal:    &quot;&quot;&quot;    rules: list of dicts with types like {&quot;rule_type&quot;: &quot;tiered&quot;, &quot;config&quot;: {...}}    returns unit_price Decimal    &quot;&quot;&quot;    # naive priority-ordered evaluation: pick highest priority active rule and compute unit price    if not rules:        return Decimal(&quot;1.0&quot;)    sorted_rules = sorted(rules, key=lambda r: r.get(&quot;priority&quot;, 100))    for r in sorted_rules:        rt = r.get(&quot;rule_type&quot;)        cfg = r.get(&quot;config&quot;) or {}        if rt == &quot;fixed&quot;:            return Decimal(cfg.get(&quot;unit_price&quot;, &quot;1.0&quot;))        if rt == &quot;tiered&quot;:            tiers = cfg.get(&quot;tiers&quot;, [])            # convert tiers into structure expected by pricing_engine.apply_tiers            return apply_tiers(usage_units, tiers)        if rt == &quot;discount&quot;:            base = Decimal(cfg.get(&quot;base_price&quot;, &quot;1.0&quot;))            pct = Decimal(cfg.get(&quot;percent&quot;, &quot;0&quot;))            return (base * (Decimal(1) - (pct / Decimal(100))))    return Decimal(&quot;1.0&quot;)


rate_rule_template_manager.py — helpers for template CRUD operations (list, get, upsert, delete) used by Streamlit.

def list_templates(session: Session):    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT template_id, name, template FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rule_templates ORDER BY created_at DESC&quot;).collect()    return [dict(r) for r in rows]
def get_template(session: Session, template_id: str):    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT template_id, name, template FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.rate_rule_templates WHERE template_id = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, [template_id]).collect()    return dict(rows[0]) if rows else None

def upsert_rule_via_call(conn, feature_key: str, rule_type: str, config_json: str, priority: int, created_by: str):    cur = conn.cursor()    try:        cur.execute(&quot;CALL AI_FEATURE_HUB.upsert_rule(%s,%s,%s,%s,%s)&quot;, [None, feature_key, rule_type, config_json, priority])    finally:        cur.close()

pricing_rule_backfill.py — job to backfill historical usage with new pricing rules (applies pricing_rule_evaluator) and writes adjusted billing_preview rows for review.

def backfill_pricing(session: Session, window_start: str, window_end: str, target_rules: list):    # fetch usage, evaluate new price per usage row and insert into billing_preview for audit    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT tenant_id, account_id, feature_key, SUM(units) AS units FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.tenant_feature_usage WHERE window_start &gt;= TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s) AND window_end &lt;= TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s) GROUP BY tenant_id, account_id, feature_key&quot;, [window_start, window_end]).collect()    previews = []    for r in rows:        units = Decimal(r[&quot;UNITS&quot;] or 0)        unit_price = evaluate_rate_rules(target_rules, units)        line_total = (units * unit_price).quantize(Decimal(&quot;0.000001&quot;))        previews.append({&quot;tenant_id&quot;: r[&quot;TENANT_ID&quot;], &quot;account_id&quot;: r[&quot;ACCOUNT_ID&quot;], &quot;feature_key&quot;: r[&quot;FEATURE_KEY&quot;], &quot;units&quot;: str(units), &quot;unit_price&quot;: str(unit_price), &quot;line_total&quot;: str(line_total)})    # write a single preview JSON    preview_json = json.dumps({&quot;window_start&quot;: window_start, &quot;window_end&quot;: window_end, &quot;lines&quot;: previews}, default=str)    session.sql(&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.billing_preview(preview_id, preview_json, created_at) VALUES (SYSTEM$UUID(), PARSE_JSON(%s), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;, [preview_json]).collect()    return {&quot;preview_rows&quot;: len(previews)}



evaluate_assembly_validations.py — Snowpark Python handler that evaluates assembly-level validation rules and returns found issues



def handler(session, assembly_json: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    assembly_json: JSON string representing an assembly with metadata and parts
    Returns: dict with assembly_id and issues list
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    import json, uuid

    payload = json.loads(assembly_json)
    issues = []

    # Example checks (extendable)
    if not payload.get(&quot;assembly_id&quot;):
        issues.append({&quot;id&quot;: str(uuid.uuid4()), &quot;severity&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;msg&quot;: &quot;missing assembly_id&quot;})

    for idx, part in enumerate(payload.get(&quot;parts&quot;, [])):
        if not part.get(&quot;type&quot;):
            issues.append({&quot;id&quot;: str(uuid.uuid4()), &quot;severity&quot;: &quot;WARN&quot;, &quot;msg&quot;: f&quot;part[{idx}] missing type&quot;})
        if part.get(&quot;type&quot;) and part.get(&quot;type&quot;) == &quot;document&quot;:
            # sample check: document must have title
            if not part.get(&quot;name&quot;):
                issues.append({&quot;id&quot;: str(uuid.uuid4()), &quot;severity&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;msg&quot;: f&quot;part[{idx}] missing name&quot;})

    # Example rule predicates (example)
    rules = payload.get(&quot;validation_rules&quot;, [])
    for r in rules:
        # example: rule checks non_empty_name on the last part if specified
        if r.get(&quot;check&quot;) == &quot;non_empty_name&quot;:
            for idx, part in enumerate(payload.get(&quot;parts&quot;, [])):
                if not part.get(&quot;name&quot;):
                    issues.append({&quot;id&quot;: str(uuid.uuid4()), &quot;severity&quot;: &quot;ERROR&quot;, &quot;msg&quot;: f&quot;part[{idx}] empty name&quot;, &quot;rule&quot;: r.get(&quot;id&quot;)})

    return {&quot;assembly_id&quot;: payload.get(&quot;assembly_id&quot;), &quot;issues&quot;: issues}



rollup_semantic_index_stats.py — archive old semantic_index_stats to semantic_index_stats_hist and delete hot rows beyond retention



def handler(session, org_id: str = None, archive_older_than_days: int = 30, batch_size: int = 1000):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Archives rows older than archive_older_than_days from
    AI_FEATURE_HUB.semantic_index_stats into
    AI_FEATURE_HUB.semantic_index_stats_hist then deletes them.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    where_clause = f&quot;WHERE org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot; if org_id else &quot;&quot;
    # Insert into history
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.semantic_index_stats_hist
        SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.semantic_index_stats
        {where_clause}
        AND created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{archive_older_than_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # Delete archived rows
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.semantic_index_stats
        WHERE created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{archive_older_than_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        {(&#39;AND org_id = &#39; + repr(org_id)) if org_id else &#39;&#39;}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;archived_older_than_days&quot;: archive_older_than_days}



update_retention_policy_and_audit.py — upsert retention policy for an org and write an audit record



def handler(session, org_id: str, embedding_days: int, provenance_days: int, changed_by: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Upserts retention policy into AI_FEATURE_HUB.retention_policies
    and writes an audit row into retention_policy_audit.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    import json

    session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.retention_policies tgt
        USING (SELECT %s AS org_id) src
        ON tgt.org_id = src.org_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET
            embedding_days = %s,
            provenance_days = %s,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (org_id, embedding_days, provenance_days, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;, [org_id, embedding_days, provenance_days, org_id, embedding_days, provenance_days]).collect()

    details = {&quot;embedding_days&quot;: embedding_days, &quot;provenance_days&quot;: provenance_days}
    session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;

upsert_producer_config.py — MERGE-based upsert for producer_config (embedding producers)



def handler(session, producer_id: str, org_id: str, embedding_model_id: str, batch_size: int):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Upserts AI_FEATURE_HUB.producer_config for an embedding producer.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.producer_config tgt
        USING (SELECT %s AS producer_id) src
        ON tgt.producer_id = src.producer_id
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET
            embedding_model_id = %s,
            batch_size = %s,
            updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT
            (producer_id, org_id, embedding_model_id, batch_size, created_at)
            VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;, [producer_id, embedding_model_id, batch_size, producer_id, org_id, embedding_model_id, batch_size]).collect()
    return {&quot;producer_id&quot;: producer_id, &quot;org_id&quot;: org_id}



purge_old_embeddings.py — delete document_embeddings older than retention and log deletion counts



def handler(session, retention_days: int = 90, org_id: str = None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Deletes embeddings older than retention_days and logs deletion into embedding_deletion_audit.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    where_org = f&quot;AND org_id = &#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot; if org_id else &quot;&quot;
    cnt_row = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT COUNT(1) AS c
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings
        WHERE created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{retention_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        {where_org}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    cnt = cnt_row[0][&quot;C&quot;] if cnt_row else 0

    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_embeddings
        WHERE created_at &lt; DATEADD(day, -{retention_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        {where_org}
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()

    session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_deletion_audit
        (audit_id, org_id, retention_days, deleted_count, acted_at)
        VALUES (SYSTEM$UUID(), %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;, [org_id, retention_days, cnt]).collect()

    return {&quot;deleted_count&quot;: cnt}

compute_quality_metrics.py — aggregate annotation-derived quality metrics and write metrics



def handler(session, window_start: str = None, window_end: str = None):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregates annotation labels into embedding quality metrics for a time window.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    cond = &quot;&quot;
    params = []
    if window_start and window_end:
        cond = &quot;WHERE created_at BETWEEN TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s) AND TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(%s)&quot;
        params = [window_start, window_end]

    rows = session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT org_id, embedding_id,
               SUM(CASE WHEN label=&#39;false_positive&#39; THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS fp_count
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.document_index_annotations
        {cond}
        GROUP BY org_id, embedding_id
    &quot;&quot;&quot;, params).collect()

    for r in rows:
        session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.embedding_quality_metrics
            (metric_id, org_id, embedding_id, false_positive_count, recorded_at)
            VALUES (SYSTEM$UUID(), %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;, [r[&quot;ORG_ID&quot;], r[&quot;EMBEDDING_ID&quot;], r[&quot;FP_COUNT&quot;]]).collect()

    return {&quot;metrics_written&quot;: len(rows)}

enforce_ann_rate_limit.py — check QPS against ann_rate_limits and write backpressure events



def handler(session, org_id: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Naive QPS check for ANN queries in last minute and write backpressure events.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    limit_row = session.sql(&quot;SELECT qps_limit FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_rate_limits WHERE org_id = %s LIMIT 1&quot;, [org_id]).collect()
    if not limit_row:
        return {&quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;no_limit&quot;}

    limit_val = limit_row[0][&quot;QPS_LIMIT&quot;]
    recent = session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT COUNT(1) AS c
        FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.similarity_query_logs
        WHERE org_id = %s AND created_at &gt;= DATEADD(minute, -1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;, [org_id]).collect()[0][&quot;C&quot;]

    if recent &gt; limit_val:
        session.sql(&quot;&quot;&quot;
            INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_backpressure_events
            (event_id, org_id, qps, limit_val, created_at)
            VALUES (SYSTEM$UUID(), %s, %s, %s, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
        &quot;&quot;&quot;, [org_id, recent, limit_val]).collect()
        return {&quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;backpressure&quot;: True, &quot;qps&quot;: recent, &quot;limit&quot;: limit_val}

    return {&quot;org_id&quot;: org_id, &quot;backpressure&quot;: False, &quot;qps&quot;: recent, &quot;limit&quot;: limit_val}
