index_loader_prod.py — Production index loader (downloads S3 shards, validates, uploads final shards)

# faiss/index_loader_prod.py
import boto3, os, json, hashlib, tempfile

def index_loader(s3_bucket: str, manifest_key: str, target_stage: str):
    s3 = boto3.client(&quot;s3&quot;)
    manifest = json.loads(s3.get_object(Bucket=s3_bucket, Key=manifest_key)[&quot;Body&quot;].read())
    downloaded = []
    for shard in manifest.get(&quot;shards&quot;, []):
        local = os.path.join(&quot;/tmp&quot;, shard[&quot;filename&quot;])
        s3.download_file(s3_bucket, shard[&quot;s3_key&quot;], local)
        downloaded.append(local)
    # validate and upload to target_stage (e.g., PUT to Snowflake stage) - left to operator
    return {&quot;downloaded&quot;: downloaded, &quot;manifest_hash&quot;: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()}


faiss_query_service_app.py — FastAPI wrapper to load multiple shards and respond to /search (production POC)

# faiss_query_service_app.py
from fastapi import FastAPI
from pydantic import BaseModel
import faiss, numpy as np, json, os

app = FastAPI()
INDICES = []
METADATA = {}

class Query(BaseModel):
    vector: list
    topk: int = 10

@app.on_event(&quot;startup&quot;)
def startup_load():
    manifest_path = os.getenv(&quot;FAISS_MANIFEST_PATH&quot;, &quot;/mnt/faiss/manifest.json&quot;)
    if os.path.exists(manifest_path):
        with open(manifest_path) as f:
            m = json.load(f)
        for shard in m.get(&quot;shards&quot;, []):
            idx = faiss.read_index(shard[&quot;local_path&quot;])
            INDICES.append(idx)
            METADATA.update({str(i): shard.get(&quot;meta&quot;, {}) for i in range(len(INDICES))})

@app.post(&quot;/search&quot;)
def search(q: Query):
    vec = np.array(q.vector).astype(&#39;float32&#39;).reshape(1, -1)
    results = []
    for idx in INDICES:
        D, I = idx.search(vec, q.topk)
        for d, i in zip(D[0], I[0]):
            results.append({&quot;score&quot;: float(d), &quot;shard_hit&quot;: int(i)})
    # sort global
    results = sorted(results, key=lambda x: x[&quot;score&quot;])[:q.topk]
    return {&quot;results&quot;: results}


assembly_service.py — FastAPI service to assemble PDF evidence and call provenance SP (assembly + provenance)

# src/assembly_service.py

        f.write(b&quot;%PDF-1.4\n%placeholder\n&quot;)

def persist_billing_runs(session: Session, older_than_minutes: int=60, preview_only: bool=True):

reconcile_billing_vs_usage (Snowpark handler for reconciling billing vs usage)

from snowflake.snowpark import Session,functions as F 
import json
def reconcile_billing_vs_usage(session: Session, account_id=None, window_start=None, window_end=None):
    cond=[]
    if account_id:cond.append(f&quot;account_id=’{account_id}’&quot;)
    if window_start and window_end:cond.append(f&quot;usage_timestamp between to_timestamp_ltz(’{window_start}’) and to_timestamp_ltz(’{window_end}’)&quot;)
    where=’ and ’.join(cond) or ’1=1’
    usage=session.sql(f&quot;select account_id,feature_key,sum(units) units from AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENATU_FEATU RE_USAGE where {where} group by account_id,feature_key&quot;).collect()
    billing=session.sql(f&quot;select account_id,feature_key,sum(line_total) billed from AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILL ING_LINE_ITEM where {where} group by account_id,feature_key&quot;).collect()
    u_map={(r[’ACCOUNT_ID’],r[’FEATURE_KEY’]):r[’UNITS’] for r in usage}
    b_map={(r[’ACCOUNT_ID’],r[’FEATURE_KEY’]):r[’BILLED’] for r in billing}
    issues=[]
    keys=set(list(u_map.keys())+list(b_map.keys()))
    for k in keys:
        u=u_map.get(k,0)
        b=b_map.get(k,0)
        if abs(u-b)&gt;0.01:
            issues.append({’account_id’:k[0],’feature_key’:k[1],’usage’:u,’billed’:b,’diff’:u-b})
    if issues:
        for i in issues:
            session.sql(&quot;insert into AI_FEATURE_HUB.RECONCILIATION_AUDIT(AUDIT_ID,ACCOUNT_ID,FEATURE_KEY,USAG E_SUM,BILL_SUM,MISMATCH,RUN_AT) values(’{0}’,’{1}’,’{2}’,{3},{4},{5},CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())”.format(uuid.uuid4().hex,i[’account_id’],i[’feature_key’],i[’usage’],i[’billed’],i[’diff’])).collect()
    return {’issues’:len(issues)}



pricing helpers — apply_tiers (tiered pricing)

# pricing helpers used by billing and preview; minified
def apply_tiers(amount,tiers): 
    # tiers: list of dict {upto,unit_price} 
    remaining=amount;cost=0.0;prev=0 
    for t in tiers: 
        upto=t.get(’upto’) 
        price=t[’unit_price’] 
        if upto is None: 
            qty=remaining 
        else: 
            qty=max(0,min(remaining,upto-prev)) 
        cost+=qty*price 
        prev=upto if upto else prev 
        remaining-=qty 
        if remaining&lt;=0:break 
    return round(cost,6) 



pricing helpers — apply_discount (percentage + flat)

def apply_discount(subtotal,percent,flat): 
    return round(max(0,subtotal*(1-percent/100.0)-flat),6)



submit_rate_rule (Snowpark handler to submit rate rule into staging and audit)

from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import json, uuid

def submit_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_json: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot; staging_json: JSON string with keys: feature_key, rule_type, config (dict), priority
    Inserts into RATE_RULES_STAGING and writes audit entry.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    payload = json.loads(staging_json)
    staging_id = f&quot;stg-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    
    # SQL INSERT into RATE_RULES_STAGING (structure inferred from context)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot; 
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING (STAGING_ID, FEATURE_KEY, RULE_TYPE, CONFIG, PRIORITY, SUBMITTED_BY, SUBMITTED_AT) 
        VALUES (&#39;{staging_id}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&#39;feature_key&#39;)}&#39;, &#39;{payload.get(&#39;rule_type&#39;)}&#39;, 
        PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(payload.get(&#39;config&#39;))}&#39;), {payload.get(&#39;priority&#39;,100)}, 
        &#39;{payload.get(&#39;submitted_by&#39;,&#39;streamlit&#39;)}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    
    # Audit
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot; 
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, ACTION_TS, DETAILS) 
        VALUES (&#39;audit-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&#39;,&#39;{staging_id}&#39;,&#39;SUBMIT&#39;,&#39;{payload.get(&#39;submitted_by&#39;,&#39;streamlit&#39;)}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(payload)}&#39;));
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;submitted&quot;,&quot;staging_id&quot;:staging_id}



approve_rate_rule (Snowpark handler to approve and MERGE staging into active rules)

from snowflake.snowpark import Session, functions as F
import json, uuid

def approve_rate_rule(session: Session, staging_id: str, approver: str):
    # Fetch data from staging table (RULE_ID derivation is critical but external)
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT feature_key, rule_type, config, priority FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING WHERE staging_id=&#39;{staging_id}&#39; AND approved=false AND rejected=false&quot;).collect()
    
    if not rows:
        return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;not_found_or_processed&quot;,&quot;staging_id&quot;:staging_id}
    
    r = rows[0].as_dict()
    # Crucially, the active RULE_ID for MERGE must be derived here, often linked to staging_id
    # Using staging_id as rule_id for simplicity based on context provided in Chunk 95&#39;s logic:
    rule_id = f&quot;rule-{r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]}-{r[&#39;RULE_TYPE&#39;]}-{staging_id}&quot; 

    # MERGE into active rules table
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES t 
        USING (SELECT &#39;{rule_id}&#39; as rule_id, &#39;{r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]}&#39; as feature_key, &#39;{r[&#39;RULE_TYPE&#39;]}&#39; as rule_type, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(r[&#39;CONFIG&#39;])}&#39;) as config, {r[&#39;PRIORITY&#39;]} as priority) s 
        ON t.rule_id=s.rule_id 
        WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET 
            feature_key=s.feature_key, rule_type=s.rule_type, config=s.config, priority=s.priority, active=true, effective_from=current_timestamp()
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (rule_id, feature_key, rule_type, config, priority, effective_from, active, created_at) 
        VALUES (s.rule_id, s.feature_key, s.rule_type, s.config, s.priority, current_timestamp(), true, current_timestamp())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    
    # Mark staging as approved and audit
    session.sql(f&quot;UPDATE AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_STAGING SET approved=TRUE, approved_by=&#39;{approver}&#39;, approved_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() WHERE staging_id = &#39;{staging_id}&#39;&quot;).collect()
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot; 
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES_APPROVAL_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, STAGING_ID, ACTION, ACTOR, ACTION_TS) 
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;approved&quot;,&quot;staging_id&quot;:staging_id,&quot;rule_id&quot;:rule_id}



(FAISS) faiss/indexsnapshotloader.py (production loader — conceptual code body from source)

# faiss/indexsnapshotloader.py - production loader for FAISS index shards (conceptual snippet)
import argparse, json, os
import boto3
import faiss

def load_vectors_from_jsonl(path):
    vecs=[]
    ids=[]
    with open(path,&#39;r&#39;) as fh:
        for l in fh:
            j=json.loads(l)
            ids.append(j[&#39;id&#39;])
            vecs.append(j[&#39;embedding&#39;])
    return ids, vecs

def build_or_load_index(vecs, index_type=&#39;HNSW32&#39;):
    if index_type.startswith(&#39;HNSW&#39;):
        d=len(vecs[0])
        index=faiss.IndexHNSWFlat(d,32)
        xb = np.array(vecs).astype(&#39;float32&#39;)
        index.add(xb)
    else:
        index=faiss.index_factory(len(vecs[0]), &quot;IVF100,Flat&quot;)
    return index

def main():
    # download jsonl from s3, build index, upload .faiss and id map to s3, output manifest
    pass

run_billing_consolidated — full Snowpark billing procedure (aggregate usage, apply rate rules, compute discounts/markup/tax, idempotent persist)

# AIFEATUREHUB.run_billing_consolidated(session, window_start, window_end, account_id=None, dry_run=True)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session, functions as F
import hashlib, json, uuid, decimal, math

def _to_decimal(v):
    try:
        return decimal.Decimal(str(v))
    except:
        return decimal.Decimal(0)

def run_billing_consolidated(session: Session, window_start: str, window_end: str, account_id: str = None, dry_run: bool = True):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Aggregate usage for window, apply RATE_RULES per feature, compute invoice, persist if dry_run=False.
    Returns a dict with invoice summary.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # 1) Read aggregated usage
    acct_filter = f&quot;AND account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39;&quot; if account_id else &quot;&quot;
    q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    SELECT account_id, feature_key, SUM(units) AS units, MAX(unit_price) AS unit_price
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE
    WHERE usage_timestamp BETWEEN TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{window_start}&#39;) AND TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{window_end}&#39;)
    {acct_filter}
    GROUP BY account_id, feature_key
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    df = session.sql(q).to_pandas()
    lines = []
    # 2) Resolve pricing rules for each line using RATE_RULES table (simple MERGE of best match)
    for _, r in df.iterrows():
        account = r[&#39;ACCOUNT_ID&#39;]
        fk = r[&#39;FEATURE_KEY&#39;]
        units = float(r[&#39;UNITS&#39;] or 0)
        unit_price = float(r[&#39;UNIT_PRICE&#39;] or 0)
        # fetch applicable rate rule (simplified: take highest precedence non-expired)
        rr_q = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.RATE_RULES
        WHERE feature_key = &#39;{fk}&#39; AND (org_id IS NULL OR org_id = (SELECT org_id FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ACCOUNTS WHERE account_id=&#39;{account}&#39; LIMIT 1))
        ORDER BY priority DESC NULLS LAST LIMIT 1
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        rr = session.sql(rr_q).collect()
        adj_unit_price = unit_price
        if rr:
            rule = rr[0].asDict()
            # example: override unit_price if rule.price_override is present
            if rule.get(&#39;PRICE_OVERRIDE&#39;) is not None:
                adj_unit_price = float(rule[&#39;PRICE_OVERRIDE&#39;])
            # example: apply discount_percent if present
            discount_pct = float(rule.get(&#39;DISCOUNT_PERCENT&#39;) or 0)
        else:
            discount_pct = 0.0
        line_subtotal = round(units * adj_unit_price * (1 - discount_pct/100.0), 6)
        lines.append({
            &quot;account_id&quot;: account,
            &quot;feature_key&quot;: fk,
            &quot;units&quot;: units,
            &quot;unit_price&quot;: adj_unit_price,
            &quot;discount_pct&quot;: discount_pct,
            &quot;line_total&quot;: float(line_subtotal)
        })
    # 3) Totals, markup, tax
    subtotal = round(sum(l[&#39;line_total&#39;] for l in lines), 6)
    # Bucket for markup/tax: markup rules table or default
    markup_pct = 8.5  # default
    try:
        m = session.sql(&quot;SELECT value::FLOAT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.CONFIG WHERE key=&#39;BILLING_MARKUP_PCT&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
        if m and m[0][0] is not None:
            markup_pct = float(m[0][0])
    except:
        pass
    markup = round(subtotal * (markup_pct/100.0), 6)
    tax_pct = 0.0
    try:
        t = session.sql(&quot;SELECT value::FLOAT FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.CONFIG WHERE key=&#39;BILLING_TAX_PCT&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;).collect()
        if t and t[0][0] is not None:
            tax_pct = float(t[0][0])
    except:
        pass
    tax = round((subtotal + markup) * (tax_pct/100.0), 6)
    total = round(subtotal + markup + tax, 6)
    invoice_hash = hashlib.sha256((json.dumps(lines, sort_keys=True) + str(total)).encode()).hexdigest()
    run_id = f&quot;billing-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    result = {
        &quot;run_id&quot;: run_id,
        &quot;invoice_hash&quot;: invoice_hash,
        &quot;subtotal&quot;: float(subtotal),
        &quot;markup&quot;: float(markup),
        &quot;tax&quot;: float(tax),
        &quot;total&quot;: float(total),
        &quot;lines&quot;: lines,
        &quot;dry_run&quot;: dry_run,
        &quot;window_start&quot;: window_start,
        &quot;window_end&quot;: window_end
    }
    # 4) Persist preview and optionally persist invoices/lines idempotently
    # persist preview
    preview_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEWS(RUN_ID, INVOICE_HASH, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT)
    SELECT &#39;{run_id}&#39;,&#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(result)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEWS WHERE INVOICE_HASH = &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;);
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(preview_sql).collect()
    if not dry_run:
        # MERGE into SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES idempotent by INVOICE_HASH
        inv_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES tgt
        USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS invoice_hash) src
        ON tgt.invoice_hash = src.invoice_hash
        WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN
          INSERT (invoice_id, account_id, period_start, period_end, subtotal, markup, tax, total, invoice_hash, created_at)
          VALUES (&#39;{run_id}&#39;,&#39;{account_id or &quot;MULTI&quot;}&#39;, TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{window_start}&#39;), TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(&#39;{window_end}&#39;), {subtotal}, {markup}, {tax}, {total}, &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        session.sql(inv_sql).collect()
        # Insert line items idempotently keyed by invoice_hash + feature_key
        for ln in lines:
            li_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
            MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_LINE_ITEM tgt
            USING (SELECT &#39;{invoice_hash}&#39; AS invoice_hash, &#39;{ln[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39; AS feature_key) src
            ON tgt.invoice_hash = src.invoice_hash AND tgt.feature_key = src.feature_key
            WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (billing_run_id,account_id,feature_key,usage_qty,unit_price,discount_pct,line_total,created_at)
            VALUES (&#39;{run_id}&#39;,&#39;{ln[&#39;account_id&#39;]}&#39;,&#39;{ln[&#39;feature_key&#39;]}&#39;,{ln[&#39;units&#39;]},{ln[&#39;unit_price&#39;]},{ln[&#39;discount_pct&#39;]},{ln[&#39;line_total&#39;]},CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
            &quot;&quot;&quot;
            session.sql(li_sql).collect()
        # audit
        audit_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_RUN_AUDIT (RUN_ID, INVOICE_HASH, PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT)
        VALUES (&#39;{run_id}&#39;,&#39;{invoice_hash}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(result)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
        &quot;&quot;&quot;
        session.sql(audit_sql).collect()
    return result



ingest_usage_idempotent — idempotent usage ingestion stored-procedure (process staged CSV/JSON into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE with MERGE dedupe)

# AIFEATUREHUB.ingest_usage_idempotent(session, stage_path, batch_id)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session, DataFrame
import uuid, json, datetime

def ingest_usage_idempotent(session: Session, stage_path: str, batch_id: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    stage_path: @~/staged/usage/{file}
    batch_id: caller-provided batch identifier to group ingest
    Behavior:
    - Reads staged JSON/CSV into a transient table
    - MERGE into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE on event_id to ensure idempotence
    - Writes provenance into USAGE_PROVENANCE
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    tmp_table = f&quot;TMP_USAGE_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}&quot;
    # Create transient table by reading staged JSONL (assumes newline-delimited JSON)
    create_tmp = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    CREATE OR REPLACE TEMPORARY TABLE {tmp_table} (event_id STRING, account_id STRING, feature_key STRING, units FLOAT, unit_price FLOAT, usage_timestamp TIMESTAMP_LTZ, raw VARIANT);
    COPY INTO {tmp_table}(raw) FROM &#39;{stage_path}&#39; FILE_FORMAT=(type=&#39;JSON&#39;) ON_ERROR=&#39;SKIP_FILE&#39;;
    -- populate columns from raw
    UPDATE {tmp_table}
    SET event_id = raw:event_id::STRING,
        account_id = raw:account_id::STRING,
        feature_key = raw:feature_key::STRING,
        units = TO_DOUBLE(raw:units),
        unit_price = TO_DOUBLE(raw:unit_price),
        usage_timestamp = TO_TIMESTAMP_LTZ(raw:usage_timestamp);
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(create_tmp).collect()
    # MERGE into TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE by event_id
    merge_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.TENANT_FEATURE_USAGE tgt
    USING (SELECT event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp FROM {tmp_table}) src
    ON tgt.event_id = src.event_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET account_id = src.account_id, feature_key = src.feature_key, units = src.units, unit_price = src.unit_price, usage_timestamp = src.usage_timestamp, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (event_id, account_id, feature_key, units, unit_price, usage_timestamp, created_at)
      VALUES (src.event_id, src.account_id, src.feature_key, src.units, src.unit_price, src.usage_timestamp, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(merge_sql).collect()
    # Record provenance for batch
    prov_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.USAGE_PROVENANCE (PROV_ID, BATCH_ID, STAGE_PATH, ROW_COUNT, CREATED_AT)
    SELECT &#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&#39;,&#39;{batch_id}&#39;,&#39;{stage_path}&#39;, COUNT(*), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP() FROM {tmp_table};
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(prov_sql).collect()
    # drop temp
    session.sql(f&quot;DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {tmp_table}&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;ok&quot;, &quot;batch_id&quot;: batch_id}



embedding_ingest_sp — idempotent embedding ingestion with provenance and optional FAISS snapshot trigger

# AIFEATUREHUB.embedding_ingest_sp(session, stage_path, batch_id, trigger_snapshot=False)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json, hashlib

def _checksum(vec):
    return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(vec, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()

def embedding_ingest_sp(session: Session, stage_path: str, batch_id: str, trigger_snapshot: bool = False):
    tmp = f&quot;TMP_EMB_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}&quot;
    # load staged JSONL where each line contains: doc_id, embedding (array), metadata
    session.sql(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE TEMPORARY TABLE {tmp}(raw VARIANT); COPY INTO {tmp}(raw) FROM &#39;{stage_path}&#39; FILE_FORMAT=(type=&#39;JSON&#39;) ON_ERROR=&#39;SKIP_FILE&#39;&quot;).collect()
    # normalize
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    CREATE OR REPLACE TEMPORARY TABLE {tmp}_norm AS
    SELECT raw:doc_id::STRING AS doc_id,
           raw:embedding AS embedding,
           raw:metadata AS metadata,
           raw:created_at::TIMESTAMP_LTZ AS created_at
    FROM {tmp};
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # compute checksum and MERGE into DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS
    # build staging view
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS tgt
    USING (SELECT doc_id, embedding, metadata, created_at FROM {tmp}_norm) src
    ON tgt.doc_id = src.doc_id
    WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET embedding = src.embedding, metadata = src.metadata, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (doc_id, embedding, metadata, created_at) VALUES (src.doc_id, src.embedding, src.metadata, src.created_at);
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # insert provenance rows for each doc (idempotent by doc_id + batch_id)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_PROVENANCE (PROV_ID, DOC_ID, BATCH_ID, RAW_PAYLOAD, CREATED_AT)
    SELECT &#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&#39;, doc_id, &#39;{batch_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(TO_VARCHAR(embedding)), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    FROM {tmp}_norm
    WHERE NOT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_PROVENANCE p WHERE p.doc_id = {tmp}_norm.doc_id AND p.batch_id = &#39;{batch_id}&#39;
    );
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # Optional: enqueue FAISS snapshot build
    if trigger_snapshot:
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ann_promote_tasks (task_id, reason, created_at)
        VALUES (&#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&#39;,&#39;embedding_ingest_snapshot&#39;,&#39;{datetime.datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}&#39;);
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # cleanup
    session.sql(f&quot;DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {tmp}; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {tmp}_norm;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;ok&quot;,&quot;batch_id&quot;:batch_id}



entitlement_check_sp — verify entitlements/quotas and return allowances

# AIFEATUREHUB.entitlement_check_sp(session, account_id, feature_key, requested_units=1)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def entitlement_check_sp(session: Session, account_id: str, feature_key: str, requested_units: float = 1.0):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    Checks ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENTS and QUOTA_USAGE and returns allowed boolean and remaining quota.
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # read entitlement
    ent_q = f&quot;SELECT quota_units, quota_window_seconds FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENTS WHERE account_id = &#39;{account_id}&#39; AND feature_key = &#39;{feature_key}&#39; LIMIT 1&quot;
    ent = session.sql(ent_q).collect()
    if not ent:
        return {&quot;allowed&quot;: False, &quot;reason&quot;: &quot;no_entitlement&quot;}
    quota_units = float(ent[0][0] or 0)
    # compute used in window (assumes QUOTA_USAGE table)
    used_q = f&quot;SELECT COALESCE(SUM(units),0) FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.QUOTA_USAGE WHERE account_id=&#39;{account_id}&#39; AND feature_key=&#39;{feature_key}&#39; AND usage_ts &gt; DATEADD(second, -{int(ent[0][1] or 86400)}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
    used = float(session.sql(used_q).collect()[0][0] or 0)
    remaining = quota_units - used
    allowed = remaining &gt;= requested_units
    return {&quot;allowed&quot;: bool(allowed), &quot;remaining&quot;: float(remaining), &quot;requested&quot;: float(requested_units)}



write_evidence_bundle — package evidence and persist to table and optional stage export

# AIFEATUREHUB.write_evidence_bundle(session, claim_id, evidence_items: list, export_stage: str=None)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import uuid, json, datetime

def write_evidence_bundle(session: Session, claim_id: str, evidence_items: list, export_stage: str = None):
    bundle_id = f&quot;eb-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    payload = {
        &quot;claim_id&quot;: claim_id,
        &quot;bundle_id&quot;: bundle_id,
        &quot;items&quot;: evidence_items,
        &quot;created_at&quot;: datetime.datetime.utcnow().isoformat()
    }
    # Persist into EVIDENCE_BUNDLES (idempotent by claim_id + bundle_id)
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    MERGE INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.EVIDENCE_BUNDLES tgt
    USING (SELECT &#39;{bundle_id}&#39; AS bundle_id) src
    ON tgt.bundle_id = src.bundle_id
    WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (bundle_id, claim_id, payload, created_at) VALUES (&#39;{bundle_id}&#39;,&#39;{claim_id}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(payload)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # optional export: write JSON to internal stage path
    if export_stage:
        # create temp table to stage single row and use COPY INTO to stage path
        tmp = f&quot;TMP_EB_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}&quot;
        session.sql(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE TEMPORARY TABLE {tmp}(payload STRING); INSERT INTO {tmp}(payload) VALUES (&#39;{json.dumps(payload)}&#39;);&quot;).collect()
        session.sql(f&quot;COPY INTO &#39;{export_stage}/{bundle_id}.json&#39; FROM (SELECT payload FROM {tmp}) FILE_FORMAT=(type=&#39;JSON&#39;) OVERWRITE=TRUE; DROP TABLE IF EXISTS {tmp};&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;ok&quot;,&quot;bundle_id&quot;:bundle_id}



generate_integration_key — create plaintext key, store salted hash

# AIFEATUREHUB.generate_integration_key(session, org_id, created_by)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import hashlib, os, uuid, base64

def generate_integration_key(session: Session, org_id: str, created_by: str):
    # generate a random plaintext key and store salted hash
    plaintext = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(os.urandom(32)).decode().rstrip(&#39;=&#39;)
    salt = uuid.uuid4().hex
    to_hash = f&quot;{salt}:{plaintext}&quot;
    h = hashlib.sha256(to_hash.encode()).hexdigest()
    key_id = f&quot;ikey-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.INTEGRATION_KEYS (key_id, org_id, salt, key_hash, created_by, created_at)
    VALUES (&#39;{key_id}&#39;,&#39;{org_id}&#39;,&#39;{salt}&#39;,&#39;{h}&#39;,&#39;{created_by}&#39;,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    # return plaintext only once
    return {&quot;key_id&quot;: key_id, &quot;plaintext_key&quot;: plaintext}



coordinate_run_billing_scheduler — enumerates tenants, invokes run_billing_consolidated (parallelizable)

# AIFEATUREHUB.coordinate_run_billing_scheduler(session, window_start, window_end, parallel_workers=4)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import concurrent.futures, functools

def _invoke_for_account(session: Session, account_id, window_start, window_end):
    # call stored-proc wrapper via SQL CALL if registered, else call local impl
    try:
        session.sql(f&quot;CALL AIFEATUREHUB.proc_run_billing(&#39;{window_start}&#39;,&#39;{window_end}&#39;,&#39;{account_id}&#39;,TRUE);&quot;).collect()
        return {&quot;account_id&quot;: account_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;submitted&quot;}
    except Exception:
        # fallback: call python function directly if available in session context
        # This is a best-effort; prefer registered proc
        return {&quot;account_id&quot;: account_id, &quot;status&quot;: &quot;error&quot;}

def coordinate_run_billing_scheduler(session: Session, window_start: str, window_end: str, parallel_workers: int = 4):
    rows = session.sql(&quot;SELECT account_id FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ACCOUNTS WHERE billing_enabled = TRUE&quot;).collect()
    accounts = [r[0] for r in rows]
    results = []
    # parallel dispatch using ThreadPool (calls will happen via SQL to Snowflake tasks/queues)
    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=parallel_workers) as ex:
        futures = {ex.submit(_invoke_for_account, session, a, window_start, window_end): a for a in accounts}
        for f in concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures):
            results.append(f.result())
    # log orchestration result
    session.sql(f&quot;INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_SCHEDULER_AUDIT (AUDIT_ID, WINDOWS, DETAIL, CREATED_AT) VALUES (&#39;{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&#39;,&#39;{window_start}|{window_end}&#39;, PARSE_JSON(&#39;{json.dumps(results)}&#39;), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP());&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;dispatched&quot;: len(results), &quot;results&quot;: results}



persist_billing_telemetry_writer — rollups for SLO/telemetry

# AIFEATUREHUB.persist_billing_telemetry_writer(session, lookback_minutes=60)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session

def persist_billing_telemetry_writer(session: Session, lookback_minutes: int = 60):
    # aggregate recent billing previews/committed runs into a telemetry table
    sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.V_BILLING_TELEMETRY (RUN_TS, RUN_ID, ACCOUNT_ID, TOTAL, LINE_COUNT, CREATED_AT)
    SELECT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(), bp.run_id, COALESCE(si.account_id, &#39;MULTI&#39;), COALESCE(si.total, bp.payload:total::FLOAT), ARRAY_SIZE(bp.payload:lines), CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
    FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.BILLING_PREVIEWS bp
    LEFT JOIN AI_FEATURE_HUB.SUBSCRIPTION_INVOICES si ON si.invoice_hash = bp.invoice_hash
    WHERE bp.created_at &gt; DATEADD(minute, -{lookback_minutes}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    session.sql(sql).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;ok&quot;}



faiss_index_snapshot_loader — Python loader for FAISS index snapshots (downloads from stage/S3, constructs FAISS index, writes artifact back)

# containers/faiss/indexsnapshotloader.py -&gt; load_snapshot(session, snapshot_url, index_output_stage)
import os, json, tempfile, faiss, numpy as np, boto3

def load_snapshot(snapshot_url: str, index_output_stage: str):
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    snapshot_url: s3://bucket/path/snapshot.jsonl  (each line: id, vector)
    index_output_stage: s3://bucket/path/output/
    Builds FAISS index and uploads .index and idmap.json
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    # This intentionally runs outside Snowflake (containerized worker)
    local_json = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=&quot;.jsonl&quot;)
    # download via boto3
    s3 = boto3.client(&#39;s3&#39;)
    parsed = snapshot_url.replace(&quot;s3://&quot;,&quot;&quot;).split(&quot;/&quot;,1)
    bucket, key = parsed[0], parsed[1]
    s3.download_file(bucket, key, local_json)
    ids = []
    vectors = []
    with open(local_json,&#39;r&#39;,encoding=&#39;utf8&#39;) as fh:
        for line in fh:
            obj = json.loads(line)
            ids.append(obj[&#39;id&#39;])
            vectors.append(obj[&#39;embedding&#39;])
    arr = np.array(vectors).astype(&#39;float32&#39;)
    d = arr.shape[1]
    index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(d)
    index.add(arr)
    # write index locally
    idx_path = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=&quot;.index&quot;)
    faiss.write_index(index, idx_path)
    # upload index and idmap
    out_index_key = index_output_stage.rstrip(&#39;/&#39;) + &#39;/index.bin&#39;
    out_map_key = index_output_stage.rstrip(&#39;/&#39;) + &#39;/idmap.json&#39;
    s3.upload_file(idx_path, bucket, out_index_key.replace(f&quot;s3://{bucket}/&quot;,&quot;&quot;))
    s3.upload_fileobj(io.BytesIO(json.dumps(ids).encode()), bucket, out_map_key.replace(f&quot;s3://{bucket}/&quot;,&quot;&quot;))
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;ok&quot;,&quot;index_count&quot;: len(ids)}



faiss_service_api — FastAPI wrapper that loads FAISS index and serves similarity queries

# faiss/similarity_service.py
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel
import faiss, numpy as np, json

app = FastAPI()
index = None
idmap = []

class Query(BaseModel):
    vector: list
    k: int = 10

@app.on_event(&quot;startup&quot;)
def load_index():
    global index, idmap
    # simplified: load from local path prepared by container
    try:
        index = faiss.read_index(&quot;/opt/index/index.bin&quot;)
        with open(&quot;/opt/index/idmap.json&quot;,&quot;r&quot;) as fh:
            idmap = json.load(fh)
    except Exception as e:
        index = None
        idmap = []
        print(&quot;index load failed&quot;, e)

@app.post(&quot;/search&quot;)
def search(q: Query):
    if index is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail=&quot;Index not available&quot;)
    arr = np.array([q.vector]).astype(&#39;float32&#39;)
    D, I = index.search(arr, q.k)
    results = []
    for dist, idx in zip(D[0], I[0]):
        results.append({&quot;id&quot;: idmap[idx] if idx &lt; len(idmap) else None, &quot;score&quot;: float(dist)})
    return {&quot;results&quot;: results}



register_procedures_helper — infra/register_via_connector.py (helper to PUT and CREATE PROCEDURE in order)

# infra/register_via_connector.py
import snowflake.connector, os, time, json

def register_files(conn_params: dict, files: list, stage=&quot;@~&quot;):
    ctx = snowflake.connector.connect(**conn_params)
    cs = ctx.cursor()
    try:
        for f in files:
            fname = os.path.basename(f)
            # PUT file into user stage
            with open(f,&#39;rb&#39;) as fh:
                sql = f&quot;PUT file://{f} {stage}/{fname} AUTO_COMPRESS=TRUE OVERWRITE=TRUE&quot;
                cs.execute(sql)
            time.sleep(0.2)
        # Optionally run registration SQL that depends on files
    finally:
        cs.close()
        ctx.close()
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;ok&quot;,&quot;files_put&quot;: [os.path.basename(f) for f in files]}

SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT=$2
NAMESPACE=$3

  IMAGE_NAME =&gt; &#39;${IMAGE_NAME}&#39;
);
EOF

external_function_faiss_template — generator that returns SQL for API_INTEGRATION + EXTERNAL FUNCTION registration

# infra/generate_external_function_sql.py
def external_function_faiss_sql(api_integration_name, ext_function_name, api_url, role=&#39;SYSADMIN&#39;):
    api_int_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    CREATE OR REPLACE API INTEGRATION {api_integration_name}
      API_PROVIDER = &#39;aws_api_gateway&#39;
      API_AWS_ROLE_ARN = &#39;&lt;AWS_ROLE_ARN_PLACEHOLDER&gt;&#39;
      ENABLED = TRUE;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    ext_fn_sql = f&quot;&quot;&quot;
    CREATE OR REPLACE EXTERNAL FUNCTION {ext_function_name}(vector ARRAY)
    RETURNS VARIANT
    API_INTEGRATION = {api_integration_name}
    AS &#39;https://{api_url}/search&#39;
    ;
    &quot;&quot;&quot;
    return api_int_sql + &quot;\n\n&quot; + ext_fn_sql



tests_harness_snowflake — pytest harness that calls registered procedures (smoke test)

# tests/test_harness_snowflake.py
import snowflake.connector, json

def call_proc(conn_params, proc_sql):
    ctx = snowflake.connector.connect(**conn_params)
    cs = ctx.cursor()
    try:
        cs.execute(proc_sql)
        return cs.fetchall()
    finally:
        cs.close()
        ctx.close()

def test_run_billing_preview():
    params = {&quot;account&quot;: &quot;DEMO&quot;}
    res = call_proc({&quot;user&quot;:&quot;u&quot;,&quot;password&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;account&quot;:&quot;acct&quot;},&quot;CALL AIFEATUREHUB.proc_run_billing(&#39;2025-01-01&#39;,&#39;2025-01-31&#39;,NULL,TRUE);&quot;)
    assert res is not None



test_deterministic_billing — unit test for invoice hash stability (local pytest)

# tests/test_deterministic_billing.py
import hashlib, json

def invoice_hash(lines, total):
    return hashlib.sha256((json.dumps(lines, sort_keys=True) + str(total)).encode()).hexdigest()

def test_invoice_hash_stable():
    lines = [{&quot;feature_key&quot;:&quot;f1&quot;,&quot;units&quot;:10,&quot;unit_price&quot;:1.0,&quot;line_total&quot;:10.0},{&quot;feature_key&quot;:&quot;f2&quot;,&quot;units&quot;:5,&quot;unit_price&quot;:2.0,&quot;line_total&quot;:10.0}]
    total = 22.0
    h1 = invoice_hash(lines, total)
    h2 = invoice_hash(lines, total)
    assert h1 == h2



billing_registration_sql — helper that builds CREATE PROCEDURE statements that import Python from @~/ and registers tasks

# infra/build_billing_registration.py
def build_registration_sql(python_files):
    puts = []
    creates = []
    for f in python_files:
        fname = f.split(&#39;/&#39;)[-1]
        puts.append(f&quot;PUT file://{f} @~/ OVERWRITE=TRUE;&quot;)
        proc_name = fname.replace(&#39;.py&#39;,&#39;&#39;)
        creates.append(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE PROCEDURE AIFEATUREHUB.proc_{proc_name}(ARGS VARIANT) RETURNS VARIANT LANGUAGE PYTHON RUNTIME_VERSION = &#39;3.8&#39; PACKAGES = (&#39;snowflake-snowpark-python&#39;) IMPORTS = (&#39;@~/{fname}&#39;) HANDLER = &#39;{proc_name}.main&#39;;&quot;)
    return &quot;\n&quot;.join(puts) + &quot;\n\n&quot; + &quot;\n&quot;.join(creates)



ann_index_promote_worker — worker that promotes ANN snapshot shards (containerized)

# containers/ann/promote_worker.py
import boto3, json, os, uuid

def promote_snapshot(s3_src, s3_dest_prefix):
    s3 = boto3.client(&#39;s3&#39;)
    # copy snapshot files (sharded) to destination (promotion)
    parsed = s3_src.replace(&quot;s3://&quot;,&quot;&quot;).split(&quot;/&quot;,1)
    bucket, key_prefix = parsed[0], parsed[1]
    # list objects
    resp = s3.list_objects_v2(Bucket=bucket, Prefix=key_prefix)
    for obj in resp.get(&#39;Contents&#39;,[]):
        src = obj[&#39;Key&#39;]
        dest = os.path.join(s3_dest_prefix, os.path.basename(src))
        s3.copy_object(Bucket=bucket, CopySource={&#39;Bucket&#39;:bucket,&#39;Key&#39;:src}, Key=dest)
    return {&quot;promoted&quot;: True}



mark_vector_for_gc_sp — mark embeddings/vectors eligible for GC (stored procedure)

# AIFEATUREHUB.mark_vector_for_gc_sp(session, doc_id, reason)

    WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.VECTOR_GC_CANDIDATES WHERE DOC_ID=&#39;{doc_id}&#39;);

apply_provenance_retention_sp — delete or archive provenance per retention rules (idempotent)

# AIFEATUREHUB.apply_provenance_retention_sp(session, org_id=None)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session
import datetime

def apply_provenance_retention_sp(session: Session, org_id: str = None):
    where_org = f&quot;AND org_id=&#39;{org_id}&#39;&quot; if org_id else &quot;&quot;
    # select rules
    rules = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT org_id, embedding_days, provenance_days FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.retention_policies WHERE 1=1 {where_org}&quot;).collect()
    for r in rules:
        oid = r[&#39;ORG_ID&#39;]
        prov_days = int(r[&#39;PROVENANCE_DAYS&#39;] or 0)
        cutoff = f&quot;DATEADD(day, -{prov_days}, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())&quot;
        # archive to provenance_archive table then delete (transactional pattern)
        session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
        INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.PROVENANCE_ARCHIVE SELECT * FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_PROVENANCE WHERE org_id=&#39;{oid}&#39; AND created_at &lt; {cutoff};
        DELETE FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_PROVENANCE WHERE org_id=&#39;{oid}&#39; AND created_at &lt; {cutoff};
        &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;ok&quot;,&quot;rules_applied&quot;: len(rules)}



validate_embedding_integrity_sp — verify embedding checksums against stored index

# AIFEATUREHUB.validate_embedding_integrity_sp(session, limit=1000)
from snowflake.snowpark import Session, functions as F

def validate_embedding_integrity_sp(session: Session, limit: int = 1000):
    # compute checksum for sample of embeddings and compare to EMBEDDING_CHECKSUM_INDEX
    rows = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT doc_id, embedding FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS LIMIT {limit}&quot;).collect()
    mismatches = []
    for r in rows:
        doc_id = r[&#39;DOC_ID&#39;]
        emb = r[&#39;EMBEDDING&#39;]
        import hashlib, json
        chk = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(emb, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
        res = session.sql(f&quot;SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.EMBEDDING_CHECKSUM_INDEX WHERE doc_id=&#39;{doc_id}&#39; AND checksum=&#39;{chk}&#39;&quot;).collect()
        if not res:
            mismatches.append(doc_id)
    return {&quot;sample_checked&quot;: len(rows), &quot;mismatches&quot;: mismatches}

def provision(conn_params, stage_name=&quot;@~/ingest&quot;, file_format_name=&quot;INGEST_JSON&quot;):
    ctx = snowflake.connector.connect(**conn_params)
    cs = ctx.cursor()
    try:
        cs.execute(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE FILE FORMAT {file_format_name} TYPE = &#39;JSON&#39; STRIP_OUTER_ARRAY = TRUE;&quot;)
        cs.execute(f&quot;CREATE OR REPLACE STAGE {stage_name} FILE_FORMAT = {file_format_name};&quot;)
        # Example role setup
        cs.execute(&quot;CREATE ROLE IF NOT EXISTS AI_FEATURE_HUB_ROLE;&quot;)
        cs.execute(&quot;GRANT USAGE ON DATABASE %s TO ROLE AI_FEATURE_HUB_ROLE;&quot; % conn_params.get(&quot;database&quot;))
        # Add more provisioning steps as required...
    finally:
        cs.close()
        ctx.close()
    return {&quot;status&quot;:&quot;ok&quot;}

# src/entitlement_admin.py

import uuid, json, datetime

def add_entitlement(session: Session, account_id: str, feature_key: str, quota_units: int, quota_window_seconds: int, created_by: str):
    ent_id = f&quot;ent-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}&quot;
    session.sql(f&quot;&quot;&quot;
      INSERT INTO AI_FEATURE_HUB.ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENTS(entitlement_id, account_id, feature_key, quota_units, quota_window_seconds, created_by, created_at)
      SELECT &#39;{ent_id}&#39;,&#39;{account_id}&#39;,&#39;{feature_key}&#39;,{quota_units},{quota_window_seconds},&#39;{created_by}&#39;,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()
      WHERE NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.ACCOUNT_ENTITLEMENTS WHERE account_id=&#39;{account_id}&#39; AND feature_key=&#39;{feature_key}&#39;);
    &quot;&quot;&quot;).collect()
    return {&quot;entitlement_id&quot;: ent_id}


usage_ingest_worker.py — worker that scans cloud storage and triggers ingest_usage_idempotentSources:

# workers/usage_ingest_worker.py
import boto3, os, requests, uuid
from subprocess import run

def scan_and_submit(s3_bucket, s3_prefix, snow_connector_payload):
    s3 = boto3.client(&#39;s3&#39;)
    resp = s3.list_objects_v2(Bucket=s3_bucket, Prefix=s3_prefix)
    files = [o[&#39;Key&#39;] for o in resp.get(&#39;Contents&#39;, [])]
    results = []
    for f in files:
        # download to local and call Snowflake PUT + CALL or invoke a RESTful ingestion endpoint
        local = f&quot;/tmp/{os.path.basename(f)}&quot;
        s3.download_file(s3_bucket, f, local)
        # Example: use register helper or SnowSQL to PUT the file then CALL ingest proc
        # Implementation detail depends on environment; record batch_id
        batch_id = f&quot;batch-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}&quot;
        # For an orchestration approach, enqueue a job for the Snowflake ingestion task
        results.append({&quot;file&quot;: f, &quot;batch_id&quot;: batch_id})
    return results

def upload_and_trigger(bucket, local_file_path, stage_prefix):

faiss_index_exporter.py — export embeddings to JSONL snapshot for FAISS indexingSources:

# containers/faiss/export_snapshot.py
import json, boto3, tempfile
from snowflake.connector import connect

def export_snapshot(snow_conn_params, s3_bucket, s3_prefix, query=&quot;SELECT doc_id, embedding FROM AI_FEATURE_HUB.DOCUMENT_EMBEDDINGS&quot;):
    ctx = connect(**snow_conn_params)
    cs = ctx.cursor()
    local_file = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=&quot;.jsonl&quot;)
    try:
        cs.execute(query)
        with open(local_file, &quot;w&quot;, encoding=&quot;utf8&quot;) as fh:
            for row in cs:
                doc_id, embedding = row[0], row[1]
                fh.write(json.dumps({&quot;id&quot;: doc_id, &quot;embedding&quot;: embedding}) + &quot;\n&quot;)
        s3 = boto3.client(&#39;s3&#39;)
        s3_key = f&quot;{s3_prefix.rstrip(&#39;/&#39;)}/snapshot-{int(time.time())}.jsonl&quot;
        s3.upload_file(local_file, s3_bucket, s3_key)
        return {&quot;s3_key&quot;: s3_key}
    finally:
        cs.close()
        ctx.close()


similarity_client.py — client to call FAISS service (HTTP) or Snowflake external functionSources:

# clients/similarity_client.py
import requests, json

class SimilarityClient:
    def __init__(self, endpoint_url, api_key=None):
        self.endpoint = endpoint_url
        self.headers = {&quot;Content-Type&quot;:&quot;application/json&quot;}
        if api_key:
            self.headers[&quot;Authorization&quot;] = f&quot;Bearer {api_key}&quot;

    def search(self, vector, k=10):
        payload = {&quot;vector&quot;: vector, &quot;k&quot;: k}
        r = requests.post(f&quot;{self.endpoint}/search&quot;, json=payload, headers=self.headers, timeout=10)
        r.raise_for_status()
        return r.json()

# src/evidence_bundle_uploader.py
import boto3, os, uuid

def upload_files_to_s3(file_paths, s3_bucket, s3_prefix):
    s3 = boto3.client(&#39;s3&#39;)
    uploaded = []
    for f in file_paths:
        key = f&quot;{s3_prefix.rstrip(&#39;/&#39;)}/{os.path.basename(f)}&quot;
        s3.upload_file(f, s3_bucket, key)
        uploaded.append({&quot;file&quot;: f, &quot;s3_key&quot;: key})
    return uploaded


notification_sender.py — send email or webhook notifications (SMTP + webhook support)Sources:

# src/notification_sender.py
import smtplib, requests
from email.message import EmailMessage

def send_email(smtp_host, smtp_port, from_addr, to_addrs, subject, body, username=None, password=None):
    msg = EmailMessage()
    msg[&quot;Subject&quot;] = subject
    msg[&quot;From&quot;] = from_addr
    msg[&quot;To&quot;] = &quot;, &quot;.join(to_addrs if isinstance(to_addrs, list) else [to_addrs])
    msg.set_content(body)
    with smtplib.SMTP(smtp_host, smtp_port) as s:
        if username and password:
            s.starttls()
            s.login(username, password)
        s.send_message(msg)
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;sent&quot;}

def send_webhook(url, payload, headers=None):
    r = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers or {}, timeout=10)
    r.raise_for_status()
    return {&quot;status&quot;: &quot;sent&quot;, &quot;status_code&quot;: r.status_code}


billing_preview_diff.py — compute diff between two billing previews for admin reviewSources:

# tools/billing_preview_diff.py
import json, difflib

def preview_diff(prev_a_json, prev_b_json):
    a = json.dumps(prev_a_json, indent=2, sort_keys=True).splitlines()
    b = json.dumps(prev_b_json, indent=2, sort_keys=True).splitlines()
    diff = list(difflib.unified_diff(a, b, lineterm=&quot;&quot;))
    return diff


rate_rule_validator.py — validates incoming rate rules for schema correctness and conflictsSources:

# src/rate_rule_validator.py
import json

def validate_rule_payload(payload):
    required = [&quot;feature_key&quot;]
    for r in required:
        if r not in payload or not payload[r]:
            return False, f&quot;Missing required field: {r}&quot;
    # basic numeric checks
    if &quot;discount_pct&quot; in payload:
        d = float(payload[&quot;discount_pct&quot;])
        if not (0 &lt;= d &lt;= 100):
            return False, &quot;discount_pct must be between 0 and 100&quot;
    return True, None
